# UGEKAZNA 3-reja: Frontend — dizayn tizimi, ilova qobig'i, auth va admin sahifalari — implementatsiya rejasi

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Tasdiqlangan prototip ko'rinishidagi React ilovasini qurish: dizayn tokenlari va primitivlar, 9 bo'limli qobiq (yuqori panel, chap menyu, URL'dagi filtrlar, Ctrl+K, Ссылка, Печать, freshness, foydalanuvchi menyusi), login → TOTP 2FA → majburiy parol almashtirish oqimi, 2FA ulash (QR + zaxira kodlar), sessiyalar va 8 ta admin sahifasi; hammasi Caddy orqali qat'iy CSP bilan beriladi va Playwright E2E bilan 390/1024/1440 kenglikda tekshiriladi.

**Architecture:** `frontend/` — Vite + React 18 + TypeScript strict SPA. Marshrutlash — kichik o'z hash-router'i (`#<bo'lim>/<sahifa>?br=&per=`, TZ formatining o'zi), holat — TanStack Query 5 (server holati) + URL (filtrlar) + xotiradagi `AuthFlow` konteksti (`mfa_token` hech qayerga yozilmaydi). API tiplari backend OpenAPI'dan `openapi-typescript` bilan generatsiya qilinadi; `api()` fetch-o'rami CSRF sarlavhasini qo'shadi va `{"error": …}` formatini `ApiError` ga aylantiradi; 401/403/429 global ishlov beriladi. Stil — oddiy CSS (tokenlar `:root` da, prototip klass nomlari saqlangan), inline `<style>`/`<script>` yo'q. Prod va dev'da frontend `frontend/Dockerfile` ning `web` bosqichi (Caddy + `dist`) sifatida beriladi.

**Tech Stack:** Node 22 (Docker, `node:22-alpine`), Vite 5, React 18.3, TypeScript 5.6 (strict), TanStack Query 5, `@fontsource/onest`, `openapi-typescript` 7, Vitest 2 + Testing Library + jsdom + axe-core, ESLint 9 (typescript-eslint strict, react-hooks, jsx-a11y), Playwright 1.49.1 + `@axe-core/playwright`; backend tomonda Python 3.12 (faqat E2E seed buyrug'i).

**Spec:** [docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-24-poydevor-design.md](../specs/2026-09-24-poydevor-design.md) — 7, 8 (frontend qismi), 9 (frontend testlari) bo'limlari. Backend kontraktlari: [1-reja](2026-09-24-01-backend-yadro.md) (auth, admin, audit, kalitlar, OpenAPI) va [2-reja](2026-09-24-02-vitrina-etl.md) (meta, ETL, buildlar, qoidalar, допущения). Vizual manba: `.superpowers/sdd/planning/prototype.css`, `prototype-body.html`, `prototype.js`. Controller qarorlari: `.superpowers/sdd/planning/plan3-decisions.md`.

Bu — poydevor spec'ining 4 rejasidan uchinchisi. 1- va 2-rejalar bajarilgan deb hisoblanadi (dev stack `https://localhost:8443` da ishlaydi, `GET /api/v1/openapi.json` dev'da ochiq).

## Global Constraints

- Stek: Vite + React 18 + TypeScript `strict: true`; TanStack Query 5 — `staleTime` 5 daqiqa, `placeholderData: keepPreviousData` (spec 6.2); Chart.js bu rejada qo'shilmaydi.
- Marshrut formati (TZ 5): `#<раздел>/<страница>?br=<filial>&per=<davr>`, masalan `#kazna/svod?br=3&per=m05`. `per` kodlari — `/meta/periods` dagi `ytd | q1..q4 | m01..m12`; standart davr (`ytd`) URL'ga yozilmaydi.
- Bo'lim id'lari — backend `Section` enum'i: `obzor, kazna, kredity, finmodel, zakupki, sklad, proizvodstvo, investicii, kadry`; guruhlar: `Обзор · Финансы: Казначейство, Кредиты, Финмодель · Обеспечение: Закупки, Склад · Производство: Производство, Инвестиции · Персонал: Кадры`.
- Dizayn tokenlari (spec 7.2): `--bg #f5f8fd`, `--card #ffffff`, `--ink #132338`, `--muted #5f7288`, `--line #e4ebf4`, `--soft #eef5ff`, `--brand #0b4fa8`, `--brand2 #1d6df2`, `--teal #5fd0e0`, `--neg #e0453a`, `--top #0b4fa8`, `--top2 #083a7d`. Kontrast uchun ongli o'zgarishlar (ledger'da): `--muted #5a6d83`, `--neg #c9362c`, fokus halqasi `#2ba3db` → `--focus #1d6df2`, yuqori panel gradienti oxiri `#2b8ae6` → `--top-end #1668d6`, yuqori paneldagi matn `--top-ink #eaf3ff`. Token ma'nolari o'zgarmaydi: qizil faqat manfiy qiymat, muddati o'tgan va xato uchun.
- Shrift — faqat Onest, lokal (`@fontsource/onest`, cyrillic + latin, 400/500/600/700/800); `html` o'lchami `clamp(16px, calc(10px + .45vw), 21px)`. Yorug' tema.
- CSP (o'zgarmaydi): `default-src 'self'; script-src 'self'; style-src 'self'; img-src 'self' data: blob:; connect-src 'self'; font-src 'self'; object-src 'none'; frame-ancestors 'none'; base-uri 'none'; form-action 'self'`. Demak: inline `<style>`/`<script>` va `on*=` atributlari yo'q, `dangerouslySetInnerHTML` yo'q, shriftlar `data:` URI bo'lmaydi (`build.assetsInlineLimit: 0`), CDN yo'q. React `style={{}}` — faqat CSSOM, lekin klass afzal.
- Ikonkalar — inline SVG React komponentlari (`src/ui/icons.tsx`), ikonka-shrift yo'q.
- Barcha UI matnlari `t()` orqali (`src/i18n/ru.ts`); interfeys ruscha, reja prozasi va kod izohlari o'zbekcha.
- Formatlar (spec 7.7): razryadlar bo'shliq bilan (U+00A0), kasr vergul bilan, manfiy belgi `−`, sana `ДД.ММ.ГГГГ`, vaqt zonasi `Asia/Tashkent`, hafta dushanbadan.
- Accessibility: matn kontrasti ≥ 4.5:1, matn bo'lmagan elementlar ≥ 3:1 (`src/styles/tokens.test.ts` tekshiradi); ko'rinadigan fokus; to'liq klaviatura; nav/menyu/dialoglarda ARIA; `prefers-reduced-motion`; teginish nishonlari ≥ 44 px (`max-width: 767.98px` va `pointer: coarse`).
- Responsive (spec 7.5): ≥1280 — to'liq, bo'limlar sig'masa 2-qatorga o'tadi (kesilmaydi); 768–1279 — chap menyu yig'iladigan panel; <768 — bo'limlar gorizontal skroll, chap menyu `Drawer`, filtrlar `BottomSheet`, 1 ustun, jadvallar kartochka; sahifada gorizontal skroll yo'q.
- API: prefiks `/api/v1`; `credentials: 'same-origin'`; GET/HEAD/OPTIONS dan boshqa har bir metodda `X-CSRF-Token` = `__Host-ugk_csrf` cookie qiymati (backend — imzolangan double-submit; frontend cookie'ni o'zgarishsiz ko'chiradi); xato formati `{"error": {"code", "message", "request_id", "params"?}}`; 401 → login (`next` bilan), 403 `password_change_required` → `#password`, boshqa 403 → «Нет доступа», 429 → toast + `Retry-After`, tarmoq xatosi → `ErrorBanner` «Повторить».
- Node faqat Docker'da (host'dagi Node v24 ishlatilmaydi): `NODE="docker compose -f deploy/compose.frontend.yml run --rm node"`, keyin `$NODE npm ...`. Korporativ TLS proksi uchun `NODE_EXTRA_CA_CERTS=/app/certs/organization-ca-3.crt`. `package-lock.json` commit qilinadi; build'larda `npm ci`.
- Playwright versiyasi va image bir xil: `@playwright/test` `1.49.1` ↔ `mcr.microsoft.com/playwright:v1.49.1-noble`.
- Backend testlari: `TEST="docker compose -f deploy/compose.test.yml run --rm test"`, keyin `$TEST pytest ...` (1-reja). E2E seed buyrug'i faqat `ENV ∈ {dev, test}` da ishlaydi.
- Buyruqlar repo ildizidan (`C:\Users\uge226\Desktop\ugekazna`) Git Bash'da. Bind-mount'li `docker run` oldidan `MSYS_NO_PATHCONV=1`.
- Har commit xabari oxirida bo'sh qatordan keyin: `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>`.
- Spec'dan ongli chetlanishlar (ledger'da ham): (1) yuqoridagi token qiymatlari — faqat kontrast uchun; (2) statika `encode zstd gzip` (Caddy'da brotli kodlovchi yo'q); (3) `core.settings` tahrirlash sahifasi bu rejada yo'q — spec 7.6 ro'yxatida yo'q, API (2-reja) tayyor; (4) «Требует внимания» hisoblagichi uchun joy (`NavItem.count`) bor, qiymat manbai — Обзор bo'limi rejasida.

## Review Focus

1. **Sessiya ish o'rtasida tugaydi (idle 30 daqiqa)** — keyingi so'rov 401 → login sahifasi `next` bilan ochiladi va 2FA'dan keyin foydalanuvchi aynan o'sha sahifa va filtrlarga qaytadi: 12-vazifa `returns to the original page with filters after re-login`.
2. **Qo'lda tahrirlangan yoki eskirgan URL** (`br=999`, `per=m13`, rolga ruxsat etilmagan bo'lim) — oq ekran yoki xato emas: filtrlar normallashtiriladi (URL `replace`), ruxsatsiz bo'lim «Нет доступа» ko'rsatadi: 10-vazifa `normalizes unknown branch and period in the URL`, `shows no-access for a forbidden section and not-found for an unknown one`.
3. **TOTP kodi ikki marta yuboriladi** (6-raqamda avto-yuborish + Enter) — backend replay himoyasi ikkinchisini rad etib, foydalanuvchiga «Неверный код» ko'rsatmasligi uchun faqat bitta so'rov ketadi: 12-vazifa `submits the code only once`.
4. **Bitta filialga bog'langan foydalanuvchi** — filial filtri qulflangan, URL'dagi boshqa `br` uning filialiga almashtiriladi, palitrada filiallar yo'q: 10-vazifa `locks the branch filter for a single-branch user`, 11-vazifa `hides branch items for a locked user`.
5. **Login'da 429 (rate limit)** — «Повторите через N с» ko'rsatiladi, login sahifasidan chiqib ketmaydi va parol maydoni tozalanadi: 12-vazifa `shows retry time on 429`.

Chegaralar: `src/ui` domen haqida hech narsa bilmaydi (faqat `i18n` va `api/client` dagi `ApiError`); `src/shell` bo'lim sahifalarini `src/sections/registry.ts` orqali topadi; `src/admin` sahifalari faqat `AdminScreen` orqali ulanadi; `src/test` faqat testlarda import qilinadi.

### Task 1: Frontend karkasi, Docker'dagi Node asboblari va test infratuzilmasi

**Files:**
- Create: `frontend/package.json`, `frontend/tsconfig.json`, `frontend/vite.config.ts`, `frontend/eslint.config.js`, `frontend/.prettierrc.json`, `frontend/index.html`, `frontend/public/favicon.svg`, `frontend/src/vite-env.d.ts`, `frontend/src/main.tsx`, `frontend/src/app/App.tsx`, `frontend/src/test/setup.ts`, `frontend/src/test/viewport.ts`, `frontend/src/test/mockApi.ts`, `frontend/src/test/axe.ts`, `frontend/certs/README.md`, `frontend/certs/organization-ca-3.crt` (nusxa), `deploy/compose.frontend.yml`
- Modify: `.gitignore`, `Makefile`
- Test: `frontend/src/test/infra.test.ts`, `frontend/src/app/App.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces:
  - `NODE="docker compose -f deploy/compose.frontend.yml run --rm node"` — barcha npm buyruqlari shu orqali.
  - npm skriptlari: `build`, `typecheck`, `lint`, `test`, `test:watch`, `gen:api`, `check`.
  - `src/test/mockApi.ts`: `MockRequest { method, path, url, body, headers }`, `MockResponse { status?, body?, headers? }`, `MockHandler = MockResponse | ((req) => MockResponse | Promise<MockResponse>)`, `json(body, status = 200, headers = {})`, `apiError(status, code, message = code, params?, headers = {})`, `mockApi(routes: Record<string, MockHandler>) -> { calls: MockRequest[]; fetchMock }` (kalit: `"GET /auth/me"` — `/api/v1` siz yo'l, query'siz).
  - `src/test/viewport.ts`: `installMatchMedia()`, `setViewport(width: number)`, `resetViewport()` (standart 1440).
  - `src/test/axe.ts`: `expectNoAxeViolations(node: Element): Promise<void>`.
  - jsdom URL — `https://ugk.test/` (`__Host-` cookie'lar ishlashi uchun).

- [ ] **Step 1: Korporativ CA va Docker'dagi Node servisi**

```bash
mkdir -p frontend/certs frontend/src/app frontend/src/test frontend/public
cp backend/certs/organization-ca-3.crt frontend/certs/ 2>/dev/null || echo "CA topilmadi — proksisiz tarmoqda kerak emas"
```

`frontend/certs/README.md`:
```markdown
# Korporativ CA

`*.crt` (PEM) fayllar npm TLS-inspeksiya qiluvchi proksi orqasida ishlashi uchun kerak:
`deploy/compose.frontend.yml` va `frontend/Dockerfile` ularni `NODE_EXTRA_CA_CERTS` orqali ulaydi.
Fayl bo'lmasa Node faqat ogohlantirish chiqaradi — proksisiz tarmoqda hammasi ishlaydi.
```

`deploy/compose.frontend.yml`:
```yaml
# Frontend asboblari Docker'da: host'dagi Node versiyasiga bog'liq emas.
#   NODE="docker compose -f deploy/compose.frontend.yml run --rm node"
#   $NODE npm ci && $NODE npm run check
# Bu faqat dev-asbob konteyneri (servis emas): node_modules nomli volume'da, egasi root.
name: ugekazna-fe

services:
  node:
    image: node:22-alpine
    working_dir: /app
    environment:
      NODE_EXTRA_CA_CERTS: /app/certs/organization-ca-3.crt
      npm_config_update_notifier: "false"
      npm_config_fund: "false"
      npm_config_audit: "false"
    volumes:
      - ../frontend:/app
      - fe_node_modules:/app/node_modules
    cap_drop: [ALL]
    cap_add: [CHOWN, DAC_OVERRIDE, FOWNER]
    security_opt: ["no-new-privileges:true"]

volumes:
  fe_node_modules:
```

`.gitignore` oxiriga qo'shing:
```
frontend/coverage/
frontend/e2e/test-results/
frontend/e2e/playwright-report/
```

`Makefile` — `.PHONY` qatoriga `fe-install fe-check` qo'shing va oxiriga:
```makefile
NODE := docker compose -f deploy/compose.frontend.yml run --rm node

fe-install: ## frontend bog'liqliklari (package-lock.json bo'yicha)
	$(NODE) npm ci

fe-check: ## tsc + eslint + vitest
	$(NODE) npm run check
```

```ts
import react from '@vitejs/plugin-react';
import { defineConfig } from 'vitest/config';

export default defineConfig({
  plugins: [react()],
  build: {
    target: 'es2022',
    sourcemap: false,
    // CSP: font-src/img-src 'self' — shrift va rasmlar data: URI bo'lib qolmasin.
    assetsInlineLimit: 0,
    modulePreload: { polyfill: false },
  },
  test: {
    environment: 'jsdom',
    environmentOptions: { jsdom: { url: 'https://ugk.test/' } },
    setupFiles: ['./src/test/setup.ts'],
    include: ['src/**/*.test.{ts,tsx}'],
    css: false,
    restoreMocks: true,
    unstubGlobals: true,
  },
});
```

```js
import js from '@eslint/js';

    languageOptions: { globals: { ...globals.node } },

);
```

```tsx
import { StrictMode } from 'react';
import { createRoot } from 'react-dom/client';
import { App } from './app/App';

const container = document.getElementById('root');
if (!container) throw new Error('#root elementi topilmadi');
createRoot(container).render(
  <StrictMode>
    <App />
  </StrictMode>,
```

```ts
import { act } from '@testing-library/react';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { apiError, json, mockApi } from './mockApi';
import { setViewport } from './viewport';

describe('mockApi', () => {
  it('returns mocked JSON and records the call', async () => {
    const api = mockApi({ 'GET /auth/me': json({ id: 1 }) });
    const response = await fetch('/api/v1/auth/me?x=1');
    expect(response.status).toBe(200);
    expect(await response.json()).toEqual({ id: 1 });
    expect(api.calls[0]).toMatchObject({ method: 'GET', path: '/auth/me' });
    expect(api.calls[0]?.url.searchParams.get('x')).toBe('1');
  });

  it('passes the parsed body to function handlers', async () => {
    mockApi({ 'POST /auth/login': (req) => json({ echo: req.body }, 200) });
    const response = await fetch('/api/v1/auth/login', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ a: 1 }) });
    expect(await response.json()).toEqual({ echo: { a: 1 } });
  });

  it('builds the backend error envelope and 204 responses', async () => {
    mockApi({
      'GET /x': apiError(409, 'conflict', 'Конфликт данных'),
      'DELETE /y': { status: 204 },
    });
    const conflict = await fetch('/api/v1/x');
    expect(conflict.status).toBe(409);
    expect(await conflict.json()).toEqual({
      error: { code: 'conflict', message: 'Конфликт данных', request_id: 'req-test' },
    });
    const empty = await fetch('/api/v1/y', { method: 'DELETE' });
    expect(empty.status).toBe(204);
  });

  it('answers unmocked routes with 501', async () => {
    mockApi({});
    const response = await fetch('/api/v1/nope');
    expect(response.status).toBe(501);
  });
});

describe('setViewport', () => {
  it('re-evaluates matchMedia queries and notifies listeners', () => {
    const mql = window.matchMedia('(max-width: 767.98px)');
    const seen: boolean[] = [];
    mql.addEventListener('change', (e) => seen.push(e.matches));
    expect(mql.matches).toBe(false);
    act(() => setViewport(390));
    expect(mql.matches).toBe(true);
    expect(seen).toEqual([true]);
    expect(window.matchMedia('(min-width: 768px) and (max-width: 1279.98px)').matches).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

`frontend/src/test/viewport.ts`:
```ts
/** jsdom'da matchMedia yo'q: oddiy min/max-width so'rovlarini window.innerWidth bo'yicha hisoblaymiz. */
type Listener = (event: MediaQueryListEvent) => void;

interface Entry {
  query: string;
  matches: boolean;
  listeners: Set<Listener>;
}

const entries = new Set<Entry>();

function evaluate(query: string): boolean {
  if (query.includes('prefers-reduced-motion') || query.includes('pointer')) return false;
  const width = window.innerWidth;
  const mins = [...query.matchAll(/min-width:\s*([\d.]+)px/g)].map((m) => Number(m[1]));
  const maxs = [...query.matchAll(/max-width:\s*([\d.]+)px/g)].map((m) => Number(m[1]));
  return mins.every((v) => width >= v) && maxs.every((v) => width <= v);
}

export function installMatchMedia(): void {
  window.matchMedia = (query: string): MediaQueryList => {
    const entry: Entry = { query, matches: evaluate(query), listeners: new Set() };
    entries.add(entry);
    const mql = {
      get matches() {
        return entry.matches;
      },
      media: query,
      onchange: null,
      addEventListener: (_type: string, listener: Listener) => entry.listeners.add(listener),
      removeEventListener: (_type: string, listener: Listener) => entry.listeners.delete(listener),
      addListener: (listener: Listener) => entry.listeners.add(listener),
      removeListener: (listener: Listener) => entry.listeners.delete(listener),
      dispatchEvent: () => true,
    };
    return mql as unknown as MediaQueryList;
  };
}

function setWidth(width: number): void {
  Object.defineProperty(window, 'innerWidth', { configurable: true, writable: true, value: width });
}

export function setViewport(width: number): void {
  setWidth(width);
  for (const entry of entries) {
    const next = evaluate(entry.query);
    if (next === entry.matches) continue;
    entry.matches = next;
    const event = { matches: next, media: entry.query } as MediaQueryListEvent;
    entry.listeners.forEach((listener) => listener(event));
  }
}

export function resetViewport(): void {
  entries.clear();
  setWidth(1440);
}
```

`frontend/src/test/mockApi.ts`:
```ts
import { vi } from 'vitest';

export interface MockRequest {
  method: string;
  path: string;
  url: URL;
  body: unknown;
  headers: Headers;
}

export interface MockResponse {
  status?: number;
  body?: unknown;
  headers?: Record<string, string>;
}

export type MockHandler = MockResponse | ((req: MockRequest) => MockResponse | Promise<MockResponse>);

export function json(body: unknown, status = 200, headers: Record<string, string> = {}): MockResponse {
  return { status, body, headers };
}

export function apiError(
  status: number,
  code: string,
  message = code,
  params?: Record<string, unknown>,
  headers: Record<string, string> = {},
): MockResponse {
  return {
    status,
    headers,
    body: { error: { code, message, request_id: 'req-test', ...(params ? { params } : {}) } },
  };
}

/** fetch'ni almashtiradi. Kalit: "METHOD /yo'l" (/api/v1 prefiksisiz, query'siz). */
export function mockApi(routes: Record<string, MockHandler>) {
  const calls: MockRequest[] = [];
  const fetchMock = vi.fn(async (input: RequestInfo | URL, init: RequestInit = {}) => {
    const raw = typeof input === 'string' ? input : input instanceof URL ? input.href : input.url;
    const url = new URL(raw, 'https://ugk.test');
    const method = (init.method ?? 'GET').toUpperCase();
    const path = url.pathname.replace(/^\/api\/v1/, '');
    const body = typeof init.body === 'string' ? (JSON.parse(init.body) as unknown) : undefined;
    const request: MockRequest = { method, path, url, body, headers: new Headers(init.headers) };
    calls.push(request);
    const handler = routes[`${method} ${path}`];
    const res =
      handler === undefined
        ? apiError(501, 'unmocked', `${method} ${path}`)
        : typeof handler === 'function'
          ? await handler(request)
          : handler;
    const status = res.status ?? 200;
    const hasBody = res.body !== undefined && status !== 204;
    return new Response(hasBody ? JSON.stringify(res.body) : null, {
      status,
      headers: { ...(hasBody ? { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } : {}), ...res.headers },
    });
  });
  vi.stubGlobal('fetch', fetchMock);
  return { calls, fetchMock };
}
```

```ts
/** jsdom rang hisoblay olmaydi — kontrast tokens.test.ts va Playwright'da tekshiriladi. */
export async function expectNoAxeViolations(node: Element): Promise<void> {
  const result = await axe.run(node, {
    rules: { 'color-contrast': { enabled: false }, region: { enabled: false } },
  });
  const problems = result.violations.map(
    (v) => `${v.id}: ${v.nodes.map((n) => n.target.join(' ')).join(', ')}`,
  );
  expect(problems).toEqual([]);
}
```

`frontend/src/i18n/format.test.ts`:
```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { dayStartIso, formatDate, formatDateTime, formatDuration, formatNumber, plural } from './format';

const NBSP = '\u00a0';

describe('formatNumber', () => {
  it('groups thousands with a no-break space and uses a decimal comma', () => {
    expect(formatNumber(1234567.891, 1)).toBe(`1${NBSP}234${NBSP}567,9`);
    expect(formatNumber(0)).toBe('0');
  });
  it('uses a typographic minus and handles non-finite values', () => {
    expect(formatNumber(-1500, 0)).toBe(`−1${NBSP}500`);
    expect(formatNumber(Number.NaN)).toBe('—');
  });
});

describe('dates', () => {
  it('formats in Tashkent time as ДД.ММ.ГГГГ', () => {
    expect(formatDate('2026-09-23T20:30:00Z')).toBe('24.09.2026');
    expect(formatDate('2026-09-24')).toBe('24.09.2026');
    expect(formatDateTime('2026-09-24T05:07:00Z')).toBe('24.09.2026 10:07');
    expect(formatDate('garbage')).toBe('—');
  });
  it('builds Tashkent day boundaries in ISO UTC', () => {
    expect(dayStartIso('2026-09-24')).toBe('2026-09-23T19:00:00.000Z');
    expect(dayStartIso('2026-09-24', 1)).toBe('2026-09-24T19:00:00.000Z');
  });
});

describe('formatDuration', () => {
  it('renders seconds, minutes and hours', () => {
    expect(formatDuration(45_000)).toBe('45 с');
    expect(formatDuration(125_000)).toBe('2 мин 05 с');
    expect(formatDuration(3_780_000)).toBe('1 ч 03 мин');
    expect(formatDuration(-1)).toBe('—');
  });
});

describe('plural', () => {
  it('picks Russian plural forms', () => {
    const forms = ['код', 'кода', 'кодов'] as const;
    expect(plural(1, forms)).toBe('код');
    expect(plural(3, forms)).toBe('кода');
    expect(plural(5, forms)).toBe('кодов');
    expect(plural(11, forms)).toBe('кодов');
    expect(plural(21, forms)).toBe('код');
  });
});
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { contrastRatio, readTokens } from './contrast';
import css from './tokens.css?raw';

const tokens = readTokens(css);
const color = (ref: string): string => {
  if (ref.startsWith('#')) return ref;
  const value = tokens[ref];
  if (!value) throw new Error(`token ${ref} topilmadi`);
  return value;
};

/** [oldingi rang, fon, minimal nisbat] — WCAG 2.1: matn 4.5, matn bo'lmagan 3.0. */
const PAIRS: Array<[string, string, number]> = [
  ['--ink', '--bg', 4.5],
  ['--ink', '--card', 4.5],
  ['--ink', '--soft', 4.5],
  ['--muted', '--bg', 4.5],
  ['--muted', '--card', 4.5],
  ['--muted', '--soft', 4.5],
  ['--muted', '--neg-bg', 4.5],
  ['--brand', '--card', 4.5],
  ['--brand', '--soft', 4.5],
  ['--brand2', '--card', 4.5],
  ['--neg', '--card', 4.5],
  ['--neg', '--bg', 4.5],
  ['--neg', '--neg-bg', 4.5],
  ['--warn', '--warn-bg', 4.5],
  ['--ok', '--ok-bg', 4.5],
  ['--ok', '--card', 4.5],
  ['#ffffff', '--brand2', 4.5],
  ['#ffffff', '--brand', 4.5],
  ['#ffffff', '--neg', 4.5],
  ['#ffffff', '--ok', 4.5],
  ['#ffffff', '--ink', 4.5],
  ['--top-ink', '--top', 4.5],
  ['--top-ink', '--top-end', 4.5],
  ['--top-ink', '--top2', 4.5],
  ['--focus', '--card', 3],
  ['--focus', '--bg', 3],
  ['--focus-on-dark', '--top-end', 3],
];

describe('design tokens', () => {
  it('parses every token used by the contrast table', () => {
    for (const [fg, bg] of PAIRS) {
      expect(color(fg)).toMatch(/^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/);
      expect(color(bg)).toMatch(/^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/);
    }
  });

  it.each(PAIRS)('%s on %s has contrast ≥ %s', (fg, bg, min) => {
    expect(contrastRatio(color(fg), color(bg))).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(min);
  });
});
```

```css
*,

  overflow-x: hidden;
}

}

:focus-visible {
  outline: 2px solid var(--focus);

}

h1,
h2,
h3 {
  text-wrap: balance;
}

.visually-hidden {
  position: absolute !important;
  width: 1px;
  height: 1px;
  margin: -1px;
  padding: 0;
  overflow: hidden;
  clip: rect(0 0 0 0);
  white-space: nowrap;

}

.no-scroll {

}

.mono {

}

.neg {
  color: var(--neg) !important;
}

.muted {
  color: var(--muted);
}

@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  *,
  *::before,
  *::after {
    animation-duration: 0.01ms !important;
    animation-iteration-count: 1 !important;
    transition-duration: 0.01ms !important;
    scroll-behavior: auto !important;
  }
}
```

`frontend/src/styles/contrast.ts`:
```ts
/** WCAG 2.1 nisbiy yorqinlik va kontrast nisbati. */
export function readTokens(css: string): Record<string, string> {
  const out: Record<string, string> = {};
  for (const match of css.matchAll(/(--[\w-]+)\s*:\s*(#[0-9a-fA-F]{6})\b/g)) {
    out[match[1]] = match[2].toLowerCase();
  }
  return out;
}

function channel(value: number): number {
  const s = value / 255;
  return s <= 0.04045 ? s / 12.92 : ((s + 0.055) / 1.055) ** 2.4;
}

export function luminance(hex: string): number {
  const n = Number.parseInt(hex.slice(1), 16);
  return 0.2126 * channel((n >> 16) & 255) + 0.7152 * channel((n >> 8) & 255) + 0.0722 * channel(n & 255);
}

export function contrastRatio(a: string, b: string): number {
  const la = luminance(a);
  const lb = luminance(b);
  const [hi, lo] = la >= lb ? [la, lb] : [lb, la];
  return (hi + 0.05) / (lo + 0.05);
}
```

```ts
} as const satisfies Record<string, string>;

export type RuKey = keyof typeof ru;
```

`frontend/src/i18n/index.ts`:
```ts
import { ru, type RuKey } from './ru';

export type MessageKey = RuKey;

const dictionary: Record<MessageKey, string> = ru;

/** `{name}` o'rniga parametr qo'yiladi; noma'lum joy-tutqich o'zgarmaydi. O'zbekcha — keyingi spec'da. */
export function t(key: MessageKey, params?: Record<string, string | number>): string {
  const template = dictionary[key];
  if (!params) return template;
  return template.replace(/\{(\w+)\}/g, (placeholder, name: string) =>
    name in params ? String(params[name]) : placeholder,
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/i18n/format.ts`:
```ts
/** Spec 7.7: razryadlar bo'shliq bilan, kasr vergul bilan, ДД.ММ.ГГГГ, Toshkent vaqti. */
export const TIME_ZONE = 'Asia/Tashkent';

const numberFormats = new Map<number, Intl.NumberFormat>();

function numberFormat(digits: number): Intl.NumberFormat {
  let format = numberFormats.get(digits);
  if (!format) {
    format = new Intl.NumberFormat('ru-RU', { minimumFractionDigits: digits, maximumFractionDigits: digits });
    numberFormats.set(digits, format);
  }
  return format;
}

export function formatNumber(value: number, digits = 0): string {
  if (!Number.isFinite(value)) return '—';
  return numberFormat(digits)
    .format(value)
    .replace(/[\u202f\u00a0]/g, '\u00a0')
    .replace(/^-/, '\u2212');
}

const dateFormat = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('ru-RU', {
  timeZone: TIME_ZONE,
  day: '2-digit',
  month: '2-digit',
  year: 'numeric',
});
const timeFormat = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('ru-RU', {
  timeZone: TIME_ZONE,
  hour: '2-digit',
  minute: '2-digit',
  hourCycle: 'h23',
});

function toDate(value: string | number | Date): Date | null {
  const date = value instanceof Date ? value : new Date(value);
  return Number.isNaN(date.getTime()) ? null : date;
}

export function formatDate(value: string | number | Date): string {
  const date = toDate(value);
  return date ? dateFormat.format(date) : '—';
}

export function formatDateTime(value: string | number | Date): string {
  const date = toDate(value);
  return date ? `${dateFormat.format(date)} ${timeFormat.format(date)}` : '—';
}

export function formatDuration(ms: number): string {
  if (!Number.isFinite(ms) || ms < 0) return '—';
  const total = Math.round(ms / 1000);
  const hours = Math.floor(total / 3600);
  const minutes = Math.floor((total % 3600) / 60);
  const seconds = total % 60;
  if (hours > 0) return `${hours} ч ${String(minutes).padStart(2, '0')} мин`;
  if (minutes > 0) return `${minutes} мин ${String(seconds).padStart(2, '0')} с`;
  return `${seconds} с`;
}

export function plural(n: number, forms: readonly [string, string, string]): string {
  const mod100 = Math.abs(n) % 100;
  const mod10 = mod100 % 10;
  if (mod100 > 10 && mod100 < 20) return forms[2];
  if (mod10 > 1 && mod10 < 5) return forms[1];
  if (mod10 === 1) return forms[0];
  return forms[2];
}

/** `YYYY-MM-DD` (Toshkent kuni) boshining UTC ISO vaqti; `offsetDays` — kun siljishi. */
export function dayStartIso(date: string, offsetDays = 0): string {
  const start = new Date(`${date}T00:00:00+05:00`);
  return new Date(start.getTime() + offsetDays * 86_400_000).toISOString();
}
```

### Task 3: Hash-router — marshrut tahlili, `safeNext`, `useRoute` va `navigate`

**Ruling (router):** react-router `HashRouter` `#/kazna/svod` (bosh `/` bilan) hosil qiladi, TZ esa `#kazna/svod?br=3` ni talab qiladi. Shuning uchun ~120 qatorli o'z router'i: marshrutlar ro'yxati kichik va qat'iy, `?next=` ni xavfsiz tahlil qilish bizning qo'limizda, bog'liqlik yo'q.

**Files:**
- Create: `frontend/src/router/hash.ts`, `frontend/src/router/store.ts`
- Test: `frontend/src/router/hash.test.ts`, `frontend/src/router/store.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces:
  - `src/router/hash.ts`:
    ```ts
    export type Route =
      | { kind: 'home' }
      | { kind: 'login'; next: string | null }
      | { kind: 'mfa' }
      | { kind: 'mfaEnroll' }
      | { kind: 'password' }
      | { kind: 'sessions' }
      | { kind: 'noaccess' }
      | { kind: 'notfound' }
      | { kind: 'admin'; page: string | null }
      | { kind: 'section'; section: string; page: string | null; br: number | null; per: string | null };
    export type SectionRoute = Extract<Route, { kind: 'section' }>;
    export function parseHash(raw: string): Route;
    export function buildHash(route: Route): string;      // '#...' ('home' va 'notfound' → '#')
    export function safeNext(next: string | null): string | null; // faqat section/admin/sessions, '#' siz
    export function isPublicRoute(route: Route): boolean; // login, mfa, mfaEnroll
    ```
    Hash'lar: `#login?next=…`, `#2fa`, `#2fa/setup`, `#password`, `#account/sessions`, `#noaccess`, `#admin[/<id>]`, `#<bo'lim>[/<sahifa>][?br=&per=]`.
  - `src/router/store.ts`: `useRoute(): Route`, `navigate(to: Route | string, options?: { replace?: boolean }): void`, `currentPath(): string` (hash, `#` siz).

- [ ] **Step 1: Testlarni yozish**

`frontend/src/router/hash.test.ts`:
```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { buildHash, isPublicRoute, parseHash, safeNext, type Route } from './hash';

describe('parseHash', () => {
  it.each<[string, Route]>([
    ['', { kind: 'home' }],
    ['#', { kind: 'home' }],
    ['#kazna/svod?br=3&per=m05', { kind: 'section', section: 'kazna', page: 'svod', br: 3, per: 'm05' }],
    ['#kazna', { kind: 'section', section: 'kazna', page: null, br: null, per: null }],
    ['#/obzor/vnimanie', { kind: 'section', section: 'obzor', page: 'vnimanie', br: null, per: null }],
    ['#kazna/svod?br=0&per=m13', { kind: 'section', section: 'kazna', page: 'svod', br: null, per: null }],
    ['#kazna/svod?br=3abc&per=YTD', { kind: 'section', section: 'kazna', page: 'svod', br: null, per: null }],
    ['#obzor/svod?per=q4&per=ytd', { kind: 'section', section: 'obzor', page: 'svod', br: null, per: 'q4' }],
    ['#2fa', { kind: 'mfa' }],
    ['#2fa/setup', { kind: 'mfaEnroll' }],
    ['#password', { kind: 'password' }],
    ['#account/sessions', { kind: 'sessions' }],
    ['#noaccess', { kind: 'noaccess' }],
    ['#admin', { kind: 'admin', page: null }],
    ['#admin/users', { kind: 'admin', page: 'users' }],
    ['#login', { kind: 'login', next: null }],
    ['#login?next=kazna%2Fsvod%3Fbr%3D3', { kind: 'login', next: 'kazna/svod?br=3' }],
    ['#KAZNA/svod', { kind: 'notfound' }],
    ['#a/b/c', { kind: 'notfound' }],
    ['#account', { kind: 'notfound' }],
    ['#2fa/other', { kind: 'notfound' }],
    ['#admin/Users', { kind: 'notfound' }],
  ])('%s', (hash, expected) => {
    expect(parseHash(hash)).toEqual(expected);
  });
});

describe('buildHash', () => {
  it('builds canonical hashes and omits empty filters', () => {
    expect(buildHash({ kind: 'section', section: 'kazna', page: 'svod', br: 3, per: 'm05' })).toBe(
      '#kazna/svod?br=3&per=m05',
    );
    expect(buildHash({ kind: 'section', section: 'kazna', page: null, br: null, per: null })).toBe('#kazna');
    expect(buildHash({ kind: 'login', next: 'kazna/svod?br=3' })).toBe('#login?next=kazna%2Fsvod%3Fbr%3D3');
    expect(buildHash({ kind: 'login', next: null })).toBe('#login');
    expect(buildHash({ kind: 'mfaEnroll' })).toBe('#2fa/setup');
    expect(buildHash({ kind: 'sessions' })).toBe('#account/sessions');
    expect(buildHash({ kind: 'admin', page: 'audit' })).toBe('#admin/audit');
    expect(buildHash({ kind: 'home' })).toBe('#');
    expect(buildHash({ kind: 'notfound' })).toBe('#');
  });

  it('round-trips through parseHash', () => {
    const route: Route = { kind: 'section', section: 'kadry', page: 'fot', br: 12, per: 'q2' };
    expect(parseHash(buildHash(route))).toEqual(route);
  });
});

describe('safeNext', () => {
  it('keeps only in-app destinations and canonicalises them', () => {
    expect(safeNext('kazna/svod?br=3&per=bad')).toBe('kazna/svod?br=3');
    expect(safeNext('#admin/users')).toBe('admin/users');
    expect(safeNext('account/sessions')).toBe('account/sessions');
  });

  it('rejects auth routes, garbage and absolute URLs', () => {
    expect(safeNext(null)).toBeNull();
    expect(safeNext('')).toBeNull();
    expect(safeNext('login?next=kazna')).toBeNull();
    expect(safeNext('2fa')).toBeNull();
    expect(safeNext('password')).toBeNull();
    expect(safeNext('javascript:alert(1)')).toBeNull();
    expect(safeNext('//evil.example/x')).toBeNull();
    expect(safeNext('https://evil.example')).toBeNull();
  });
});

it('marks only sign-in routes as public', () => {
  expect(isPublicRoute({ kind: 'login', next: null })).toBe(true);
  expect(isPublicRoute({ kind: 'mfa' })).toBe(true);
  expect(isPublicRoute({ kind: 'mfaEnroll' })).toBe(true);
  expect(isPublicRoute({ kind: 'password' })).toBe(false);
  expect(isPublicRoute({ kind: 'home' })).toBe(false);
});
```

`frontend/src/router/store.test.tsx`:
```tsx
import { act, renderHook } from '@testing-library/react';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { currentPath, navigate, useRoute } from './store';

describe('router store', () => {
  it('pushes a new history entry and updates useRoute', () => {
    const { result } = renderHook(() => useRoute());
    expect(result.current).toEqual({ kind: 'home' });
    const before = window.history.length;
    act(() => navigate({ kind: 'section', section: 'kazna', page: 'svod', br: 3, per: null }));
    expect(window.location.hash).toBe('#kazna/svod?br=3');
    expect(window.history.length).toBe(before + 1);
    expect(result.current).toEqual({ kind: 'section', section: 'kazna', page: 'svod', br: 3, per: null });
    expect(currentPath()).toBe('kazna/svod?br=3');
  });

  it('replaces the current entry when asked', () => {
    const { result } = renderHook(() => useRoute());
    act(() => navigate('#admin/users'));
    const before = window.history.length;
    act(() => navigate('admin/audit', { replace: true }));
    expect(window.history.length).toBe(before);
    expect(result.current).toEqual({ kind: 'admin', page: 'audit' });
  });

  it('does nothing when the target equals the current hash', () => {
    act(() => navigate('#obzor/svod'));
    const before = window.history.length;
    act(() => navigate({ kind: 'section', section: 'obzor', page: 'svod', br: null, per: null }));
    expect(window.history.length).toBe(before);
  });

  it('reacts to hashchange events from the browser', () => {
    const { result } = renderHook(() => useRoute());
    act(() => {
      window.history.replaceState(null, '', '#2fa');
      window.dispatchEvent(new HashChangeEvent('hashchange'));
    });
    expect(result.current).toEqual({ kind: 'mfa' });
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implementatsiya**

`frontend/src/router/hash.ts`:
```ts
/** TZ 5: "#<раздел>/<страница>" + filtrlar parametrlarda. Marshrutlar qat'iy ro'yxat; qolgani — notfound. */
export type Route =
  | { kind: 'home' }
  | { kind: 'login'; next: string | null }
  | { kind: 'mfa' }
  | { kind: 'mfaEnroll' }
  | { kind: 'password' }
  | { kind: 'sessions' }
  | { kind: 'noaccess' }
  | { kind: 'notfound' }
  | { kind: 'admin'; page: string | null }
  | { kind: 'section'; section: string; page: string | null; br: number | null; per: string | null };

export type SectionRoute = Extract<Route, { kind: 'section' }>;

const ID_RE = /^[a-z][a-z0-9_-]{0,31}$/;
const PER_RE = /^(?:ytd|q[1-4]|m(?:0[1-9]|1[0-2]))$/;
const BR_RE = /^[1-9]\d{0,4}$/;
const NOT_FOUND: Route = { kind: 'notfound' };

export function parseHash(raw: string): Route {
  const hash = raw.startsWith('#') ? raw.slice(1) : raw;
  const q = hash.indexOf('?');
  const path = q >= 0 ? hash.slice(0, q) : hash;
  const params = new URLSearchParams(q >= 0 ? hash.slice(q + 1) : '');
  const parts = path.split('/').filter((part) => part !== '');
  if (parts.length === 0) return { kind: 'home' };
  if (parts.length > 2) return NOT_FOUND;
  const [first, second] = parts as [string, string | undefined];

  switch (first) {
    case 'login':
      return second === undefined ? { kind: 'login', next: safeNext(params.get('next')) } : NOT_FOUND;
    case '2fa':
      if (second === undefined) return { kind: 'mfa' };
      return second === 'setup' ? { kind: 'mfaEnroll' } : NOT_FOUND;
    case 'password':
      return second === undefined ? { kind: 'password' } : NOT_FOUND;
    case 'account':
      return second === 'sessions' ? { kind: 'sessions' } : NOT_FOUND;
    case 'noaccess':
      return second === undefined ? { kind: 'noaccess' } : NOT_FOUND;
    case 'admin':
      if (second === undefined) return { kind: 'admin', page: null };
      return ID_RE.test(second) ? { kind: 'admin', page: second } : NOT_FOUND;
  }

  if (!ID_RE.test(first) || (second !== undefined && !ID_RE.test(second))) return NOT_FOUND;
  const br = params.get('br');
  const per = params.get('per');
  return {
    kind: 'section',
    section: first,
    page: second ?? null,
    br: br !== null && BR_RE.test(br) ? Number(br) : null,
    per: per !== null && PER_RE.test(per) ? per : null,
  };
}

export function buildHash(route: Route): string {
  switch (route.kind) {
    case 'home':
    case 'notfound':
      return '#';
    case 'login':
      return route.next ? `#login?next=${encodeURIComponent(route.next)}` : '#login';
    case 'mfa':
      return '#2fa';
    case 'mfaEnroll':
      return '#2fa/setup';
    case 'password':
      return '#password';
    case 'sessions':
      return '#account/sessions';
    case 'noaccess':
      return '#noaccess';
    case 'admin':
      return route.page ? `#admin/${route.page}` : '#admin';
    case 'section': {
      const params = new URLSearchParams();
      if (route.br !== null) params.set('br', String(route.br));
      if (route.per !== null) params.set('per', route.per);
      const query = params.toString();
      return `#${route.section}${route.page ? `/${route.page}` : ''}${query ? `?${query}` : ''}`;
    }
  }
}

/** Login'dan keyin qaytiladigan joy: faqat ilova ichidagi sahifa, kanonik ko'rinishda (open redirect yo'q). */
export function safeNext(next: string | null): string | null {
  if (!next) return null;
  const route = parseHash(next);
  if (route.kind !== 'section' && route.kind !== 'admin' && route.kind !== 'sessions') return null;
  return buildHash(route).slice(1);
}

export function isPublicRoute(route: Route): boolean {
  return route.kind === 'login' || route.kind === 'mfa' || route.kind === 'mfaEnroll';
}
```

`frontend/src/router/store.ts`:
```ts
import { useMemo, useSyncExternalStore } from 'react';
import { buildHash, parseHash, type Route } from './hash';

type Listener = () => void;
const listeners = new Set<Listener>();

function emit(): void {
  listeners.forEach((listener) => listener());
}

if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
  window.addEventListener('hashchange', emit);
  window.addEventListener('popstate', emit);
}

function subscribe(listener: Listener): () => void {
  listeners.add(listener);
  return () => {
    listeners.delete(listener);
  };
}

function snapshot(): string {
  return window.location.hash;
}

export function useRoute(): Route {
  const hash = useSyncExternalStore(subscribe, snapshot, snapshot);
  return useMemo(() => parseHash(hash), [hash]);
}

export function currentPath(): string {
  return window.location.hash.replace(/^#/, '');
}

/** pushState/replaceState + qo'lda xabar: hashchange'ni kutmasdan, sinxron yangilanadi. */
export function navigate(to: Route | string, options: { replace?: boolean } = {}): void {
  const target = typeof to === 'string' ? (to.startsWith('#') ? to : `#${to}`) : buildHash(to);
  const normalized = target === '#' ? '' : target;
  if (normalized === window.location.hash) return;
  const url = `${window.location.pathname}${window.location.search}${target}`;
  if (options.replace) window.history.replaceState(window.history.state, '', url);
  else window.history.pushState(null, '', url);
  emit();
}
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { apiError, json, mockApi } from '../test/mockApi';
import { api, ApiError, apiUrl, isApiError, readCookie } from './client';

describe('apiUrl', () => {
  it('adds the prefix and skips empty query values', () => {
    expect(apiUrl('/audit', { page: 2, action: 'auth.', user_id: null, result: undefined, q: '' })).toBe(
      '/api/v1/audit?page=2&action=auth.',
    );
    expect(apiUrl('/auth/me')).toBe('/api/v1/auth/me');
  });
});

describe('api', () => {
  it('GETs JSON with same-origin credentials and no CSRF header', async () => {
    document.cookie = '__Host-ugk_csrf=tok123; Secure; Path=/';
    const server = mockApi({ 'GET /auth/me': json({ id: 1 }) });
    await expect(api('/auth/me')).resolves.toEqual({ id: 1 });
    const init = server.fetchMock.mock.calls[0]?.[1];
    expect(init?.credentials).toBe('same-origin');
    expect(server.calls[0]?.headers.get('X-CSRF-Token')).toBeNull();
  });

  it('sends the CSRF token and JSON body on unsafe methods', async () => {
    document.cookie = '__Host-ugk_csrf=tok123; Secure; Path=/';
    expect(readCookie('__Host-ugk_csrf')).toBe('tok123');
    const server = mockApi({ 'PATCH /admin/users/5': json({ ok: true }) });
    await api('/admin/users/5', { method: 'PATCH', body: { full_name: 'А' } });
    expect(server.calls[0]?.headers.get('X-CSRF-Token')).toBe('tok123');
    expect(server.calls[0]?.headers.get('Content-Type')).toBe('application/json');
    expect(server.calls[0]?.body).toEqual({ full_name: 'А' });
  });

  it('returns undefined for 204', async () => {
    mockApi({ 'POST /auth/logout': { status: 204 } });
    await expect(api('/auth/logout', { method: 'POST' })).resolves.toBeUndefined();
  });

  it('turns the error envelope into ApiError', async () => {
    mockApi({ 'POST /admin/users': apiError(409, 'username_taken', 'Логин уже занят', { field: 'username' }) });
    const error = await api('/admin/users', { method: 'POST', body: {} }).catch((e: unknown) => e);
    expect(error).toBeInstanceOf(ApiError);
    expect(error).toMatchObject({
      status: 409,
      code: 'username_taken',
      message: 'Логин уже занят',
      requestId: 'req-test',
      params: { field: 'username' },
      retryAfter: null,
    });
    expect(isApiError(error, 409, 'username_taken')).toBe(true);
    expect(isApiError(error, 401)).toBe(false);
  });

  it('reads Retry-After on 429', async () => {
    mockApi({
      'POST /auth/login': apiError(429, 'rate_limited', 'Слишком много запросов', { retry_after: 42 }, { 'Retry-After': '42' }),
    });
    const error = await api('/auth/login', { method: 'POST', body: {} }).catch((e: unknown) => e);
    expect(error).toMatchObject({ status: 429, retryAfter: 42 });
  });

  it('handles non-JSON errors and network failures', async () => {
    mockApi({ 'GET /x': { status: 502 } });
    await expect(api('/x')).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 502, code: 'http_502', message: 'Не удалось выполнить запрос' });
    mockApi({ 'GET /y': () => Promise.reject(new TypeError('Failed to fetch')) });
    await expect(api('/y')).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 0, code: 'network' });
  });

  it('rethrows aborts untouched', async () => {
    const abort = Object.assign(new Error('aborted'), { name: 'AbortError' });
    mockApi({ 'GET /z': () => Promise.reject(abort) });
    await expect(api('/z')).rejects.toBe(abort);
  });
});
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { ApiError } from './client';
import { createQueryClient, handleApiError, type GlobalErrorHooks } from './queryClient';

function hooks(): GlobalErrorHooks {
  return { onUnauthenticated: vi.fn(), onPasswordChange: vi.fn(), onForbidden: vi.fn(), notify: vi.fn() };
}

describe('handleApiError', () => {
  it('redirects to login on 401', () => {
    const h = hooks();
    handleApiError(new ApiError(401, 'session_expired', 'x'), 'query', h);
    expect(h.onUnauthenticated).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
  });

  it('sends password_change_required to the password page', () => {
    const h = hooks();
    handleApiError(new ApiError(403, 'password_change_required', 'x'), 'mutation', h);
    expect(h.onPasswordChange).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
    expect(h.onForbidden).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('shows no-access for query 403 and a toast for mutation 403', () => {
    const h = hooks();
    handleApiError(new ApiError(403, 'scope_violation', 'Нет доступа к данным этого филиала'), 'query', h);
    expect(h.onForbidden).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
    handleApiError(new ApiError(403, 'csrf_failed', 'Запрос отклонён'), 'mutation', h);
    expect(h.notify).toHaveBeenCalledWith('Запрос отклонён');
  });

  it('notifies about 429 with the retry time', () => {
    const h = hooks();
    handleApiError(new ApiError(429, 'rate_limited', 'x', null, {}, 30), 'query', h);
    expect(h.notify).toHaveBeenCalledWith('Слишком много запросов. Повторите через 30 с.');
  });

  it('leaves validation, conflicts, server and network errors to the component', () => {
    const h = hooks();
    for (const status of [0, 404, 409, 422, 500]) handleApiError(new ApiError(status, 'x', 'x'), 'query', h);
    handleApiError(new Error('plain'), 'query', h);
    expect(h.onUnauthenticated).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(h.onForbidden).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(h.notify).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});

describe('createQueryClient', () => {
  it('routes query errors through the hooks unless meta.silent is set', async () => {
    const h = hooks();
    const client = createQueryClient(h);
    const fail = () => Promise.reject(new ApiError(401, 'unauthenticated', 'x'));
    await client.fetchQuery({ queryKey: ['silent'], queryFn: fail, retry: false, meta: { silent: true } }).catch(() => undefined);
    expect(h.onUnauthenticated).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    await client.fetchQuery({ queryKey: ['loud'], queryFn: fail, retry: false }).catch(() => undefined);
    expect(h.onUnauthenticated).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
  });

  it('uses the spec cache defaults and retries only transient errors', () => {
    const client = createQueryClient(hooks());
    const defaults = client.getDefaultOptions().queries;
    expect(defaults?.staleTime).toBe(5 * 60_000);
    const retry = defaults?.retry as (count: number, error: Error) => boolean;
    expect(retry(0, new ApiError(503, 'x', 'x'))).toBe(true);
    expect(retry(0, new ApiError(0, 'network', 'x'))).toBe(true);
    expect(retry(2, new ApiError(503, 'x', 'x'))).toBe(false);
    expect(retry(0, new ApiError(403, 'forbidden', 'x'))).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implementatsiya**

`frontend/src/api/client.ts`:
```ts
import { t } from '../i18n';

export const API_PREFIX = '/api/v1';
export const CSRF_COOKIE = '__Host-ugk_csrf';
export const CSRF_HEADER = 'X-CSRF-Token';
/** Backend imzolangan double-submit: GET/HEAD/OPTIONS dan boshqa har bir so'rovda cookie qiymati sarlavhada. */
const SAFE_METHODS = new Set(['GET', 'HEAD', 'OPTIONS']);

export type Method = 'GET' | 'HEAD' | 'OPTIONS' | 'POST' | 'PUT' | 'PATCH' | 'DELETE';
export type Query = Record<string, string | number | boolean | null | undefined>;

export interface RequestOptions {
  method?: Method;
  body?: unknown;
  query?: Query;
  signal?: AbortSignal;
}

/** Backend `{"error": {code, message, request_id, params?}}` formatidagi xato. */
export class ApiError extends Error {
  constructor(
    readonly status: number,
    readonly code: string,
    message: string,
    readonly requestId: string | null = null,
    readonly params: Record<string, unknown> = {},
    readonly retryAfter: number | null = null,
  ) {
    super(message);
    this.name = 'ApiError';
  }
}

export function isApiError(error: unknown, status?: number, code?: string): error is ApiError {
  return (
    error instanceof ApiError &&
    (status === undefined || error.status === status) &&
    (code === undefined || error.code === code)
  );
}

export function readCookie(name: string): string | null {
  for (const part of document.cookie.split(';')) {
    const [key, ...rest] = part.trim().split('=');
    if (key === name) return decodeURIComponent(rest.join('='));
  }
  return null;
}

export function apiUrl(path: string, query?: Query): string {
  const params = new URLSearchParams();
  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(query ?? {})) {
    if (value === null || value === undefined || value === '') continue;
    params.set(key, String(value));
  }
  const qs = params.toString();
  return `${API_PREFIX}${path}${qs ? `?${qs}` : ''}`;
}

interface ErrorEnvelope {
  error: { code: string; message: string; request_id?: string | null; params?: Record<string, unknown> };
}

function isEnvelope(payload: unknown): payload is ErrorEnvelope {
  if (typeof payload !== 'object' || payload === null || !('error' in payload)) return false;
  const error = (payload as { error: unknown }).error;
  return typeof error === 'object' && error !== null && 'code' in error && 'message' in error;
}

function toApiError(response: Response, payload: unknown): ApiError {
  const body = isEnvelope(payload) ? payload.error : null;
  const header = response.headers.get('Retry-After');
  const fromParams = body?.params?.retry_after;
  const retryAfter =
    header !== null && /^\d+$/.test(header)
      ? Number(header)
      : typeof fromParams === 'number'
        ? fromParams
        : null;
  return new ApiError(
    response.status,
    body?.code ?? `http_${response.status}`,
    body?.message ?? t('error.generic'),
    body?.request_id ?? null,
    body?.params ?? {},
    retryAfter,
  );
}

export async function api<T>(path: string, options: RequestOptions = {}): Promise<T> {
  const method = options.method ?? 'GET';
  const headers: Record<string, string> = { Accept: 'application/json' };
  if (options.body !== undefined) headers['Content-Type'] = 'application/json';
  if (!SAFE_METHODS.has(method)) {
    const token = readCookie(CSRF_COOKIE);
    if (token) headers[CSRF_HEADER] = token;
  }
  let response: Response;
  try {
    response = await fetch(apiUrl(path, options.query), {
      method,
      headers,
      credentials: 'same-origin',
      body: options.body === undefined ? undefined : JSON.stringify(options.body),
      signal: options.signal,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    if ((error as { name?: string } | null)?.name === 'AbortError') throw error;
    throw new ApiError(0, 'network', t('error.network'));
  }
  if (response.status === 204) return undefined as T;
  const payload: unknown = await response.json().catch(() => null);
  if (response.ok) return payload as T;
  throw toApiError(response, payload);
}
```

`frontend/src/api/queryClient.ts`:
```ts
import { keepPreviousData, MutationCache, QueryCache, QueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { t } from '../i18n';
import { ApiError } from './client';

interface AppMeta extends Record<string, unknown> {
  /** true — global ishlov (redirect/toast) o'tkazib yuboriladi; komponent o'zi ko'rsatadi. */
  silent?: boolean;
}

declare module '@tanstack/react-query' {
  interface Register {
    queryMeta: AppMeta;
    mutationMeta: AppMeta;
  }
}

export interface GlobalErrorHooks {
  onUnauthenticated(): void;
  onPasswordChange(): void;
  onForbidden(): void;
  notify(message: string): void;
}

/** Spec 8: 401 → login; 403 password_change_required → parol; 403 → нет доступа / toast; 429 → toast. */
export function handleApiError(error: unknown, kind: 'query' | 'mutation', hooks: GlobalErrorHooks): void {
  if (!(error instanceof ApiError)) return;
  if (error.status === 401) {
    hooks.onUnauthenticated();
    return;
  }
  if (error.status === 403 && error.code === 'password_change_required') {
    hooks.onPasswordChange();
    return;
  }
  if (error.status === 429) {
    hooks.notify(t('error.rateLimited', { seconds: error.retryAfter ?? 60 }));
    return;
  }
  if (error.status === 403) {
    if (kind === 'query') hooks.onForbidden();
    else hooks.notify(error.message);
  }
}

function shouldRetry(failureCount: number, error: Error): boolean {
  if (failureCount >= 2) return false;
  if (error instanceof ApiError) return error.status === 0 || error.status >= 500;
  return true;
}

export function createQueryClient(hooks: GlobalErrorHooks): QueryClient {
  return new QueryClient({
    queryCache: new QueryCache({
      onError: (error, query) => {
        if (!query.meta?.silent) handleApiError(error, 'query', hooks);
      },
    }),
    mutationCache: new MutationCache({
      onError: (error, _variables, _context, mutation) => {
        if (!mutation.meta?.silent) handleApiError(error, 'mutation', hooks);
      },
    }),
    defaultOptions: {
      queries: {
        staleTime: 5 * 60_000,
        placeholderData: keepPreviousData,
        refetchOnWindowFocus: false,
        retry: shouldRetry,
      },
      mutations: { retry: false },
    },
  });
}
```

### Task 5: Ikonkalar va forma primitivlari — Button, IconButton, TextInput, PasswordInput, Checkbox, FormError, OtpInput, Select, Combobox

**Files:**
- Create: `frontend/src/ui/cx.ts`, `frontend/src/ui/icons.tsx`, `frontend/src/ui/Button.tsx`, `frontend/src/ui/Field.tsx`, `frontend/src/ui/OtpInput.tsx`, `frontend/src/ui/Select.tsx`, `frontend/src/ui/Combobox.tsx`, `frontend/src/ui/controls.css`
- Modify: `frontend/src/i18n/ru.ts` (kalitlar)
- Test: `frontend/src/ui/controls.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `t`, `MessageKey` (Task 2), `ApiError` (Task 4), `expectNoAxeViolations` (Task 1).
- Produces:
  - `cx(...parts: Array<string | false | null | undefined>) -> string`.
  - `Icon({ name: IconName; className?; title? })`, `type IconName` = `grid people cart bank bolt card box in out table cal doc target check net diff factory plug list clock alert pct coins menu close search link print lock user logout key shield copy download eye eyeOff filter chevronDown chevronLeft chevronRight refresh panel plus edit info database history layers`.
  - `Button` (`forwardRef<HTMLButtonElement>`, props: `variant?: 'primary' | 'secondary' | 'ghost' | 'danger'`, `icon?: IconName`, `loading?: boolean`, `block?: boolean` + button atributlari; `type` standart `'button'`).
  - `IconButton` (`icon: IconName`, `label: string`, `variant?: 'default' | 'ghost'`).
  - `Field({ label, hint?, error?, required?, className?, hideLabel?, children(ids: { id; describedBy; invalid }) })`.
  - `TextInput`, `PasswordInput` (`forwardRef<HTMLInputElement>`, `label`, `hint?`, `error?: string | null`, `hideLabel?` + input atributlari).
  - `Checkbox({ label: ReactNode; checked; onChange(checked: boolean); hint?; disabled?; name? })`.
  - `FormError({ error: unknown })` — `ApiError.message` yoki umumiy matn, `role="alert"`.
  - `OtpInput({ label; value; onChange(v); onComplete?(v); mode?: 'totp' | 'backup'; error?; hint?; disabled?; focusOnMount?; testId? })` — `backup` rejimi backend formati `XXXX-XXXX-XXXX` (12 belgi, ≤14 bilan chiziqchalar).
  - `Select({ label; value: string; options: readonly SelectOption[]; onChange(v: string); hint?; error?; disabled?; icon?: IconName; hideLabel?; className?; required?; name? })`, `SelectOption { value: string; label: string }`.
  - `Combobox({ label; options: readonly ComboOption[]; value: string | null; onChange(v: string | null); placeholder?; hint?; error?; required?; disabled?; emptyText? })`, `ComboOption { value: string; label: string; hint?: string }`.

`frontend/src/ui/controls.test.tsx`:
```tsx
import { render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { ApiError } from '../api/client';
import { expectNoAxeViolations } from '../test/axe';
import { Button, IconButton } from './Button';
import { Combobox, type ComboOption } from './Combobox';
import { Checkbox, FormError, PasswordInput, TextInput } from './Field';
import { OtpInput } from './OtpInput';
import { Select } from './Select';

describe('Button', () => {
  it('is disabled and busy while loading', async () => {
    const onClick = vi.fn();
    render(<Button loading onClick={onClick}>Сохранить</Button>);
    const button = screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Сохранить' });
    expect(button).toBeDisabled();
    expect(button).toHaveAttribute('aria-busy', 'true');
    expect(button).toHaveAttribute('type', 'button');
    await userEvent.click(button);
    expect(onClick).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('names icon-only buttons with their label', () => {
    render(<IconButton icon="close" label="Закрыть" />);
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Закрыть' })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});

describe('TextInput', () => {
  it('links label, hint and error to the input', () => {
    render(<TextInput label="Логин" hint="латиница" error="Обязательное поле" />);
    const input = screen.getByLabelText('Логин');
    expect(input).toHaveAttribute('aria-invalid', 'true');
    expect(input).toHaveAccessibleDescription('латиница Обязательное поле');
  });
});

describe('PasswordInput', () => {
  it('toggles visibility with a pressed button', async () => {
    render(<PasswordInput label="Пароль" />);
    const input = screen.getByLabelText('Пароль');
    expect(input).toHaveAttribute('type', 'password');
    const toggle = screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Показать пароль' });
    await userEvent.click(toggle);
    expect(input).toHaveAttribute('type', 'text');
    expect(toggle).toHaveAttribute('aria-pressed', 'true');
  });
});

describe('Checkbox', () => {
  it('reports the checked state', async () => {
    const onChange = vi.fn();
    render(<Checkbox label="Я сохранил коды" checked={false} onChange={onChange} />);
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByLabelText('Я сохранил коды'));
    expect(onChange).toHaveBeenCalledWith(true);
  });
});

describe('FormError', () => {
  it('shows the server message as an alert', () => {
    render(<FormError error={new ApiError(409, 'last_admin', 'Нельзя оставить систему без администратора')} />);
    expect(screen.getByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent('Нельзя оставить систему без администратора');
  });

  it('renders nothing without an error', () => {
    const { container } = render(<FormError error={null} />);
    expect(container).toBeEmptyDOMElement();
  });
});

function Otp({ onComplete, mode = 'totp' }: { onComplete(v: string): void; mode?: 'totp' | 'backup' }) {
  const [value, setValue] = useState('');
  return <OtpInput label="Код" value={value} onChange={setValue} onComplete={onComplete} mode={mode} />;
}

describe('OtpInput', () => {
  it('keeps digits only and completes once at six digits', async () => {
    const onComplete = vi.fn();
    render(<Otp onComplete={onComplete} />);
    const input = screen.getByLabelText('Код');
    expect(input).toHaveAttribute('inputmode', 'numeric');
    expect(input).toHaveAttribute('autocomplete', 'one-time-code');
    await userEvent.type(input, '12 3-45a67');
    expect(input).toHaveValue('123456');
    expect(onComplete).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(onComplete).toHaveBeenCalledWith('123456');
  });

  it('accepts pasted backup codes in upper case', async () => {
    const onComplete = vi.fn();
    render(<Otp onComplete={onComplete} mode="backup" />);
    const input = screen.getByLabelText('Код');
    await userEvent.click(input);
    await userEvent.paste('abcd-efgh-2345!');
    expect(input).toHaveValue('ABCD-EFGH-2345');
    expect(onComplete).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});

describe('Select', () => {
  it('reports the chosen value', async () => {
    const onChange = vi.fn();
    render(
      <Select
        label="Период"
        value="ytd"
        options={[
          { value: 'ytd', label: 'С начала года' },
          { value: 'q1', label: 'I квартал' },
        ]}
        onChange={onChange}
      />,
    );
    await userEvent.selectOptions(screen.getByLabelText('Период'), 'q1');
    expect(onChange).toHaveBeenCalledWith('q1');
  });
});

const POSITIONS: ComboOption[] = [
  { value: '1', label: 'Администратор' },
  { value: '2', label: 'Экономист филиала', hint: 'economist_filiala' },
  { value: '3', label: 'Специалист казначейства' },
];

function Combo({ onChange }: { onChange(v: string | null): void }) {
  const [value, setValue] = useState<string | null>('1');
  return (
    <Combobox
      label="Должность"
      options={POSITIONS}
      value={value}
      onChange={(v) => {
        setValue(v);
        onChange(v);
      }}
    />
  );
}

describe('Combobox', () => {
  it('filters by typing and selects with the keyboard', async () => {
    const onChange = vi.fn();
    render(<Combo onChange={onChange} />);
    const input = screen.getByRole('combobox', { name: 'Должность' });
    expect(input).toHaveValue('Администратор');
    await userEvent.clear(input);
    await userEvent.type(input, 'кон');
    expect(screen.getAllByRole('option')).toHaveLength(1);
    await userEvent.keyboard('{ArrowDown}{Enter}');
    expect(onChange).toHaveBeenCalledWith('2');
    expect(input).toHaveValue('Экономист филиала');
    expect(input).toHaveAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
  });

  it('restores the selected label on Escape', async () => {
    render(<Combo onChange={vi.fn()} />);
    const input = screen.getByRole('combobox', { name: 'Должность' });
    await userEvent.clear(input);
    await userEvent.type(input, 'xyz');
    expect(screen.getByText('Ничего не найдено')).toBeInTheDocument();
    await userEvent.keyboard('{Escape}');
    expect(input).toHaveValue('Администратор');
  });
});

it('has no axe violations in a typical form', async () => {
  const { container } = render(
    <form aria-label="Тест">
      <TextInput label="Логин" required />
      <PasswordInput label="Пароль" error="Неверный пароль" />
      <Select label="Период" value="a" options={[{ value: 'a', label: 'A' }]} onChange={vi.fn()} />
      <Combobox label="Должность" options={POSITIONS} value={null} onChange={vi.fn()} />
      <Checkbox label="Запомнить" checked={false} onChange={vi.fn()} />
      <OtpInput label="Код" value="" onChange={vi.fn()} />
      <Button type="submit">Войти</Button>
    </form>,
  );
  await expectNoAxeViolations(container);
});
```

```ts
export function cx(...parts: Array<string | false | null | undefined>): string {

}
```

```tsx
} satisfies Record<string, ReactElement>;

export type IconName = keyof typeof PATHS;

interface IconProps {
  name: IconName;
  className?: string;
  /** Berilsa — ikonka mustaqil ma'no tashiydi (role="img"), aks holda bezak (aria-hidden). */
  title?: string;
}

export function Icon({ name, className, title }: IconProps) {
  return (
    <svg
      className={className ? `icon ${className}` : 'icon'}
      viewBox="0 0 24 24"
      fill="none"
      stroke="currentColor"
      strokeWidth={1.8}
      strokeLinecap="round"
      strokeLinejoin="round"
      aria-hidden={title ? undefined : true}
      role={title ? 'img' : undefined}
      focusable="false"
    >
      {title ? <title>{title}</title> : null}
      {PATHS[name]}
    </svg>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/ui/Button.tsx`:
```tsx
import { forwardRef, type ButtonHTMLAttributes } from 'react';
import { cx } from './cx';
import { Icon, type IconName } from './icons';
import './controls.css';

export type ButtonVariant = 'primary' | 'secondary' | 'ghost' | 'danger';

export interface ButtonProps extends ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement> {
  variant?: ButtonVariant;
  icon?: IconName;
  loading?: boolean;
  block?: boolean;
}

export const Button = forwardRef<HTMLButtonElement, ButtonProps>(function Button(
  { variant = 'primary', icon, loading = false, block = false, className, children, disabled, type = 'button', ...rest },
  ref,
) {
  return (
    <button
      ref={ref}
      type={type}
      className={cx('btn', `btn--${variant}`, block && 'btn--block', className)}
      disabled={disabled || loading}
      aria-busy={loading || undefined}
      {...rest}
    >
      {loading ? <span className="spinner" aria-hidden="true" /> : icon ? <Icon name={icon} /> : null}
      <span>{children}</span>
    </button>
  );
});

export interface IconButtonProps extends Omit<ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement>, 'children'> {
  icon: IconName;
  label: string;
  variant?: 'default' | 'ghost';
}

export const IconButton = forwardRef<HTMLButtonElement, IconButtonProps>(function IconButton(
  { icon, label, variant = 'default', className, type = 'button', ...rest },
  ref,
) {
  return (
    <button
      ref={ref}
      type={type}
      className={cx('iconbtn', variant === 'ghost' && 'iconbtn--ghost', className)}
      aria-label={label}
      title={label}
      {...rest}
    >
      <Icon name={icon} />
    </button>
  );
});
```

`frontend/src/ui/Field.tsx`:
```tsx
import { forwardRef, useId, useState, type InputHTMLAttributes, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import { ApiError } from '../api/client';
import { t } from '../i18n';
import { cx } from './cx';
import { Icon } from './icons';
import './controls.css';

export interface FieldIds {
  id: string;
  describedBy: string | undefined;
  invalid: boolean;
}

export interface FieldProps {
  label: string;
  hint?: string | undefined;
  error?: string | null | undefined;
  required?: boolean | undefined;
  className?: string | undefined;
  hideLabel?: boolean | undefined;
  children(ids: FieldIds): ReactNode;
}

export function Field({ label, hint, error, required, className, hideLabel, children }: FieldProps) {
  const id = useId();
  const hintId = `${id}-hint`;
  const errorId = `${id}-error`;
  const describedBy = [hint ? hintId : null, error ? errorId : null].filter(Boolean).join(' ') || undefined;
  return (
    <div className={cx('field', className)}>
      <label className={cx('field__label', hideLabel && 'visually-hidden')} htmlFor={id}>
        {label}
        {required ? (
          <span className="field__req" aria-hidden="true">
            {' *'}
          </span>
        ) : null}
      </label>
      {children({ id, describedBy, invalid: Boolean(error) })}
      {hint ? (
        <p id={hintId} className="field__hint">
          {hint}
        </p>
      ) : null}
      {error ? (
        <p id={errorId} className="field__error">
          {error}
        </p>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}

export interface TextInputProps extends Omit<InputHTMLAttributes<HTMLInputElement>, 'id'> {
  label: string;
  hint?: string;
  error?: string | null;
  hideLabel?: boolean;
}

export const TextInput = forwardRef<HTMLInputElement, TextInputProps>(function TextInput(
  { label, hint, error, hideLabel, className, required, ...rest },
  ref,
) {
  return (
    <Field label={label} hint={hint} error={error} required={required} className={className} hideLabel={hideLabel}>
      {({ id, describedBy, invalid }) => (
        <input
          ref={ref}
          id={id}
          className="input"
          aria-describedby={describedBy}
          aria-invalid={invalid || undefined}
          required={required}
          {...rest}
        />
      )}
    </Field>
  );
});

export const PasswordInput = forwardRef<HTMLInputElement, Omit<TextInputProps, 'type'>>(function PasswordInput(
  { label, hint, error, hideLabel, className, required, ...rest },
  ref,
) {
  const [visible, setVisible] = useState(false);
  return (
    <Field label={label} hint={hint} error={error} required={required} className={className} hideLabel={hideLabel}>
      {({ id, describedBy, invalid }) => (
        <div className="input-wrap">
          <input
            ref={ref}
            id={id}
            type={visible ? 'text' : 'password'}
            className="input"
            aria-describedby={describedBy}
            aria-invalid={invalid || undefined}
            required={required}
            spellCheck={false}
            {...rest}
          />
          <button
            type="button"
            className="iconbtn iconbtn--ghost"
            aria-label={t('field.showPassword')}
            aria-pressed={visible}
            onClick={() => setVisible((v) => !v)}
          >
            <Icon name={visible ? 'eyeOff' : 'eye'} />
          </button>
        </div>
      )}
    </Field>
  );
});

export interface CheckboxProps {
  label: ReactNode;
  checked: boolean;
  onChange(checked: boolean): void;
  hint?: string;
  disabled?: boolean;
  name?: string;
  className?: string;
}

export function Checkbox({ label, checked, onChange, hint, disabled, name, className }: CheckboxProps) {
  const id = useId();
  return (
    <div className={cx('check-field', className)}>
      <label className="check" htmlFor={id}>
        <input
          id={id}
          type="checkbox"
          name={name}
          checked={checked}
          disabled={disabled}
          aria-describedby={hint ? `${id}-hint` : undefined}
          onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.checked)}
        />
        <span>{label}</span>
      </label>
      {hint ? (
        <p id={`${id}-hint`} className="field__hint">
          {hint}
        </p>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}

export function FormError({ error }: { error: unknown }) {
  if (!error) return null;
  const message = error instanceof ApiError ? error.message : t('error.generic');
  return (
    <p className="form-error" role="alert">
      <Icon name="alert" />
      <span>{message}</span>
    </p>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/ui/OtpInput.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useEffect, useRef, type ChangeEvent } from 'react';
import { t } from '../i18n';
import { cx } from './cx';
import { Field } from './Field';

export interface OtpInputProps {
  label: string;
  value: string;
  onChange(value: string): void;
  /** 'totp' rejimida 6-raqam kiritilganda bir marta chaqiriladi (avto-yuborish). */
  onComplete?(value: string): void;
  mode?: 'totp' | 'backup';
  error?: string | null;
  hint?: string;
  disabled?: boolean;
  focusOnMount?: boolean;
  testId?: string;
}

function sanitize(raw: string, mode: 'totp' | 'backup'): string {
  if (mode === 'totp') return raw.replace(/\D/g, '').slice(0, 6);
  return raw.toUpperCase().replace(/[^A-Z0-9-]/g, '').slice(0, 14);
}

/** Bitta maydon (6 katak emas): paste, parol menejeri va ekran o'quvchi uchun qulay (ledger). */
export function OtpInput({
  label,
  value,
  onChange,
  onComplete,
  mode = 'totp',
  error,
  hint,
  disabled,
  focusOnMount = false,
  testId,
}: OtpInputProps) {
  const ref = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  const completed = useRef<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (focusOnMount) ref.current?.focus();
  }, [focusOnMount]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (value.length < 6) completed.current = null;
  }, [value]);

  const handleChange = (event: ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const next = sanitize(event.target.value, mode);
    onChange(next);
    if (mode === 'totp' && next.length === 6 && completed.current !== next) {
      completed.current = next;
      onComplete?.(next);
    }
  };

  return (
    <Field label={label} hint={hint ?? (mode === 'backup' ? t('otp.backupHint') : undefined)} error={error}>
      {({ id, describedBy, invalid }) => (
        <input
          ref={ref}
          id={id}
          className={cx('input', 'otp', mode === 'backup' && 'otp--backup')}
          inputMode={mode === 'totp' ? 'numeric' : 'text'}
          autoComplete="one-time-code"
          autoCapitalize="characters"
          spellCheck={false}
          maxLength={mode === 'totp' ? 6 : 14}
          value={value}
          disabled={disabled}
          aria-describedby={describedBy}
          aria-invalid={invalid || undefined}
          data-testid={testId}
          onChange={handleChange}
        />
      )}
    </Field>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { cx } from './cx';
import { Field } from './Field';
import { Icon, type IconName } from './icons';
import './controls.css';

export interface SelectOption {
  value: string;
  label: string;
}

export interface SelectProps {
  label: string;
  value: string;
  options: readonly SelectOption[];
  onChange(value: string): void;
  hint?: string;
  error?: string | null;
  disabled?: boolean;
  icon?: IconName;
  hideLabel?: boolean;
  className?: string;
  required?: boolean;
  name?: string;
}

export function Select({
  label,
  value,
  options,
  onChange,
  hint,
  error,
  disabled,
  icon,
  hideLabel,
  className,
  required,
  name,
}: SelectProps) {
  return (
    <Field label={label} hint={hint} error={error} required={required} className={className} hideLabel={hideLabel}>
      {({ id, describedBy, invalid }) => (
        <div className={cx('select-wrap', icon && 'select-wrap--icon')}>
          {icon ? <Icon name={icon} /> : null}
          <select
            id={id}
            className="select"
            value={value}
            name={name}
            disabled={disabled}
            required={required}
            aria-describedby={describedBy}
            aria-invalid={invalid || undefined}
            onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value)}
          >
            {options.map((option) => (
              <option key={option.value} value={option.value}>
                {option.label}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </div>
      )}
    </Field>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/ui/Combobox.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useEffect, useId, useMemo, useState, type KeyboardEvent } from 'react';
import { t } from '../i18n';
import { cx } from './cx';
import { Field } from './Field';
import './controls.css';

export interface ComboOption {
  value: string;
  label: string;
  hint?: string;
}

export interface ComboboxProps {
  label: string;
  options: readonly ComboOption[];
  value: string | null;
  onChange(value: string | null): void;
  placeholder?: string;
  hint?: string;
  error?: string | null;
  required?: boolean;
  disabled?: boolean;
  emptyText?: string;
}

/** WAI-ARIA 1.2 combobox (list autocomplete): yozish filtrlaydi, ↑↓ Enter tanlaydi, Esc qaytaradi. */
export function Combobox({
  label,
  options,
  value,
  onChange,
  placeholder,
  hint,
  error,
  required,
  disabled,
  emptyText,
}: ComboboxProps) {
  const listId = useId();
  const selectedLabel = options.find((o) => o.value === value)?.label ?? '';
  const [query, setQuery] = useState(selectedLabel);
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const [active, setActive] = useState(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) setQuery(selectedLabel);
  }, [open, selectedLabel]);

  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    const q = query.trim().toLocaleLowerCase('ru');
    if (!q || q === selectedLabel.toLocaleLowerCase('ru')) return options;
    return options.filter((o) => o.label.toLocaleLowerCase('ru').includes(q));
  }, [options, query, selectedLabel]);

  const choose = (option: ComboOption) => {
    onChange(option.value);
    setQuery(option.label);
    setOpen(false);
  };

  const onKeyDown = (event: KeyboardEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    if (event.key === 'ArrowDown') {
      event.preventDefault();
      if (!open) {
        setOpen(true);
        setActive(0);
      } else setActive((i) => Math.min(i + 1, filtered.length - 1));
    } else if (event.key === 'ArrowUp') {
      event.preventDefault();
      setActive((i) => Math.max(i - 1, 0));
    } else if (event.key === 'Enter' && open) {
      const option = filtered[active];
      if (option) {
        event.preventDefault();
        choose(option);
      }
    } else if (event.key === 'Escape' && open) {
      event.preventDefault();
      event.stopPropagation();
      setOpen(false);
      setQuery(selectedLabel);
    }
  };

  const activeOption = open ? filtered[active] : undefined;

  return (
    <Field label={label} hint={hint} error={error} required={required}>
      {({ id, describedBy, invalid }) => (
        <div className="combo">
          <input
            id={id}
            className="input"
            role="combobox"
            aria-expanded={open}
            aria-controls={listId}
            aria-autocomplete="list"
            aria-activedescendant={activeOption ? `${listId}-${active}` : undefined}
            aria-describedby={describedBy}
            aria-invalid={invalid || undefined}
            autoComplete="off"
            placeholder={placeholder ?? t('combo.placeholder')}
            required={required}
            disabled={disabled}
            value={query}
            onChange={(e) => {
              setQuery(e.target.value);
              setOpen(true);
              setActive(0);
            }}
            onFocus={() => setOpen(true)}
            onBlur={() => setOpen(false)}
            onKeyDown={onKeyDown}
          />
          <ul id={listId} role="listbox" aria-label={label} className="combo__list" hidden={!open}>
            {filtered.length === 0 ? (
              <li className="combo__empty" role="presentation">
                {emptyText ?? t('combo.empty')}
              </li>
            ) : (
              filtered.map((option, index) => (
                <li
                  key={option.value}
                  id={`${listId}-${index}`}
                  role="option"
                  tabIndex={-1}
                  aria-selected={option.value === value}
                  className={cx('combo__opt', index === active && 'is-active')}
                  onMouseDown={(e) => {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    choose(option);
                  }}
                >
                  <span>{option.label}</span>
                  {option.hint ? <small>{option.hint}</small> : null}
                </li>
              ))
            )}
          </ul>
        </div>
      )}
    </Field>
  );
}
```
