```css
.btn {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  gap: 0.5rem;
  min-height: 2.5rem;
  padding: 0.5rem 1rem;
  border: 1px solid transparent;
  border-radius: var(--radius-ctl);
  font-weight: 600;
  line-height: 1.2;
  cursor: pointer;
  white-space: nowrap;
  text-decoration: none;
  transition: filter 0.15s, border-color 0.15s, color 0.15s, background-color 0.15s;
}
.btn .icon { width: 1.1rem; height: 1.1rem; flex: none; }
.btn--primary {
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--brand2), var(--brand));
  color: #fff;
  box-shadow: 0 10px 22px -14px rgba(29, 109, 242, 0.9);
}
.btn--primary:hover:not(:disabled), .btn--danger:hover:not(:disabled) { filter: brightness(1.08); }
.btn--secondary { background: var(--card); border-color: var(--line); color: var(--ink); }
.btn--secondary:hover:not(:disabled) { border-color: var(--brand2); color: var(--brand2); }
.btn--ghost { background: transparent; color: var(--brand2); }
.btn--ghost:hover:not(:disabled) { background: var(--soft); }
.btn--danger { background: var(--neg); color: #fff; }
.btn:disabled { opacity: 0.55; cursor: not-allowed; box-shadow: none; }
.btn--block { width: 100%; }

.spinner {
  display: inline-block;
  width: 1rem;
  height: 1rem;
  border-radius: 50%;
  border: 2px solid currentColor;
  border-right-color: transparent;
  animation: ugk-spin 0.7s linear infinite;
}
@keyframes ugk-spin { to { transform: rotate(360deg); } }

.iconbtn {
  display: inline-grid;
  place-items: center;
  flex: none;
  width: 2.5rem;
  height: 2.5rem;
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius: var(--radius-ctl);
  background: var(--card);
  color: var(--muted);
  cursor: pointer;
}
.iconbtn .icon { width: 1.2rem; height: 1.2rem; }
.iconbtn:hover:not(:disabled) { color: var(--brand2); border-color: var(--brand2); }
.iconbtn--ghost { border-color: transparent; background: transparent; }
.iconbtn:disabled { opacity: 0.45; cursor: not-allowed; }

.field { display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 0.3rem; min-width: 0; }
.field__label { font-size: 0.9rem; font-weight: 500; color: var(--muted); }
.field__req { color: var(--neg); }
.field__hint { margin: 0; font-size: 0.85rem; color: var(--muted); }
.field__error { margin: 0; font-size: 0.88rem; font-weight: 500; color: var(--neg); }

.input, .select {
  width: 100%;
  min-height: 2.6rem;
  padding: 0.5rem 0.75rem;
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius: 0.45rem;
  background: var(--card);
  color: var(--ink);
}
.input:hover:not(:disabled), .select:hover:not(:disabled) { border-color: #c9d8ea; }
.input[aria-invalid='true'], .select[aria-invalid='true'] { border-color: var(--neg); }
.input:disabled, .select:disabled { background: var(--soft); color: var(--muted); cursor: not-allowed; }
.input::placeholder { color: var(--muted); opacity: 1; }
.select {
  appearance: none;
  padding-right: 2rem;
  cursor: pointer;
  background-image: linear-gradient(45deg, transparent 50%, var(--muted) 50%),
    linear-gradient(135deg, var(--muted) 50%, transparent 50%);
  background-position: calc(100% - 0.95rem) 52%, calc(100% - 0.65rem) 52%;
  background-size: 0.3rem 0.3rem;
  background-repeat: no-repeat;
}
.select-wrap { position: relative; display: flex; align-items: center; }
.select-wrap > .icon {
  position: absolute;
  left: 0.7rem;
  width: 1rem;
  height: 1rem;
  color: var(--muted);
  pointer-events: none;
}
.select-wrap--icon .select { padding-left: 2.1rem; }

.input-wrap { position: relative; display: flex; }
.input-wrap .input { padding-right: 2.9rem; }
.input-wrap .iconbtn {
  position: absolute;
  top: 50%;
  right: 0.2rem;
  width: 2.2rem;
  height: 2.2rem;
  transform: translateY(-50%);
}

.otp {
  min-height: 3.2rem;
  padding-left: 1.25rem;
  font-size: 1.6rem;
  font-weight: 600;
  letter-spacing: 0.5em;
  text-align: center;
}
.otp--backup {
  font-family: ui-monospace, 'SFMono-Regular', Consolas, monospace;
  font-size: 1.25rem;
  letter-spacing: 0.2em;
}

.combo { position: relative; }
.combo__list {
  position: absolute;
  z-index: 45;
  top: calc(100% + 0.25rem);
  left: 0;
  right: 0;
  max-height: 16rem;
  margin: 0;
  padding: 0.3rem;
  overflow: auto;
  list-style: none;
  background: var(--card);
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius: 0.6rem;
  box-shadow: var(--shadow-hover);
}
.combo__list[hidden] { display: none; }
.combo__opt {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: space-between;
  gap: 1rem;
  padding: 0.55rem 0.7rem;
  border-radius: 0.45rem;
  cursor: pointer;
}
.combo__opt.is-active { background: var(--soft); color: var(--brand); }
.combo__opt[aria-selected='true'] { font-weight: 600; }
.combo__opt small { color: var(--muted); }
.combo__empty { padding: 0.6rem 0.7rem; color: var(--muted); }

.check { display: flex; align-items: flex-start; gap: 0.55rem; line-height: 1.35; cursor: pointer; }
.check input { flex: none; width: 1.15rem; height: 1.15rem; margin: 0.1rem 0 0; accent-color: var(--brand2); }

.form-error {

  gap: 0.6rem;
  margin: 0;
  padding: 0.7rem 0.85rem;
  border-radius: 0.55rem;

  color: var(--neg);
  font-weight: 500;
}
.form-error .icon { flex: none; width: 1.2rem; height: 1.2rem; margin-top: 0.1rem; }

@media (max-width: 767.98px), (pointer: coarse) {
  .btn, .input, .select { min-height: 44px; }
  .iconbtn { width: 44px; height: 44px; }
  .check input { width: 1.4rem; height: 1.4rem; }
}
```

**Files:**
- Create: `frontend/src/ui/Card.tsx`, `frontend/src/ui/Chip.tsx`, `frontend/src/ui/Badge.tsx`, `frontend/src/ui/KpiTile.tsx`, `frontend/src/ui/Tabs.tsx`, `frontend/src/ui/Tooltip.tsx`, `frontend/src/ui/Skeleton.tsx`, `frontend/src/ui/EmptyState.tsx`, `frontend/src/ui/ErrorBanner.tsx`, `frontend/src/ui/display.css`
- Modify: `frontend/src/i18n/ru.ts`
- Test: `frontend/src/ui/display.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Icon`, `IconName`, `cx`, `Button`, `IconButton` (Task 5), `ApiError` (Task 4), `formatDateTime` (Task 2).
- Produces:
  - `Card({ title?; sub?; actions?: ReactNode; exportSlot?: ReactNode; className?; children })` — `<section class="card">`, sarlavha bo'lsa `aria-labelledby`.
  - `Chip({ label; value; onRemove?(): void })`.
  - `Badge({ tone?: BadgeTone; children })`, `type BadgeTone = 'neutral' | 'blue' | 'ok' | 'warn' | 'neg'`.
  - `KpiTile({ icon: IconName; label; value: ReactNode; unit?; delta?: ReactNode; deltaDown?; negative?; hint? })`, `KpiGrid({ children })`.
  - `Tabs({ label; tabs: readonly TabItem[]; value; onChange(id); children })`, `TabItem { id: string; label: string }` — faqat aktiv panel render qilinadi.
  - `Tooltip({ content: string; children(trigger: TooltipTriggerProps): ReactNode })`.
  - `Skeleton({ variant?: 'line' | 'title' | 'kpi' | 'card' | 'row'; className? })`, `PageSkeleton()` (`role="status"`, "Загрузка…").
  - `EmptyState({ icon?: IconName; title; text?; action?: ReactNode })`.
  - `ErrorBanner({ error: unknown; onRetry?(): void; lastSuccessAt?: number | null; title?; retrying? })` — `role="alert"`, oxirgi muvaffaqiyatli yuklash vaqti, so'rov kodi, «Повторить».

```tsx
import { render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { ApiError } from '../api/client';
import { expectNoAxeViolations } from '../test/axe';
import { Badge } from './Badge';
import { Card } from './Card';
import { Chip } from './Chip';
import { EmptyState } from './EmptyState';
import { ErrorBanner } from './ErrorBanner';
import { KpiGrid, KpiTile } from './KpiTile';
import { PageSkeleton } from './Skeleton';
import { Tabs } from './Tabs';
import { Tooltip } from './Tooltip';

describe('Card', () => {
  it('is a region named by its title', () => {
    render(
      <Card title="Остатки по банкам" sub="На 24.09.2026">
        <p>тело</p>
      </Card>,
    );
    expect(screen.getByRole('region', { name: 'Остатки по банкам' })).toHaveTextContent('На 24.09.2026');
  });
});

describe('Chip', () => {
  it('removes itself with a labelled button', async () => {
    const onRemove = vi.fn();
    render(<Chip label="Филиал" value="Чарвакская ГЭС" onRemove={onRemove} />);
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Сбросить: Филиал' }));
    expect(onRemove).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
  });

  it('has no remove button when locked', () => {
    render(<Chip label="Филиал" value="Чирчик" />);
    expect(screen.queryByRole('button')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});

describe('KpiTile', () => {
  it('shows value, unit, delta and a formula tooltip', async () => {
    render(
      <KpiGrid>
        <KpiTile icon="coins" label="Поступления" value="1 234,5" unit="млрд" delta="−3,2% к 2025" deltaDown hint="Все зачисления на счета" />
      </KpiGrid>,
    );
    expect(screen.getByText('1 234,5')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('млрд')).toBeInTheDocument();
    const trigger = screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Как считается: Поступления' });
    expect(trigger).toHaveAccessibleDescription('Все зачисления на счета');
  });
});

describe('Tooltip', () => {
  it('is dismissed with Escape and comes back after blur', async () => {
    render(<Tooltip content="Подсказка">{(p) => <button type="button" {...p}>?</button>}</Tooltip>);
    const trigger = screen.getByRole('button', { name: '?' });
    await userEvent.tab();
    expect(trigger).toHaveFocus();
    await userEvent.keyboard('{Escape}');
    expect(trigger.parentElement).toHaveClass('is-dismissed');
    await userEvent.tab();
    expect(trigger.parentElement).not.toHaveClass('is-dismissed');
  });
});

function TabsHarness() {
  const [value, setValue] = useState('pos');
  return (
    <Tabs
      label="Справочники"
      tabs={[
        { id: 'pos', label: 'Должности' },
        { id: 'roles', label: 'Роли' },
      ]}
      value={value}
      onChange={setValue}
    >
      <p>панель {value}</p>
    </Tabs>
  );
}

describe('Tabs', () => {
  it('moves selection with arrow keys (roving tabindex)', async () => {
    render(<TabsHarness />);
    const first = screen.getByRole('tab', { name: 'Должности' });
    expect(first).toHaveAttribute('aria-selected', 'true');
    expect(screen.getByRole('tabpanel')).toHaveTextContent('панель pos');
    first.focus();
    await userEvent.keyboard('{ArrowRight}');
    const second = screen.getByRole('tab', { name: 'Роли' });
    expect(second).toHaveAttribute('aria-selected', 'true');
    expect(second).toHaveFocus();
    expect(first).toHaveAttribute('tabindex', '-1');
    expect(screen.getByRole('tabpanel', { name: 'Роли' })).toHaveTextContent('панель roles');
  });
});

describe('ErrorBanner', () => {
  it('shows message, last success time, request id and retries', async () => {
    const onRetry = vi.fn();
    render(
      <ErrorBanner
        error={new ApiError(503, 'unavailable', 'Сервис недоступен', 'abc123')}
        lastSuccessAt={Date.parse('2026-09-24T05:07:00Z')}
        onRetry={onRetry}
      />,
    );
    const alert = screen.getByRole('alert');
    expect(alert).toHaveTextContent('Сервис недоступен');
    expect(alert).toHaveTextContent('Последняя успешная загрузка: 24.09.2026 10:07');
    expect(alert).toHaveTextContent('Код запроса: abc123');
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Повторить' }));
    expect(onRetry).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
  });

  it('uses the network text for non-API errors and hides empty metadata', () => {
    render(<ErrorBanner error={new ApiError(0, 'network', 'Нет связи с сервером.')} lastSuccessAt={0} />);
    expect(screen.getByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent('Нет связи с сервером.');
    expect(screen.queryByText(/Последняя успешная/)).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByRole('button')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});

it('announces loading skeletons politely', () => {
  render(<PageSkeleton />);
  expect(screen.getByRole('status')).toHaveTextContent('Загрузка…');
});

it('has no axe violations', async () => {
  const { container } = render(
    <main>
      <Card title="Карточка" actions={<Badge tone="ok">Активен</Badge>}>
        <EmptyState icon="clock" title="Раздел в разработке" text="Страница появится позже." />
      </Card>
      <Badge tone="neg">Заблокирован</Badge>
      <Chip label="Период" value="I квартал 2026" onRemove={vi.fn()} />
      <TabsHarness />
      <ErrorBanner error={new Error('x')} onRetry={vi.fn()} />
    </main>,
  );
  await expectNoAxeViolations(container);
});
```

```tsx
import { useId, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import { cx } from './cx';
import './display.css';

export interface CardProps {
  title?: string;
  sub?: string;
  /** Sarlavha qatorining o'ng tomoni (filtr, tugma). */
  actions?: ReactNode;
  /** TZ 6: kartada hover'da chiqadigan eksport tugmasi joyi (PNG/CSV — keyingi rejalar). */
  exportSlot?: ReactNode;
  className?: string;
  children: ReactNode;
}

export function Card({ title, sub, actions, exportSlot, className, children }: CardProps) {
  const titleId = useId();
  return (
    <section className={cx('card', className)} aria-labelledby={title ? titleId : undefined}>
      {title || actions ? (
        <div className="card__head">
          <div>
            {title ? <h2 id={titleId}>{title}</h2> : null}
            {sub ? <p className="card__sub">{sub}</p> : null}
          </div>
          {actions ? <div className="card__actions">{actions}</div> : null}
        </div>
      ) : null}
      {children}
      {exportSlot ? <div className="card__exp">{exportSlot}</div> : null}
    </section>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/ui/Tooltip.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useId, useState, type KeyboardEvent, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import { cx } from './cx';
import './display.css';

export interface TooltipTriggerProps {
  'aria-describedby': string;
  onKeyDown(event: KeyboardEvent<HTMLElement>): void;
  onBlur(): void;
  onMouseLeave(): void;
}

/** Hover/fokusda ko'rinadi (CSS), Esc bilan yopiladi (WCAG 1.4.13). */
export function Tooltip({ content, children }: { content: string; children(trigger: TooltipTriggerProps): ReactNode }) {
  const id = useId();
  const [dismissed, setDismissed] = useState(false);
  const trigger: TooltipTriggerProps = {
    'aria-describedby': id,
    onKeyDown: (event) => {
      if (event.key === 'Escape') setDismissed(true);
    },
    onBlur: () => setDismissed(false),
    onMouseLeave: () => setDismissed(false),
  };
  return (
    <span className={cx('tip', dismissed && 'is-dismissed')}>
      {children(trigger)}
      <span role="tooltip" id={id} className="tip__bubble">
        {content}
      </span>
    </span>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
import { t } from '../i18n';
import { cx } from './cx';
import { Icon, type IconName } from './icons';
import { Tooltip } from './Tooltip';
import './display.css';

export interface KpiTileProps {
  icon: IconName;
  label: string;
  value: ReactNode;
  unit?: string;
  delta?: ReactNode;
  deltaDown?: boolean;
  negative?: boolean;
  /** TZ 6: har ko'rsatkichda formula bilan qalqib chiquvchi izoh. */
  hint?: string;
}

export function KpiTile({ icon, label, value, unit, delta, deltaDown, negative, hint }: KpiTileProps) {
  return (
    <div className="kpi">
      <div className="kpi__top">
        <span className="kpi__ic">
          <Icon name={icon} />
        </span>
        <span className="kpi__label">{label}</span>
        {hint ? (
          <Tooltip content={hint}>
            {(trigger) => (
              <button type="button" className="kpi__hint" aria-label={t('kpi.hint', { label })} {...trigger}>
                <Icon name="info" />
              </button>
            )}
          </Tooltip>
        ) : null}
      </div>
      <div className={cx('kpi__value', negative && 'kpi__value--neg')}>
        {value}
        {unit ? <small>{unit}</small> : null}
      </div>
      {delta ? <div className={cx('kpi__delta', deltaDown && 'kpi__delta--down')}>{delta}</div> : null}
    </div>
  );
}

export function KpiGrid({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  return <section className="kpis">{children}</section>;
}
```

`frontend/src/ui/Tabs.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useId, useRef, type KeyboardEvent, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import './display.css';

export interface TabItem {
  id: string;
  label: string;
}

export interface TabsProps {
  label: string;
  tabs: readonly TabItem[];
  value: string;
  onChange(id: string): void;
  children: ReactNode;
}

/** WAI-ARIA tabs: roving tabindex, ←/→/Home/End, avtomatik faollashtirish. */
export function Tabs({ label, tabs, value, onChange, children }: TabsProps) {
  const base = useId();
  const refs = useRef<Array<HTMLButtonElement | null>>([]);
  const index = Math.max(
    0,
    tabs.findIndex((tab) => tab.id === value),
  );

  const move = (next: number) => {
    const target = tabs[(next + tabs.length) % tabs.length];
    if (!target) return;
    onChange(target.id);
    refs.current[(next + tabs.length) % tabs.length]?.focus();
  };

  const onKeyDown = (event: KeyboardEvent<HTMLButtonElement>) => {
    if (event.key === 'ArrowRight') move(index + 1);
    else if (event.key === 'ArrowLeft') move(index - 1);
    else if (event.key === 'Home') move(0);
    else if (event.key === 'End') move(tabs.length - 1);
    else return;
    event.preventDefault();
  };

  return (
    <div className="tabs">
      <div role="tablist" aria-label={label} className="tabs__list">
        {tabs.map((tab, i) => (
          <button
            key={tab.id}
            ref={(el) => {
              refs.current[i] = el;
            }}
            type="button"
            role="tab"
            id={`${base}-tab-${tab.id}`}
            aria-selected={tab.id === value}
            aria-controls={`${base}-panel`}
            tabIndex={tab.id === value ? 0 : -1}
            className="tabs__tab"
            onClick={() => onChange(tab.id)}
            onKeyDown={onKeyDown}
          >
            {tab.label}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>
      <div
        role="tabpanel"
        id={`${base}-panel`}
        aria-labelledby={`${base}-tab-${tabs[index]?.id ?? ''}`}
        className="tabs__panel"
        tabIndex={0}
      >
        {children}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { t } from '../i18n';
import { cx } from './cx';
import './display.css';

export type SkeletonVariant = 'line' | 'title' | 'kpi' | 'card' | 'row';

export function Skeleton({ variant = 'line', className }: { variant?: SkeletonVariant; className?: string }) {
  return <span className={cx('skel', `skel--${variant}`, className)} aria-hidden="true" />;
}

/** Sahifa kontenti yuklanmoqda: kartalar va jadval shaklida, maket siljimaydi (TZ 6 «Загрузка»). */
export function PageSkeleton() {
  return (
    <div className="page-skel" role="status" aria-live="polite">
      <span className="visually-hidden">{t('common.loading')}</span>
      <div className="page-skel__kpis">
        <Skeleton variant="kpi" />
        <Skeleton variant="kpi" />
        <Skeleton variant="kpi" />
      </div>
      <Skeleton variant="card" />
      <Skeleton variant="row" />
      <Skeleton variant="row" />
      <Skeleton variant="row" />
    </div>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/ui/ErrorBanner.tsx`:
```tsx
import { ApiError } from '../api/client';
import { t } from '../i18n';
import { formatDateTime } from '../i18n/format';
import { Button } from './Button';
import { Icon } from './icons';
import './display.css';

export interface ErrorBannerProps {
  error: unknown;
  onRetry?(): void;
  /** TanStack `dataUpdatedAt` (ms): 0 — hali muvaffaqiyatli yuklanmagan. */
  lastSuccessAt?: number | null;
  title?: string;
  retrying?: boolean;
}

/** TZ 6 «Ошибка загрузки витрины»: plashka + oxirgi muvaffaqiyatli yuklash vaqti + «Повторить». */
export function ErrorBanner({ error, onRetry, lastSuccessAt, title, retrying }: ErrorBannerProps) {
  const message = error instanceof ApiError ? error.message : t('error.generic');
  const requestId = error instanceof ApiError ? error.requestId : null;
  return (
    <div className="error-banner" role="alert">
      <Icon name="alert" className="error-banner__ic" />
      <div className="error-banner__body">
        <p className="error-banner__title">{title ?? t('error.loadFailed')}</p>
        <p className="error-banner__text">{message}</p>
        {lastSuccessAt ? (
          <p className="error-banner__meta">{t('error.lastSuccess', { time: formatDateTime(lastSuccessAt) })}</p>
        ) : null}
        {requestId ? <p className="error-banner__meta">{t('error.requestId', { id: requestId })}</p> : null}
      </div>
      {onRetry ? (
        <Button variant="secondary" icon="refresh" loading={retrying} onClick={onRetry}>
          {t('common.retry')}
        </Button>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}
```

```css
.card {
  position: relative;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  min-width: 0;
  padding: 1.2rem 1.35rem;
  background: var(--card);
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius: var(--radius-card);
  box-shadow: var(--shadow);
  transition: box-shadow 0.18s, border-color 0.18s;
}
.card:hover { box-shadow: var(--shadow-hover); border-color: #cfe0f5; }
.card__head { display: flex; align-items: flex-start; justify-content: space-between; gap: 1rem; margin-bottom: 0.6rem; }
.card h2 { margin: 0; font-size: 1.2rem; font-weight: 600; }
.card__sub { margin: 0.2rem 0 0; font-size: 0.92rem; color: var(--muted); }
.card__actions { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; align-items: center; gap: 0.5rem; }
.card__exp { position: absolute; top: 0.85rem; right: 0.9rem; z-index: 2; opacity: 0; transition: opacity 0.15s; }
.card:hover .card__exp, .card:focus-within .card__exp { opacity: 1; }
@media (hover: none) { .card__exp { opacity: 1; } }

.chips { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; align-items: center; gap: 0.4rem; }
.chip {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 0.35rem;
  padding: 0.2rem 0.3rem 0.2rem 0.8rem;
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius: 999px;
  background: var(--soft);
  font-size: 0.9rem;
}
.chip--static { padding-right: 0.8rem; }
.chip b { font-weight: 600; }
.chip button {
  display: inline-grid;
  place-items: center;
  width: 1.75rem;
  height: 1.75rem;
  border: 0;
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: none;
  color: var(--muted);
  font-size: 1.1rem;
  line-height: 1;
  cursor: pointer;
}
.chip button:hover { background: var(--card); color: var(--neg); }

.badge {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 0.3rem;
  padding: 0.1rem 0.45rem;
  border-radius: 0.3rem;
  font-size: 0.85rem;
  font-weight: 600;
  white-space: nowrap;
}
.badge--neutral { background: var(--soft); color: var(--muted); }
.badge--blue { background: var(--soft); color: var(--brand); }
.badge--ok { background: var(--ok-bg); color: var(--ok); }
.badge--warn { background: var(--warn-bg); color: var(--warn); }
.badge--neg { background: var(--neg-bg); color: var(--neg); }

.kpis { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(3, minmax(0, 1fr)); gap: 1rem; }
@media (min-width: 1600px) { .kpis { grid-template-columns: repeat(6, minmax(0, 1fr)); } }
.kpi {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 0.25rem;
  padding: 1rem 1.15rem;
  background: linear-gradient(180deg, #ffffff, #fbfdff);
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius: var(--radius-card);
  box-shadow: var(--shadow);
}
.kpi__top { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.5rem; }
.kpi__ic {
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  flex: none;
  width: 1.9rem;
  height: 1.9rem;
  border-radius: 0.45rem;
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, #e7f1ff, #e2f8fb);
  color: var(--brand2);
}
.kpi__ic .icon { width: 1.15rem; height: 1.15rem; }
.kpi__label { flex: 1; font-size: 1rem; font-weight: 500; }
.kpi__hint { display: inline-grid; place-items: center; width: 1.75rem; height: 1.75rem; border: 0; border-radius: 50%; background: none; color: var(--muted); cursor: help; }
.kpi__hint .icon { width: 1rem; height: 1rem; }
.kpi__value {
  margin-top: 0.15rem;
  font-size: 2.1rem;
  font-weight: 700;
  line-height: 1.15;
  letter-spacing: -0.015em;
  white-space: nowrap;
  background: linear-gradient(90deg, var(--brand), #2f8ef5);
  -webkit-background-clip: text;
  background-clip: text;
  color: transparent;
}
.kpi__value small { margin-left: 0.3rem; font-size: 0.5em; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0; color: var(--muted); -webkit-text-fill-color: var(--muted); }
.kpi__value--neg { background: none; color: var(--neg); -webkit-text-fill-color: var(--neg); }
.kpi__delta { font-size: 0.88rem; color: var(--muted); }
.kpi__delta--down { color: var(--neg); }

.tabs__list { display: inline-flex; flex-wrap: wrap; overflow: hidden; background: var(--card); border: 1px solid var(--line); border-radius: 0.55rem; }
.tabs__tab { padding: 0.5rem 1rem; border: 0; background: none; color: var(--ink); font-weight: 500; cursor: pointer; }
.tabs__tab + .tabs__tab { border-left: 1px solid var(--line); }
.tabs__tab[aria-selected='true'] { background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--brand2), var(--brand)); color: #fff; }
.tabs__panel { margin-top: 1rem; }
.tabs__panel:focus-visible { outline-offset: 4px; }

.tip { position: relative; display: inline-flex; }
.tip__bubble {
  position: absolute;
  z-index: 50;
  bottom: calc(100% + 0.45rem);
  left: 50%;
  width: max-content;
  max-width: min(22rem, 80vw);
  padding: 0.5rem 0.7rem;
  border-radius: 0.45rem;
  background: var(--ink);
  color: #fff;
  font-size: 0.85rem;
  font-weight: 400;
  line-height: 1.4;
  box-shadow: var(--shadow-hover);
  transform: translateX(-50%);
  opacity: 0;
  visibility: hidden;
  transition: opacity 0.12s;
}
.tip__bubble::after { content: ''; position: absolute; top: 100%; left: 0; right: 0; height: 0.5rem; }
.tip:hover .tip__bubble, .tip:focus-within .tip__bubble { opacity: 1; visibility: visible; }
.tip.is-dismissed .tip__bubble { opacity: 0; visibility: hidden; }

.skel {
  display: block;
  border-radius: 0.45rem;
  background: linear-gradient(90deg, #e9f1fb 25%, #f4f8fd 50%, #e9f1fb 75%);
  background-size: 200% 100%;
  animation: ugk-shimmer 1.2s ease-in-out infinite;
}
@keyframes ugk-shimmer { from { background-position: 200% 0; } to { background-position: -200% 0; } }
.skel--line { width: 60%; height: 1rem; }
.skel--title { width: 18rem; max-width: 80%; height: 2rem; }
.skel--kpi { height: 7.2rem; border-radius: var(--radius-card); }
.skel--card { height: 18rem; border-radius: var(--radius-card); }
.skel--row { height: 2.6rem; }
.page-skel { display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 1rem; }
.page-skel__kpis { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(3, minmax(0, 1fr)); gap: 1rem; }

.empty-state { display: flex; flex-direction: column; align-items: center; gap: 0.6rem; padding: 3rem 1.5rem; text-align: center; color: var(--muted); }
.empty-state__ic { display: grid; place-items: center; width: 3.5rem; height: 3.5rem; border-radius: 1rem; background: linear-gradient(135deg, #e7f1ff, #dff6fa); color: var(--brand2); }
.empty-state__ic .icon { width: 1.8rem; height: 1.8rem; }
.empty-state h2 { margin: 0.4rem 0 0; font-size: 1.3rem; color: var(--ink); }
.empty-state p { max-width: 34rem; margin: 0; }
.empty-state__action { margin-top: 0.4rem; }

.error-banner {
  display: flex;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 0.6rem 1rem;
  padding: 0.9rem 1.1rem;
  border: 1px solid #f3c7c3;
  border-radius: 0.75rem;
  background: var(--neg-bg);
  color: var(--ink);
}
.error-banner__ic { flex: none; width: 1.4rem; height: 1.4rem; color: var(--neg); }
.error-banner__body { flex: 1; min-width: 14rem; }
.error-banner__body p { margin: 0; }
.error-banner__title { font-weight: 600; color: var(--neg); }
.error-banner__meta { margin-top: 0.15rem !important; font-size: 0.88rem; color: var(--muted); }

@media (max-width: 767.98px) {
  .kpis, .page-skel__kpis { grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr)); }
  .kpi__value { font-size: 1.55rem; }
  .kpi__ic { display: none; }
  .chip button, .kpi__hint { width: 44px; height: 44px; }
}
@media print {
  .kpi__value { background: none; color: var(--brand); -webkit-text-fill-color: var(--brand); }
  .card__exp { display: none; }
}
```

- Consumes: `Button`, `IconButton`, `cx` (Task 5).
- Produces:
  - `Overlay({ open; onClose(); variant: 'modal' | 'drawer' | 'sheet' | 'palette'; labelledBy?; label?; initialFocusRef?: RefObject<HTMLElement>; className?; children })` — `document.body` ga portal, `role="dialog" aria-modal="true"`, Tab tuzog'i, Esc va fon bosilganda yopish, `#root` ga `inert`, `body.no-scroll`, yopilganda fokus qaytadi.
  - `Modal({ open; onClose(); title; note?; footer?: ReactNode; size?: 'md' | 'sm'; initialFocusRef?; children })`.
  - `ConfirmDialog({ open; title; message: ReactNode; confirmLabel; onConfirm(); onCancel(); danger?; busy?; error?: unknown })`.
  - `Drawer({ open; onClose(); title; children })` (chapdan).
  - `BottomSheet({ open; onClose(); title; footer?: ReactNode; children })`.
  - `ToastProvider({ children })`, `useToast() -> { show(message: string, tone?: 'info' | 'success' | 'error'): void }`, `TOAST_MS = 6000`; toastlar `document.body` dagi `aria-live="polite"` hududida.

```tsx
import { act, render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { expectNoAxeViolations } from '../test/axe';
import { BottomSheet } from './BottomSheet';
import { Button } from './Button';
import { ConfirmDialog } from './ConfirmDialog';
import { Drawer } from './Drawer';
import { Modal } from './Modal';
import { TOAST_MS, ToastProvider, useToast } from './Toast';

function ModalHarness() {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  return (
    <div id="root">
      <Button onClick={() => setOpen(true)}>Открыть</Button>
      <Modal
        open={open}
        onClose={() => setOpen(false)}
        title="Новый пользователь"
        footer={<Button onClick={() => setOpen(false)}>Создать</Button>}
      >
        <label htmlFor="n">Логин</label>
        <input id="n" />
      </Modal>
    </div>
  );
}

describe('Modal', () => {
  it('traps focus, marks the app inert and restores focus on Escape', async () => {
    const { container } = render(<ModalHarness />);
    const root = container.querySelector('#root');
    const opener = screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Открыть' });
    await userEvent.click(opener);
    const dialog = screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Новый пользователь' });
    expect(dialog).toHaveAttribute('aria-modal', 'true');
    expect(root).toHaveAttribute('inert');
    expect(document.body).toHaveClass('no-scroll');
    const close = screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Закрыть' });
    expect(close).toHaveFocus();
    await userEvent.tab();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Логин')).toHaveFocus();
    await userEvent.tab();
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Создать' })).toHaveFocus();
    await userEvent.tab();
    expect(close).toHaveFocus();
    await userEvent.tab({ shift: true });
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Создать' })).toHaveFocus();
    await userEvent.keyboard('{Escape}');
    expect(screen.queryByRole('dialog')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(root).not.toHaveAttribute('inert');
    expect(document.body).not.toHaveClass('no-scroll');
    expect(opener).toHaveFocus();
  });

  it('closes on backdrop mousedown but not on panel clicks', async () => {
    const onClose = vi.fn();
    render(
      <Modal open onClose={onClose} title="Окно">
        <p>текст</p>
      </Modal>,
    );
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByText('текст'));
    expect(onClose).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    const backdrop = screen.getByRole('dialog').parentElement as HTMLElement;
    await userEvent.click(backdrop);
    expect(onClose).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
  });
});

describe('ConfirmDialog', () => {
  it('confirms and cancels', async () => {
    const onConfirm = vi.fn();
    const onCancel = vi.fn();
    render(
      <ConfirmDialog
        open
        title="Заблокировать пользователя?"
        message="Все его сеансы будут завершены."
        confirmLabel="Заблокировать"
        danger
        onConfirm={onConfirm}
        onCancel={onCancel}
      />,
    );
    expect(screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Заблокировать пользователя?' })).toHaveTextContent('Все его сеансы');
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Заблокировать' }));
    expect(onConfirm).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Отмена' }));
    expect(onCancel).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
  });
});

describe('Drawer and BottomSheet', () => {
  it('render labelled dialogs with a close button', async () => {
    const onClose = vi.fn();
    const { rerender } = render(
      <Drawer open onClose={onClose} title="Казначейство">
        <a href="#kazna/svod">Сводка</a>
      </Drawer>,
    );
    expect(screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Казначейство' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Закрыть' }));
    expect(onClose).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
    rerender(
      <BottomSheet open onClose={onClose} title="Фильтры" footer={<Button>Готово</Button>}>
        <p>фильтры</p>
      </BottomSheet>,
    );
    expect(screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Фильтры' })).toHaveTextContent('фильтры');
    await expectNoAxeViolations(screen.getByRole('dialog'));
  });
});

function ToastButton() {
  const toast = useToast();
  return <Button onClick={() => toast.show('Пароль изменён', 'success')}>Показать</Button>;
}

describe('Toast', () => {
  it('announces messages in a live region and auto-dismisses', async () => {
    vi.useFakeTimers();
    try {
      render(
        <ToastProvider>
          <ToastButton />
        </ToastProvider>,
      );
      act(() => screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Показать' }).click());
      const region = screen.getByRole('status', { name: 'Уведомления' });
      expect(region).toHaveTextContent('Пароль изменён');
      act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTime(TOAST_MS + 10));
      expect(region).not.toHaveTextContent('Пароль изменён');
    } finally {
      vi.useRealTimers();
    }
  });

  it('can be dismissed manually', async () => {
    render(
      <ToastProvider>
        <ToastButton />
      </ToastProvider>,
    );
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Показать' }));
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Закрыть' }));
    expect(screen.getByRole('status', { name: 'Уведомления' })).toBeEmptyDOMElement();
  });
});
```

`frontend/src/ui/Overlay.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useEffect, useRef, type KeyboardEvent, type MouseEvent, type ReactNode, type RefObject } from 'react';
import { createPortal } from 'react-dom';
import { cx } from './cx';
import './overlay.css';

const FOCUSABLE =
  'a[href],button:not([disabled]),input:not([disabled]):not([type="hidden"]),select:not([disabled]),textarea:not([disabled]),[tabindex]:not([tabindex="-1"])';

let openCount = 0;

function setAppInert(inert: boolean): void {
  const root = document.getElementById('root');
  if (!root) return;
  if (inert) root.setAttribute('inert', '');
  else root.removeAttribute('inert');
}

export type OverlayVariant = 'modal' | 'drawer' | 'sheet' | 'palette';

export interface OverlayProps {
  open: boolean;
  onClose(): void;
  variant: OverlayVariant;
  labelledBy?: string;
  label?: string;
  initialFocusRef?: RefObject<HTMLElement>;
  className?: string;
  children: ReactNode;
}

/** Barcha modal qatlamlar asosi: portal, aria-modal, fokus tuzog'i, Esc, fon, inert ilova, fokusni qaytarish. */
export function Overlay({ open, onClose, variant, labelledBy, label, initialFocusRef, className, children }: OverlayProps) {
  const panelRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return undefined;
    const previous = document.activeElement instanceof HTMLElement ? document.activeElement : null;
    openCount += 1;
    if (openCount === 1) {
      setAppInert(true);
      document.body.classList.add('no-scroll');
    }
    const panel = panelRef.current;
    const target = initialFocusRef?.current ?? panel?.querySelector<HTMLElement>(FOCUSABLE) ?? panel;
    target?.focus();
    return () => {
      openCount -= 1;
      if (openCount === 0) {
        setAppInert(false);
        document.body.classList.remove('no-scroll');
      }
      previous?.focus();
    };
  }, [open, initialFocusRef]);

  if (!open) return null;

  const onKeyDown = (event: KeyboardEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
    if (event.key === 'Escape') {
      event.stopPropagation();
      onClose();
      return;
    }
    if (event.key !== 'Tab') return;
    const items = Array.from(panelRef.current?.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(FOCUSABLE) ?? []);
    const first = items[0];
    const last = items[items.length - 1];
    if (!first || !last) {
      event.preventDefault();
      return;
    }
    if (event.shiftKey && document.activeElement === first) {
      event.preventDefault();
      last.focus();
    } else if (!event.shiftKey && document.activeElement === last) {
      event.preventDefault();
      first.focus();
    }
  };

  const onBackdrop = (event: MouseEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
    if (event.target === event.currentTarget) onClose();
  };

  return createPortal(
    // eslint-disable-next-line jsx-a11y/no-static-element-interactions -- fon: sichqoncha bilan yopish; klaviaturada Esc
    <div className={cx('overlay', `overlay--${variant}`)} onMouseDown={onBackdrop}>
      <div
        ref={panelRef}
        className={cx('overlay__panel', className)}
        role="dialog"
        aria-modal="true"
        aria-labelledby={labelledBy}
        aria-label={labelledBy ? undefined : label}
        tabIndex={-1}
        onKeyDown={onKeyDown}
      >
        {children}
      </div>
    </div>,
    document.body,
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { useId, type ReactNode, type RefObject } from 'react';
import { t } from '../i18n';
import { IconButton } from './Button';
import { Overlay } from './Overlay';

export interface ModalProps {
  open: boolean;
  onClose(): void;
  title: string;
  note?: string;
  footer?: ReactNode;
  size?: 'md' | 'sm';
  initialFocusRef?: RefObject<HTMLElement>;
  children: ReactNode;
}

export function Modal({ open, onClose, title, note, footer, size = 'md', initialFocusRef, children }: ModalProps) {
  const titleId = useId();
  return (
    <Overlay
      open={open}
      onClose={onClose}
      variant="modal"
      labelledBy={titleId}
      initialFocusRef={initialFocusRef}
      className={size === 'sm' ? 'modal--sm' : undefined}
    >
      <header className="dh">
        <div>
          {note ? <p className="note">{note}</p> : null}
          <h2 id={titleId}>{title}</h2>
        </div>
        <IconButton icon="close" label={t('overlay.close')} variant="ghost" onClick={onClose} />
      </header>
      <div className="db">{children}</div>
      {footer ? <footer className="df">{footer}</footer> : null}
    </Overlay>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
import { t } from '../i18n';
import { Button } from './Button';
import { FormError } from './Field';
import { Modal } from './Modal';

export interface ConfirmDialogProps {
  open: boolean;
  title: string;
  message: ReactNode;
  confirmLabel: string;
  onConfirm(): void;
  onCancel(): void;
  danger?: boolean;
  busy?: boolean;
  error?: unknown;
}

export function ConfirmDialog({ open, title, message, confirmLabel, onConfirm, onCancel, danger, busy, error }: ConfirmDialogProps) {
  return (
    <Modal
      open={open}
      onClose={onCancel}
      title={title}
      size="sm"
      footer={
        <>
          <Button variant="secondary" onClick={onCancel} disabled={busy}>
            {t('common.cancel')}
          </Button>
          <Button variant={danger ? 'danger' : 'primary'} loading={busy} onClick={onConfirm}>
            {confirmLabel}
          </Button>
        </>
      }
    >
      <div className="confirm">
        <div className="confirm__text">{message}</div>
        <FormError error={error} />
      </div>
    </Modal>
  );
}
```

```tsx
  open: boolean;
  onClose(): void;
  title: string;
  footer?: ReactNode;
  children: ReactNode;
}

export function BottomSheet({ open, onClose, title, footer, children }: BottomSheetProps) {
  const titleId = useId();
  return (
    <Overlay open={open} onClose={onClose} variant="sheet" labelledBy={titleId}>
      <div className="sheet__grip" aria-hidden="true" />
      <header className="dh">
        <h2 id={titleId}>{title}</h2>
        <IconButton icon="close" label={t('overlay.close')} variant="ghost" onClick={onClose} />
      </header>
      <div className="db">{children}</div>
      {footer ? <footer className="df">{footer}</footer> : null}
    </Overlay>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/ui/Toast.tsx`:
```tsx
import { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useMemo, useRef, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import { createPortal } from 'react-dom';
import { t } from '../i18n';
import './overlay.css';

export type ToastTone = 'info' | 'success' | 'error';
export const TOAST_MS = 6000;

interface ToastItem {
  id: number;
  message: string;
  tone: ToastTone;
}

interface ToastApi {
  show(message: string, tone?: ToastTone): void;
}

const ToastContext = createContext<ToastApi | null>(null);

/** Live-hudud `document.body` da: modal ochiqligida `#root` inert bo'lsa ham e'lon qilinadi. */
export function ToastProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [items, setItems] = useState<ToastItem[]>([]);
  const nextId = useRef(1);

  const dismiss = useCallback((id: number) => {
    setItems((list) => list.filter((item) => item.id !== id));
  }, []);

  const show = useCallback(
    (message: string, tone: ToastTone = 'info') => {
      const id = nextId.current++;
      setItems((list) => [...list.filter((item) => item.message !== message), { id, message, tone }].slice(-4));
      window.setTimeout(() => dismiss(id), TOAST_MS);
    },
    [dismiss],
  );

  const api = useMemo<ToastApi>(() => ({ show }), [show]);

  return (
    <ToastContext.Provider value={api}>
      {children}
      {createPortal(
        <div className="toasts" role="status" aria-live="polite" aria-label={t('toast.region')}>
          {items.map((item) => (
            <div key={item.id} className={`toast toast--${item.tone}`}>
              <span>{item.message}</span>
              <button type="button" aria-label={t('overlay.close')} onClick={() => dismiss(item.id)}>
                ×
              </button>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>,
        document.body,
      )}
    </ToastContext.Provider>
  );
}

export function useToast(): ToastApi {
  const context = useContext(ToastContext);
  if (!context) throw new Error('useToast: ToastProvider topilmadi');
  return context;
}
```

`frontend/src/ui/overlay.css`:
```css
.overlay {
  position: fixed;
  inset: 0;
  z-index: 60;
  display: flex;
  background: rgba(10, 30, 60, 0.4);
  animation: ugk-fade 0.16s ease-out;
}
.overlay__panel {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  max-height: 100%;
  background: var(--card);
  color: var(--ink);
  box-shadow: 0 24px 60px rgba(10, 30, 60, 0.3);
  outline: none;
}
.overlay--modal, .overlay--palette {
  align-items: flex-start;
  justify-content: center;
  padding: 6vh 1rem 1rem;
  overflow-y: auto;
}
.overlay--modal .overlay__panel {
  width: min(44rem, 100%);
  max-height: none;
  border-radius: 1rem;
  animation: ugk-rise 0.2s var(--ease-out);
}
.overlay--modal .overlay__panel.modal--sm { width: min(30rem, 100%); }
.overlay--palette .overlay__panel { width: min(42rem, 100%); border-radius: 1rem; overflow: hidden; }
.overlay--drawer { justify-content: flex-start; }
.overlay--drawer .overlay__panel {
  width: min(20rem, 88vw);
  height: 100%;
  border-radius: 0 1rem 1rem 0;
  animation: ugk-slide 0.22s var(--ease-out);
}
.overlay--sheet { align-items: flex-end; }
.overlay--sheet .overlay__panel {
  width: 100%;
  max-height: 85vh;
  border-radius: 1rem 1rem 0 0;
  padding-bottom: env(safe-area-inset-bottom);
  animation: ugk-up 0.22s var(--ease-out);
}
.sheet__grip { width: 2.5rem; height: 0.3rem; margin: 0.6rem auto 0; border-radius: 0.2rem; background: var(--line); }

.dh {
  display: flex;
  align-items: flex-start;
  justify-content: space-between;
  gap: 1rem;
  padding: 1rem 1.2rem;
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--line);
}
.dh h2 { margin: 0; font-size: 1.3rem; }
.dh .note { margin: 0 0 0.15rem; font-size: 0.9rem; color: var(--muted); }
.db { display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 1rem; padding: 1rem 1.2rem 1.2rem; overflow: auto; }
.db--flush { padding: 0.6rem; }
.df {
  display: flex;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  justify-content: flex-end;
  gap: 0.6rem;
  padding: 0.9rem 1.2rem;
  border-top: 1px solid var(--line);
}
.confirm { display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 0.8rem; }
.confirm__text { color: var(--ink); }

.toasts {
  position: fixed;
  z-index: 80;
  right: 1rem;
  bottom: 1rem;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 0.5rem;
  width: min(26rem, calc(100vw - 2rem));
}
.toasts:empty { display: none; }
.toast {
  display: flex;
  align-items: flex-start;
  gap: 0.7rem;
  padding: 0.8rem 0.9rem;
  border-radius: 0.7rem;
  background: var(--ink);
  color: #fff;
  box-shadow: var(--shadow-hover);
  animation: ugk-rise 0.2s var(--ease-out);
}
.toast--error { background: var(--neg); }
.toast--success { background: var(--ok); }
.toast span { flex: 1; }
.toast button {
  min-width: 1.75rem;
  min-height: 1.75rem;
  padding: 0 0.2rem;
  border: 0;
  background: none;
  color: inherit;
  font-size: 1.2rem;
  line-height: 1;
  cursor: pointer;
}
.toast button:focus-visible { outline-color: #fff; }

@keyframes ugk-fade { from { opacity: 0; } }
@keyframes ugk-rise { from { opacity: 0; transform: translateY(8px); } }
@keyframes ugk-slide { from { transform: translateX(-100%); } }
@keyframes ugk-up { from { transform: translateY(100%); } }

@media (max-width: 767.98px) {
  .overlay--modal { padding: 0; align-items: stretch; }
  .overlay--modal .overlay__panel { border-radius: 0; min-height: 100%; }
  .df .btn { flex: 1; }
  .toast button { min-width: 44px; min-height: 44px; }
}
@media print {
  .overlay, .toasts { display: none !important; }
}
```

```tsx
import { render, screen, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { expectNoAxeViolations } from '../test/axe';
import { Pagination } from './Pagination';
import { Table, type Column } from './Table';

interface Row {
  id: number;
  name: string;
  amount: number | null;
}

const ROWS: Row[] = [
  { id: 1, name: 'Чарвакская ГЭС', amount: 120 },
  { id: 2, name: 'Андижанская ГЭС', amount: null },
  { id: 3, name: 'Бозсу', amount: 540 },
];

const COLUMNS: Column<Row>[] = [
  { key: 'name', header: 'Филиал', align: 'left', cell: (r) => r.name, sortValue: (r) => r.name },
  { key: 'amount', header: 'Сумма', cell: (r) => (r.amount === null ? '—' : String(r.amount)), sortValue: (r) => r.amount },
  { key: 'note', header: 'Примечание', cell: () => 'x' },
];

const names = () =>
  screen
    .getAllByRole('row')
    .slice(1)
    .map((row) => within(row).getAllByRole('cell')[0]?.textContent);

describe('Table', () => {
  it('sorts by clickable headers with aria-sort and keeps nulls last', async () => {
    render(<Table caption="Филиалы" columns={COLUMNS} rows={ROWS} rowKey={(r) => r.id} />);
    expect(screen.getByRole('table', { name: 'Филиалы' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: 'Примечание' })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Сумма' }));
    expect(screen.getByRole('columnheader', { name: 'Сумма' })).toHaveAttribute('aria-sort', 'ascending');
    expect(names()).toEqual(['Чарвакская ГЭС', 'Бозсу', 'Андижанская ГЭС']);
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Сумма' }));
    expect(screen.getByRole('columnheader', { name: 'Сумма' })).toHaveAttribute('aria-sort', 'descending');
    expect(names()).toEqual(['Бозсу', 'Чарвакская ГЭС', 'Андижанская ГЭС']);
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Филиал' }));
    expect(names()).toEqual(['Андижанская ГЭС', 'Бозсу', 'Чарвакская ГЭС']);
  });

  it('activates rows with click, Enter and Space', async () => {
    const onRowClick = vi.fn();
    render(<Table caption="Филиалы" columns={COLUMNS} rows={ROWS} rowKey={(r) => r.id} onRowClick={onRowClick} activeKey={3} />);
    const rows = screen.getAllByRole('row').slice(1);
    await userEvent.click(within(rows[0] as HTMLElement).getByText('Чарвакская ГЭС'));
    expect(onRowClick).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith(ROWS[0]);
    (rows[1] as HTMLElement).focus();
    await userEvent.keyboard('{Enter}');
    expect(onRowClick).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith(ROWS[1]);
    await userEvent.keyboard(' ');
    expect(onRowClick).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(3);
    expect(rows[2]).toHaveClass('active');
    expect(rows[2]).toHaveAttribute('tabindex', '0');
  });

  it('labels every cell for the mobile card layout and shows the empty state', () => {
    const { rerender } = render(<Table caption="Филиалы" columns={COLUMNS} rows={ROWS} rowKey={(r) => r.id} />);
    const firstCell = screen.getAllByRole('cell')[1];
    expect(firstCell).toHaveAttribute('data-label', 'Сумма');
    expect(screen.getByRole('table').parentElement).toHaveClass('tw--cards');
    rerender(<Table caption="Филиалы" columns={COLUMNS} rows={[]} rowKey={(r) => r.id} empty="Пользователи не найдены" />);
    expect(screen.getByText('Пользователи не найдены')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('has no axe violations', async () => {
    const { container } = render(
      <Table caption="Филиалы" columns={COLUMNS} rows={ROWS} rowKey={(r) => r.id} onRowClick={vi.fn()} />,
    );
    await expectNoAxeViolations(container);
  });
});

const NBSP = String.fromCharCode(0xa0);

describe('Pagination', () => {
  it('shows the range and moves between pages', async () => {
    const onPage = vi.fn();
    render(<Pagination page={2} pageSize={50} total={1234} onPage={onPage} />);
    expect(screen.getByRole('navigation', { name: 'Страницы' })).toHaveTextContent('51–100 из 1' + NBSP + '234');
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Предыдущая страница' }));
    expect(onPage).toHaveBeenCalledWith(1);
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Следующая страница' }));
    expect(onPage).toHaveBeenCalledWith(3);
  });

  it('disables the edges', () => {
    render(<Pagination page={1} pageSize={50} total={10} onPage={vi.fn()} />);
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Предыдущая страница' })).toBeDisabled();
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Следующая страница' })).toBeDisabled();
  });
});
```

`frontend/src/ui/Table.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useMemo, useState, type KeyboardEvent, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import { t } from '../i18n';
import { cx } from './cx';
import './table.css';

export interface Column<T> {
  key: string;
  header: string;
  cell(row: T): ReactNode;
  sortValue?(row: T): string | number | null;
  align?: 'left' | 'right';
  wrap?: boolean;
}

export interface SortState {
  key: string;
  dir: 'asc' | 'desc';
}

export interface TableProps<T> {
  caption: string;
  captionHidden?: boolean;
  columns: readonly Column<T>[];
  rows: readonly T[];
  rowKey(row: T): string | number;
  onRowClick?(row: T): void;
  activeKey?: string | number | null;
  empty?: ReactNode;
  initialSort?: SortState;
  mobile?: 'cards' | 'scroll';
  /** true — balandlik cheklanmaydi (sahifa bo'yi jadval). */
  free?: boolean;
  footer?: ReactNode;
  className?: string;
}

function compare(a: string | number | null, b: string | number | null): number {
  if (a === null && b === null) return 0;
  if (a === null) return 1;
  if (b === null) return -1;
  if (typeof a === 'number' && typeof b === 'number') return a - b;
  return String(a).localeCompare(String(b), 'ru');
}

export function Table<T>({
  caption,
  captionHidden = true,
  columns,
  rows,
  rowKey,
  onRowClick,
  activeKey = null,
  empty,
  initialSort,
  mobile = 'cards',
  free = false,
  footer,
  className,
}: TableProps<T>) {
  const [sort, setSort] = useState<SortState | null>(initialSort ?? null);

  const sorted = useMemo(() => {
    const column = sort ? columns.find((c) => c.key === sort.key) : undefined;
    if (!sort || !column?.sortValue) return rows;
    const value = column.sortValue;
    const sign = sort.dir === 'asc' ? 1 : -1;
    return [...rows].sort((x, y) => {
      const a = value(x);
      const b = value(y);
      if (a === null || b === null) return compare(a, b);
      return sign * compare(a, b);
    });
  }, [rows, columns, sort]);

  const toggle = (key: string) => {
    setSort((current) =>
      current?.key === key ? { key, dir: current.dir === 'asc' ? 'desc' : 'asc' } : { key, dir: 'asc' },
    );
  };

  const onRowKeyDown = (event: KeyboardEvent<HTMLTableRowElement>, row: T) => {
    if (event.key !== 'Enter' && event.key !== ' ') return;
    event.preventDefault();
    onRowClick?.(row);
  };

  return (
    <div className={cx('tw', mobile === 'cards' && 'tw--cards', free && 'tw--free', className)}>
      <table className="table">
        <caption className={captionHidden ? 'visually-hidden' : undefined}>{caption}</caption>
        <thead>
          <tr>
            {columns.map((column) => {
              const dir = sort !== null && sort.key === column.key ? sort.dir : null;
              return (
                <th
                  key={column.key}
                  scope="col"
                  className={cx(column.align === 'left' && 'l')}
                  aria-sort={dir ? (dir === 'asc' ? 'ascending' : 'descending') : undefined}
                >
                  {column.sortValue ? (
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      className="th-sort"
                      data-dir={dir ?? undefined}
                      onClick={() => toggle(column.key)}
                    >
                      {column.header}
                    </button>
                  ) : (
                    column.header
                  )}
                </th>
              );
            })}
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          {sorted.length === 0 ? (
            <tr>
              <td colSpan={columns.length} className="empty">
                {empty ?? t('table.empty')}
              </td>
            </tr>
          ) : (
            sorted.map((row) => {
              const key = rowKey(row);
              const clickable = Boolean(onRowClick);
              return (
                // eslint-disable-next-line jsx-a11y/no-noninteractive-element-interactions -- TZ 6: jadval qatorlarida Enter/Space
                <tr
                  key={key}
                  className={cx(clickable && 'click', activeKey === key && 'active')}
                  // eslint-disable-next-line jsx-a11y/no-noninteractive-tabindex -- qator klaviatura bilan tanlanadi
                  tabIndex={clickable ? 0 : undefined}
                  onClick={clickable ? () => onRowClick?.(row) : undefined}
                  onKeyDown={clickable ? (event) => onRowKeyDown(event, row) : undefined}
                >
                  {columns.map((column) => (
                    <td
                      key={column.key}
                      data-label={column.header}
                      className={cx(column.align === 'left' && 'l', column.wrap && 'w')}
                    >
                      {column.cell(row)}
                    </td>
                  ))}
                </tr>
              );
            })
          )}
        </tbody>
        {footer ? <tfoot>{footer}</tfoot> : null}
      </table>
    </div>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/ui/Pagination.tsx`:
```tsx
import { t } from '../i18n';
import { formatNumber } from '../i18n/format';
import { IconButton } from './Button';
import './table.css';

export interface PaginationProps {
  page: number;
  pageSize: number;
  total: number;
  onPage(page: number): void;
}

export function Pagination({ page, pageSize, total, onPage }: PaginationProps) {
  const pages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(total / pageSize));
  const from = total === 0 ? 0 : (page - 1) * pageSize + 1;
  const to = Math.min(total, page * pageSize);
  return (
    <nav className="pager" aria-label={t('table.pagination')}>
      <span aria-live="polite">
        {t('table.range', { from: formatNumber(from), to: formatNumber(to), total: formatNumber(total) })}
      </span>
      <IconButton icon="chevronLeft" label={t('table.prev')} disabled={page <= 1} onClick={() => onPage(page - 1)} />
      <span>{t('table.pageOf', { page, pages })}</span>
      <IconButton icon="chevronRight" label={t('table.next')} disabled={page >= pages} onClick={() => onPage(page + 1)} />
    </nav>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/ui/table.css`:
```css
.tw {
  max-height: clamp(22rem, 58vh, 46rem);
  overflow: auto;
  background: var(--card);
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius: 0.5rem;
}
.tw--free { max-height: none; }
.table { width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; font-size: 0.98rem; }
.table th, .table td { padding: 0.65rem 0.85rem; text-align: right; white-space: nowrap; }
.table .l { text-align: left; }
.table .w { min-width: 12rem; white-space: normal; }
.table thead th {
  position: sticky;
  top: 0;
  z-index: 1;
  background: linear-gradient(180deg, #f2f8ff, #eaf3ff);
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--line);
  color: var(--ink);
  font-size: 0.92rem;
  font-weight: 600;
}
.th-sort { all: unset; cursor: pointer; border-radius: 0.25rem; }
.th-sort:focus-visible { outline: 2px solid var(--focus); outline-offset: 2px; }
.th-sort[data-dir='desc']::after { content: ' ↓'; }
.th-sort[data-dir='asc']::after { content: ' ↑'; }
.table tbody tr { border-bottom: 1px solid var(--line); }
.table tbody tr:last-child { border-bottom: 0; }
.table tbody tr:hover { background: #f3f9ff; }
.table tbody tr.click { cursor: pointer; }
.table tbody tr.click:focus-visible { outline: 2px solid var(--focus); outline-offset: -2px; }
.table tbody tr.active { background: #eaf4ff; box-shadow: inset 3px 0 0 var(--brand2); }
.table tfoot td { position: sticky; bottom: 0; background: var(--card); border-top: 2px solid var(--line); font-weight: 700; }
.table .empty { padding: 2.5rem 1rem; text-align: center; white-space: normal; color: var(--muted); }

.cell-main { display: flex; flex-direction: column; align-items: flex-start; gap: 0.2rem; }
.cell-main small { font-size: 0.85rem; color: var(--muted); }
.table .actions { display: inline-flex; flex-wrap: wrap; justify-content: flex-end; gap: 0.4rem; }

.pager {
  display: flex;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: flex-end;
  gap: 0.6rem;
  padding-top: 0.8rem;
  font-size: 0.92rem;
  color: var(--muted);
}

@media (max-width: 767.98px) {
  .tw--cards { max-height: none; overflow: visible; background: transparent; border: 0; }
  .tw--cards .table,
  .tw--cards .table tbody,
  .tw--cards .table tr,
  .tw--cards .table td { display: block; width: 100%; }
  .tw--cards .table thead { position: absolute; width: 1px; height: 1px; overflow: hidden; clip: rect(0 0 0 0); }
  .tw--cards .table tbody { display: grid; gap: 0.7rem; }
  .tw--cards .table tbody tr {
    padding: 0.4rem 0.9rem;
    background: var(--card);
    border: 1px solid var(--line);
    border-radius: 0.75rem;
    box-shadow: var(--shadow);
  }
  .tw--cards .table tbody tr:last-child { border-bottom: 1px solid var(--line); }
  .tw--cards .table td {
    display: flex;
    justify-content: space-between;
    gap: 1rem;
    padding: 0.45rem 0;
    white-space: normal;
    text-align: right;
    border-bottom: 1px solid var(--line);
  }
  .tw--cards .table td:last-child { border-bottom: 0; }
  .tw--cards .table td::before {
    content: attr(data-label);
    flex: none;
    max-width: 45%;
    color: var(--muted);
    font-weight: 500;
    text-align: left;
  }
  .tw--cards .table td.empty { display: block; text-align: center; }
  .tw--cards .table td.empty::before { content: none; }
  .tw--cards .table tfoot { display: block; }
}
@media print {
  .tw { max-height: none !important; overflow: visible !important; }
}
```

### Task 9: Ma'lumot qatlami — auth va meta hook'lari, `AuthFlow` konteksti, test fixture'lari

**Files:**
- Create: `frontend/src/auth/api.ts`, `frontend/src/auth/AuthFlow.tsx`, `frontend/src/auth/destination.ts`, `frontend/src/meta/api.ts`, `frontend/src/test/render.tsx`, `frontend/src/test/fixtures.ts`
- Test: `frontend/src/auth/api.test.tsx`, `frontend/src/meta/api.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `api`, `ApiError` (Task 4), tiplar (`MeOut`, `LoginIn`, `LoginOut`, `MfaIn`, `MfaTokenIn`, `EnrollStartOut`, `EnrollConfirmOut`, `PasswordChangeIn`, `SessionOut`, `MenuOut`, `BranchOut`, `PeriodsOut`, `FreshnessOut`), `navigate`, `parseHash`, `Route` (Task 3), `ToastProvider` (Task 7).
- Produces:
  - `src/auth/api.ts`: `meKey = ['auth', 'me']`, `sessionsKey = ['auth', 'sessions']`, `useMe()`, `useLogin()`, `useVerifyMfa()`, `useEnrollStart()`, `useEnrollConfirm()`, `useChangePassword()`, `useLogout()`, `useSessions()`, `useRevokeSession()`. Login/2FA mutatsiyalari `meta: { silent: true }` (xato formada ko'rsatiladi). `useVerifyMfa`/`useEnrollConfirm` muvaffaqiyatda `meKey` keshini to'ldiradi; `useChangePassword` keshdagi `must_change_password` ni darhol `false` qiladi va `me` ni qayta so'raydi; `useLogout` natijadan qat'i nazar `#login` ga o'tadi va keshni tozalaydi.
  - `src/auth/AuthFlow.tsx`: `interface Challenge { mfaToken: string; step: 'mfa_verify' | 'mfa_enroll'; username: string; returnTo: string | null }`, `AuthFlowProvider`, `useAuthFlow() -> { challenge; returnTo; begin(c: Challenge); finish(); clear() }` — `mfa_token` faqat xotirada; `finish()` challenge'ni o'chiradi, `returnTo` ni parol almashtirish qadami uchun saqlaydi; `clear()` hammasini o'chiradi.
  - `src/auth/destination.ts`: `destinationAfterSignIn(me: MeOut, returnTo: string | null) -> Route`.
  - `src/meta/api.ts`: `menuKey`, `useMenu(enabled = true)`, `useBranches()`, `usePeriods()`, `useFreshness()` (5 daqiqada qayta so'raydi).
  - `src/test/render.tsx`: `createTestClient()`, `Providers({ client, children })`, `renderWithProviders(ui, { hash?, client? }) -> RenderResult & { user; client }`.
  - `src/test/fixtures.ts`: `ALL_PERMISSIONS`, `ME_ADMIN`, `ME_BRANCH` (`branch_ids: [3]`), `ME_AUDITOR` (faqat `audit:read` + obzor), `MENU_FULL`, `MENU_BRANCH`, `MENU_AUDITOR`, `BRANCHES` (id 1–4), `PERIODS` (`default: 'ytd'`), `FRESH_OK`, `FRESH_STALE`, `shellRoutes(me?, menu?) -> Record<string, MockHandler>`.

```ts
  { id: 3, code: 'CHIRCHIK', name: 'Каскад Чирчикских ГЭС', short_name: 'Чирчик', kind: 'cascade', is_hq: false, capacity_mw: 190, lat: 41.47, lon: 69.58 },
  { id: 4, code: 'ANDIJAN', name: 'Андижанская ГЭС', short_name: 'Андижан', kind: 'ges', is_hq: false, capacity_mw: 190, lat: 40.76, lon: 72.99 },
];

export const PERIODS: PeriodsOut = {
  default: 'ytd',
  today: '2026-09-24',
  items: [
    { code: 'ytd', kind: 'ytd', label: 'Январь – сентябрь 2026', date_from: '2026-01-01', date_to: '2026-09-24' },
    { code: 'q1', kind: 'quarter', label: 'I квартал 2026', date_from: '2026-01-01', date_to: '2026-03-31' },
    { code: 'q2', kind: 'quarter', label: 'II квартал 2026', date_from: '2026-04-01', date_to: '2026-06-30' },
    { code: 'q3', kind: 'quarter', label: 'III квартал 2026', date_from: '2026-07-01', date_to: '2026-09-24' },
    { code: 'm01', kind: 'month', label: 'Январь 2026', date_from: '2026-01-01', date_to: '2026-01-31' },
    { code: 'm05', kind: 'month', label: 'Май 2026', date_from: '2026-05-01', date_to: '2026-05-31' },
    { code: 'm09', kind: 'month', label: 'Сентябрь 2026', date_from: '2026-09-01', date_to: '2026-09-24' },
  ],
};

export const FRESH_OK: FreshnessOut = { last_success_at: '2026-09-24T05:10:00Z', stale: false, max_age_hours: 6, active_build_id: 12 };
export const FRESH_STALE: FreshnessOut = { last_success_at: '2026-09-23T20:00:00Z', stale: true, max_age_hours: 6, active_build_id: 11 };

/** Qobiq uchun kerakli barcha GET'lar (me, menyu, filiallar RLS bo'yicha, davrlar, freshness). */
export function shellRoutes(me: MeOut = ME_ADMIN, menuOut: MenuOut = MENU_FULL): Record<string, MockHandler> {
  const scope = me.branch_ids;
  return {
    'GET /auth/me': json(me),
    'GET /meta/menu': json(menuOut),
    'GET /meta/branches': json(scope === null ? BRANCHES : BRANCHES.filter((b) => scope.includes(b.id))),
    'GET /meta/periods': json(PERIODS),
    'GET /meta/freshness': json(FRESH_OK),
  };
}
```

`frontend/src/test/render.tsx`:
```tsx
import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { render, type RenderResult } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import type { ReactElement, ReactNode } from 'react';
import { AuthFlowProvider } from '../auth/AuthFlow';
import { ToastProvider } from '../ui/Toast';

export function createTestClient(): QueryClient {
  return new QueryClient({
    defaultOptions: { queries: { retry: false, gcTime: Infinity, staleTime: 60_000 }, mutations: { retry: false } },
  });
}

export function Providers({ client, children }: { client: QueryClient; children: ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <ToastProvider>
      <QueryClientProvider client={client}>
        <AuthFlowProvider>{children}</AuthFlowProvider>
      </QueryClientProvider>
    </ToastProvider>
  );
}

export function renderWithProviders(
  ui: ReactElement,
  options: { hash?: string; client?: QueryClient } = {},
): RenderResult & { user: ReturnType<typeof userEvent.setup>; client: QueryClient } {
  if (options.hash !== undefined) window.history.replaceState(null, '', options.hash || '/');
  const client = options.client ?? createTestClient();
  const user = userEvent.setup();
  const result = render(<Providers client={client}>{ui}</Providers>);
  return { ...result, user, client };
}
```

`frontend/src/auth/api.test.tsx`:
```tsx
import { act, renderHook, waitFor } from '@testing-library/react';
import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { json, mockApi } from '../test/mockApi';
import { ME_ADMIN } from '../test/fixtures';
import { createTestClient, Providers } from '../test/render';
import { meKey, useLogin, useLogout, useMe, useRevokeSession, useSessions, useVerifyMfa } from './api';
import { useAuthFlow } from './AuthFlow';
import { destinationAfterSignIn } from './destination';

function setup() {
  const client = createTestClient();
  const wrapper = ({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) => <Providers client={client}>{children}</Providers>;
  return { client, wrapper };
}

describe('auth hooks', () => {
  it('loads the current user', async () => {
    mockApi({ 'GET /auth/me': json(ME_ADMIN) });
    const { wrapper } = setup();
    const { result } = renderHook(() => useMe(), { wrapper });
    await waitFor(() => expect(result.current.data?.username).toBe('admin'));
  });

  it('logs in silently and returns the next step', async () => {
    const server = mockApi({ 'POST /auth/login': json({ next: 'mfa_verify', mfa_token: 't'.repeat(32) }) });
    const { wrapper } = setup();
    const { result } = renderHook(() => useLogin(), { wrapper });
    let next = '';
    await act(async () => {
      next = (await result.current.mutateAsync({ username: 'admin', password: 'x' })).next;
    });
    expect(next).toBe('mfa_verify');
    expect(server.calls[0]?.body).toEqual({ username: 'admin', password: 'x' });
  });

  it('stores the user in the cache after 2FA', async () => {
    mockApi({ 'POST /auth/2fa/verify': json(ME_ADMIN) });
    const { client, wrapper } = setup();
    const { result } = renderHook(() => useVerifyMfa(), { wrapper });
    await act(async () => {
      await result.current.mutateAsync({ mfa_token: 't'.repeat(32), code: '123456' });
    });
    expect(client.getQueryData(meKey)).toEqual(ME_ADMIN);
  });

  it('logs out to the login page and clears the cache even if the call fails', async () => {
    mockApi({ 'POST /auth/logout': { status: 500 } });
    const { client, wrapper } = setup();
    client.setQueryData(meKey, ME_ADMIN);
    window.history.replaceState(null, '', '#kazna/svod');
    const { result } = renderHook(() => useLogout(), { wrapper });
    await act(async () => {
      await result.current.mutateAsync().catch(() => undefined);
    });
    expect(window.location.hash).toBe('#login');
    await waitFor(() => expect(client.getQueryData(meKey)).toBeUndefined());
  });

  it('lists and revokes own sessions', async () => {
    const sessions = [
      { id: 'a', created_at: '2026-09-24T05:00:00Z', last_seen_at: '2026-09-24T05:30:00Z', ua_summary: 'Chrome · Windows', ip: '10.0.0.5', current: true },
      { id: 'b', created_at: '2026-09-20T05:00:00Z', last_seen_at: '2026-09-21T05:30:00Z', ua_summary: 'Firefox · Linux', ip: null, current: false },
    ];
    const server = mockApi({ 'GET /auth/sessions': json(sessions), 'DELETE /auth/sessions/b': { status: 204 } });
    const { wrapper } = setup();
    const list = renderHook(() => useSessions(), { wrapper });
    await waitFor(() => expect(list.result.current.data).toHaveLength(2));
    const revoke = renderHook(() => useRevokeSession(), { wrapper });
    await act(async () => {
      await revoke.result.current.mutateAsync('b');
    });
    expect(server.calls.map((c) => `${c.method} ${c.path}`)).toContain('DELETE /auth/sessions/b');
  });
});

describe('AuthFlow', () => {
  it('keeps the challenge in memory and the return target after finish', () => {
    const { wrapper } = setup();
    const { result } = renderHook(() => useAuthFlow(), { wrapper });
    act(() => result.current.begin({ mfaToken: 'tok', step: 'mfa_verify', username: 'admin', returnTo: 'kazna/svod?br=3' }));
    expect(result.current.challenge?.mfaToken).toBe('tok');
    act(() => result.current.finish());
    expect(result.current.challenge).toBeNull();
    expect(result.current.returnTo).toBe('kazna/svod?br=3');
    act(() => result.current.clear());
    expect(result.current.returnTo).toBeNull();
    expect(window.sessionStorage.length).toBe(0);
    expect(window.localStorage.length).toBe(0);
  });
});

describe('destinationAfterSignIn', () => {
  it('forces the password page and otherwise returns to the safe target', () => {
    expect(destinationAfterSignIn({ ...ME_ADMIN, must_change_password: true }, 'kazna/svod')).toEqual({ kind: 'password' });
    expect(destinationAfterSignIn(ME_ADMIN, 'kazna/svod?br=3')).toEqual({ kind: 'section', section: 'kazna', page: 'svod', br: 3, per: null });
    expect(destinationAfterSignIn(ME_ADMIN, null)).toEqual({ kind: 'home' });
    expect(destinationAfterSignIn(ME_ADMIN, 'login')).toEqual({ kind: 'home' });
  });
});
```

`frontend/src/auth/AuthFlow.tsx`:
```tsx
import { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useMemo, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react';

/** Login → 2FA oralig'idagi holat. mfa_token faqat xotirada: sahifa yangilansa — login qaytadan. */
export interface Challenge {
  mfaToken: string;
  step: 'mfa_verify' | 'mfa_enroll';
  username: string;
  returnTo: string | null;
}

interface AuthFlowValue {
  challenge: Challenge | null;
  /** 2FA'dan keyin (masalan, majburiy parol almashtirishdan so'ng) qaytiladigan joy. */
  returnTo: string | null;
  begin(challenge: Challenge): void;
  finish(): void;
  clear(): void;
}

const AuthFlowContext = createContext<AuthFlowValue | null>(null);

export function AuthFlowProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [challenge, setChallenge] = useState<Challenge | null>(null);
  const [returnTo, setReturnTo] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const begin = useCallback((next: Challenge) => {
    setChallenge(next);
    setReturnTo(next.returnTo);
  }, []);
  const finish = useCallback(() => setChallenge(null), []);
  const clear = useCallback(() => {
    setChallenge(null);
    setReturnTo(null);
  }, []);

  const value = useMemo(() => ({ challenge, returnTo, begin, finish, clear }), [challenge, returnTo, begin, finish, clear]);
  return <AuthFlowContext.Provider value={value}>{children}</AuthFlowContext.Provider>;
}

export function useAuthFlow(): AuthFlowValue {
  const context = useContext(AuthFlowContext);
  if (!context) throw new Error('useAuthFlow: AuthFlowProvider topilmadi');
  return context;
}
```

`frontend/src/auth/destination.ts`:
```ts
import type { MeOut } from '../api/types';
import { parseHash, safeNext, type Route } from '../router/hash';

export function destinationAfterSignIn(me: MeOut, returnTo: string | null): Route {
  if (me.must_change_password) return { kind: 'password' };
  const target = safeNext(returnTo);
  return target ? parseHash(target) : { kind: 'home' };
}
```

`frontend/src/auth/api.ts`:
```ts
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { api } from '../api/client';
import type {
  EnrollConfirmOut,
  EnrollStartOut,
  LoginIn,
  LoginOut,
  MeOut,
  MfaIn,
  MfaTokenIn,
  PasswordChangeIn,
  SessionOut,
} from '../api/types';
import { navigate } from '../router/store';

export const meKey = ['auth', 'me'] as const;
export const sessionsKey = ['auth', 'sessions'] as const;

export function useMe() {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: meKey,
    queryFn: ({ signal }) => api<MeOut>('/auth/me', { signal }),
    retry: false,
    staleTime: 60_000,
  });
}

export function useLogin() {
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (body: LoginIn) => api<LoginOut>('/auth/login', { method: 'POST', body }),
    meta: { silent: true },
  });
}

export function useVerifyMfa() {
  const client = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (body: MfaIn) => api<MeOut>('/auth/2fa/verify', { method: 'POST', body }),
    meta: { silent: true },
    onSuccess: (me) => client.setQueryData(meKey, me),
  });
}

export function useEnrollStart() {
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (body: MfaTokenIn) => api<EnrollStartOut>('/auth/2fa/enroll/start', { method: 'POST', body }),
    meta: { silent: true },
  });
}

export function useEnrollConfirm() {
  const client = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (body: MfaIn) => api<EnrollConfirmOut>('/auth/2fa/enroll/confirm', { method: 'POST', body }),
    meta: { silent: true },
    onSuccess: (result) => client.setQueryData(meKey, result.user),
  });
}

/** 422 (joriy parol / siyosat) formada ko'rsatiladi; 401 global ishlovga ketadi.
 *  Muvaffaqiyatda keshdagi `must_change_password` darhol false — RequireAuth qayta #password ga qaytarmasin. */
export function useChangePassword() {
  const client = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (body: PasswordChangeIn) => api<void>('/auth/password/change', { method: 'POST', body }),
    onSuccess: async () => {
      client.setQueryData<MeOut>(meKey, (old) => (old ? { ...old, must_change_password: false } : old));
      await client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: meKey });
    },
  });
}

export function useLogout() {
  const client = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: () => api<void>('/auth/logout', { method: 'POST' }),
    meta: { silent: true },
    onSettled: () => {
      navigate({ kind: 'login', next: null }, { replace: true });
      queueMicrotask(() => client.clear());
    },
  });
}

export function useSessions() {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: sessionsKey,
    queryFn: ({ signal }) => api<SessionOut[]>('/auth/sessions', { signal }),
    staleTime: 0,
  });
}

export function useRevokeSession() {
  const client = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (id: string) => api<void>(`/auth/sessions/${encodeURIComponent(id)}`, { method: 'DELETE' }),
    onSuccess: () => client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: sessionsKey }),
  });
}
```

`frontend/src/meta/api.ts`:
```ts
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { api } from '../api/client';
import type { BranchOut, FreshnessOut, MenuOut, PeriodsOut } from '../api/types';

export const menuKey = ['meta', 'menu'] as const;

export function useMenu(enabled = true) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: menuKey,
    queryFn: ({ signal }) => api<MenuOut>('/meta/menu', { signal }),
    staleTime: 30 * 60_000,
    enabled,
  });
}

export function useBranches() {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['meta', 'branches'],
    queryFn: ({ signal }) => api<BranchOut[]>('/meta/branches', { signal }),
    staleTime: 30 * 60_000,
  });
}

export function usePeriods() {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['meta', 'periods'],
    queryFn: ({ signal }) => api<PeriodsOut>('/meta/periods', { signal }),
    staleTime: 30 * 60_000,
  });
}

export function useFreshness() {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['meta', 'freshness'],
    queryFn: ({ signal }) => api<FreshnessOut>('/meta/freshness', { signal }),
    staleTime: 60_000,
    refetchInterval: 5 * 60_000,
  });
}
```
