```tsx
import { act, screen, waitFor, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import type { MeOut, MenuOut } from '../api/types';
import { useRoute } from '../router/store';
import { expectNoAxeViolations } from '../test/axe';
import { ME_ADMIN, ME_BRANCH, MENU_BRANCH, MENU_FULL, shellRoutes } from '../test/fixtures';
import { apiError, mockApi } from '../test/mockApi';
import { renderWithProviders } from '../test/render';
import { setViewport } from '../test/viewport';
import { SectionScreen } from './SectionScreen';

function Harness({ me = ME_ADMIN, menu = MENU_FULL }: { me?: MeOut; menu?: MenuOut }) {
  const route = useRoute();
  if (route.kind !== 'section') return <p>route:{route.kind}</p>;
  return <SectionScreen me={me} menu={menu} route={route} />;
}

describe('SectionScreen', () => {
  it('renders section navigation, the side menu and the placeholder page with filters', async () => {
    mockApi(shellRoutes());
    renderWithProviders(<Harness />, { hash: '#kazna/platezhi?br=2&per=q1' });
    expect(await screen.findByRole('heading', { level: 1, name: 'Исполнение платежей' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    const nav = screen.getByRole('navigation', { name: 'Разделы' });
    expect(within(nav).getAllByRole('link')).toHaveLength(9);
    expect(within(nav).getByRole('link', { name: 'Казначейство' })).toHaveAttribute('aria-current', 'page');
    expect(within(nav).getByRole('link', { name: 'Кредиты' })).toHaveAttribute('href', '#kredity/svod?br=2&per=q1');
    const side = screen.getByRole('complementary', { name: 'Казначейство' });
    expect(within(side).getByRole('link', { name: 'Исполнение платежей' })).toHaveAttribute('aria-current', 'page');
    expect(within(side).getByRole('link', { name: 'Платёжный календарь' })).toHaveAttribute(
      'href',
      '#kazna/calendar?br=2&per=q1',
    );
    expect(await screen.findByText('Раздел в разработке')).toBeInTheDocument();
    const chips = screen.getByRole('group', { name: 'Активные фильтры' });
    expect(chips).toHaveTextContent('Филиал: Чарвакская ГЭС');
    expect(chips).toHaveTextContent('Период: I квартал 2026');
    expect(within(chips).getByRole('button', { name: 'Сбросить всё' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(document.title).toBe('Исполнение платежей | Казначейство | UGEKAZNA');
  });

  it('fills in the first page when the page is missing or unknown', async () => {
    mockApi(shellRoutes());
    renderWithProviders(<Harness />, { hash: '#kazna' });
    await waitFor(() => expect(window.location.hash).toBe('#kazna/svod'));
    expect(await screen.findByRole('heading', { level: 1, name: 'Казначейство: сводка' })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('normalizes unknown branch and period in the URL', async () => {
    mockApi(shellRoutes());
    renderWithProviders(<Harness />, { hash: '#obzor/svod?br=99&per=m12' });
    await waitFor(() => expect(window.location.hash).toBe('#obzor/svod'));
    expect(screen.queryByRole('group', { name: 'Активные фильтры' })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('drops the default period from the URL', async () => {
    mockApi(shellRoutes());
    renderWithProviders(<Harness />, { hash: '#obzor/svod?per=ytd' });
    await waitFor(() => expect(window.location.hash).toBe('#obzor/svod'));
  });

  it('shows no-access for a forbidden section and not-found for an unknown one', async () => {
    mockApi(shellRoutes(ME_BRANCH, MENU_BRANCH));
    const { unmount } = renderWithProviders(<Harness me={ME_BRANCH} menu={MENU_BRANCH} />, { hash: '#kredity/svod' });
    expect(await screen.findByRole('heading', { name: 'Нет доступа' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    unmount();
    renderWithProviders(<Harness me={ME_BRANCH} menu={MENU_BRANCH} />, { hash: '#foo/bar' });
    expect(await screen.findByRole('heading', { name: 'Страница не найдена' })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('locks the branch filter for a single-branch user', async () => {
    mockApi(shellRoutes(ME_BRANCH, MENU_BRANCH));
    renderWithProviders(<Harness me={ME_BRANCH} menu={MENU_BRANCH} />, { hash: '#obzor/svod?br=2' });
    await waitFor(() => expect(window.location.hash).toBe('#obzor/svod?br=3'));
    const select = await screen.findByLabelText('Филиал');
    await waitFor(() => expect(select).toHaveDisplayValue('Каскад Чирчикских ГЭС'));
    expect(select).toBeDisabled();
    expect(screen.getByText('Доступны только данные вашего филиала')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: 'Сбросить: Филиал' })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('writes filter changes to the URL and resets them', async () => {
    mockApi(shellRoutes());
    const { user } = renderWithProviders(<Harness />, { hash: '#obzor/svod' });
    const period = await screen.findByLabelText('Период');
    await waitFor(() => expect(period).toBeEnabled());
    await user.selectOptions(period, 'q2');
    expect(window.location.hash).toBe('#obzor/svod?per=q2');
    await user.selectOptions(screen.getByLabelText('Филиал'), '4');
    expect(window.location.hash).toBe('#obzor/svod?br=4&per=q2');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Сбросить: Период' }));
    expect(window.location.hash).toBe('#obzor/svod?br=4');
    await user.selectOptions(screen.getByLabelText('Период'), 'm05');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Сбросить всё' }));
    expect(window.location.hash).toBe('#obzor/svod');
  });

  it('shows an error banner with retry when filter dictionaries fail', async () => {
    mockApi({ ...shellRoutes(), 'GET /meta/branches': apiError(500, 'internal_error', 'Внутренняя ошибка сервера') });
    renderWithProviders(<Harness />, { hash: '#obzor/svod' });
    const alert = await screen.findByRole('alert');
    expect(alert).toHaveTextContent('Не удалось загрузить справочники фильтров');
    expect(within(alert).getByRole('button', { name: 'Повторить' })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('mobile: side menu opens in a drawer and filters in a bottom sheet', async () => {
    setViewport(390);
    mockApi(shellRoutes());
    const { user } = renderWithProviders(<Harness />, { hash: '#kazna/svod' });
    await screen.findByRole('heading', { level: 1, name: 'Казначейство: сводка' });
    expect(screen.queryByRole('complementary')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Меню раздела' }));
    const drawer = screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Казначейство' });
    await user.click(within(drawer).getByRole('link', { name: 'Платёжный календарь' }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(window.location.hash).toBe('#kazna/calendar'));
    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.queryByRole('dialog')).not.toBeInTheDocument());
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Фильтры' }));
    const sheet = screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Фильтры' });
    expect(within(sheet).getByLabelText('Период')).toBeInTheDocument();
    await user.click(within(sheet).getByRole('button', { name: 'Готово' }));
    expect(screen.queryByRole('dialog')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('tablet: the side menu collapses and expands', async () => {
    setViewport(1024);
    mockApi(shellRoutes());
    const { user } = renderWithProviders(<Harness />, { hash: '#kazna/svod' });
    const toggle = await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'Меню раздела' });
    expect(toggle).toHaveAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true');
    expect(screen.getByRole('complementary', { name: 'Казначейство' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    await user.click(toggle);
    expect(toggle).toHaveAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
    expect(screen.queryByRole('complementary')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    act(() => setViewport(1440));
    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: 'Меню раздела' })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole('complementary', { name: 'Казначейство' })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('has no axe violations on desktop', async () => {
    mockApi(shellRoutes());
    const { container } = renderWithProviders(<Harness />, { hash: '#kazna/svod?br=2' });
    await screen.findByText('Раздел в разработке');
    await expectNoAxeViolations(container);
  });
});
```

```ts
import type { MenuOut, MenuPageOut } from '../api/types';
import { t, type MessageKey } from '../i18n';
import type { IconName } from '../ui/icons';

/** Backend Section enum'i (TZ 5). Ikonkalar va tavsiflar — prototip SECT jadvalidan. */
export const SECTION_IDS = ['obzor', 'kazna', 'kredity', 'finmodel', 'zakupki', 'sklad', 'proizvodstvo', 'investicii', 'kadry'] as const;

interface SectionMeta {
  icon: IconName;
  titleKey: MessageKey;
  descKey: MessageKey;
}

const SECTION_META: Record<string, SectionMeta> = {
  obzor: { icon: 'grid', titleKey: 'section.obzor.title', descKey: 'section.obzor.desc' },
  kazna: { icon: 'bank', titleKey: 'section.kazna.title', descKey: 'section.kazna.desc' },
  kredity: { icon: 'card', titleKey: 'section.kredity.title', descKey: 'section.kredity.desc' },
  finmodel: { icon: 'net', titleKey: 'section.finmodel.title', descKey: 'section.finmodel.desc' },
  zakupki: { icon: 'cart', titleKey: 'section.zakupki.title', descKey: 'section.zakupki.desc' },
  sklad: { icon: 'box', titleKey: 'section.sklad.title', descKey: 'section.sklad.desc' },
  proizvodstvo: { icon: 'bolt', titleKey: 'section.proizvodstvo.title', descKey: 'section.proizvodstvo.desc' },
  investicii: { icon: 'target', titleKey: 'section.investicii.title', descKey: 'section.investicii.desc' },
  kadry: { icon: 'people', titleKey: 'section.kadry.title', descKey: 'section.kadry.desc' },
};

const PAGE_ICONS: Record<string, IconName> = {
  svod: 'grid',
  vnimanie: 'alert',
  postup: 'in',
  rasxod: 'out',
  excel: 'table',
  platezhi: 'pct',
  calendar: 'cal',
  dogovory: 'doc',
  celevye: 'target',
  reestr: 'list',
  grafik: 'cal',
  dohody: 'coins',
  dolg: 'card',
  scenarii: 'alert',
  loty: 'list',
  postavshiki: 'doc',
  ekonomiya: 'pct',
  ostatki: 'list',
  minimum: 'alert',
  nelikvid: 'clock',
  stancii: 'factory',
  realiz: 'coins',
  obekty: 'list',
  istochniki: 'coins',
  riski: 'alert',
  chislennost: 'list',
  fot: 'coins',
  dvizhenie: 'net',
};

/** «Требует внимания» — chap menyuda hisoblagich joyi. */
export const ATTENTION_PAGE = 'vnimanie';

export function isKnownSection(id: string): boolean {
  return Object.hasOwn(SECTION_META, id);
}

export function sectionIcon(id: string): IconName {
  return isKnownSection(id) ? SECTION_META[id].icon : 'grid';
}

export function sectionTitle(id: string): string {
  return isKnownSection(id) ? t(SECTION_META[id].titleKey) : id;
}

export function sectionDesc(id: string): string {
  return isKnownSection(id) ? t(SECTION_META[id].descKey) : '';
}

export function pageIcon(id: string): IconName {
  return Object.hasOwn(PAGE_ICONS, id) ? PAGE_ICONS[id] : 'list';
}

export interface FlatSection {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  group: string | null;
  pages: MenuPageOut[];
}

export function flattenMenu(menu: MenuOut): FlatSection[] {
  return menu.groups.flatMap((group) =>
    group.sections.map((section) => ({ id: section.id, title: section.title, group: group.title ?? null, pages: section.pages })),
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/shell/useMediaQuery.ts`:
```ts
import { useCallback, useSyncExternalStore } from 'react';

export const MOBILE_QUERY = '(max-width: 767.98px)';
export const TABLET_QUERY = '(min-width: 768px) and (max-width: 1279.98px)';

export function useMediaQuery(query: string): boolean {
  const subscribe = useCallback(
    (onChange: () => void) => {
      const list = window.matchMedia(query);
      list.addEventListener('change', onChange);
      return () => list.removeEventListener('change', onChange);
    },
    [query],
  );
  return useSyncExternalStore(
    subscribe,
    () => window.matchMedia(query).matches,
    () => false,
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/sections/registry.ts`:
```ts
import { lazy, type ComponentType, type LazyExoticComponent } from 'react';
import type { FilterState } from '../shell/useFilters';

/** Bo'lim sahifasi: src/sections/<bo'lim>/<sahifa>.tsx, default eksport. Reyestrni tahrirlash shart emas. */
export interface SectionPageProps {
  section: string;
  page: string;
  filters: FilterState;
}

export interface SectionPageModule {
  default: ComponentType<SectionPageProps>;
}

export type SectionPageLoader = () => Promise<SectionPageModule>;
export type SectionPageComponent = LazyExoticComponent<ComponentType<SectionPageProps>>;

export function createRegistry(modules: Record<string, SectionPageLoader>) {
  const cache = new Map<string, SectionPageComponent>();
  return function resolve(section: string, page: string): SectionPageComponent | null {
    const key = `./${section}/${page}.tsx`;
    const loader = modules[key];
    if (!loader) return null;
    let component = cache.get(key);
    if (!component) {
      component = lazy(loader);
      cache.set(key, component);
    }
    return component;
  };
}

export const resolveSectionPage = createRegistry(
  import.meta.glob<SectionPageModule>(['./*/*.tsx', '!./*/*.test.tsx']),
);
```

`frontend/src/shell/useFilters.ts`:
```ts
import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo } from 'react';
import type { BranchOut, MeOut, PeriodOut } from '../api/types';
import { t } from '../i18n';
import { useBranches, usePeriods } from '../meta/api';
import { buildHash, type SectionRoute } from '../router/hash';
import { navigate } from '../router/store';

export interface FilterState {
  br: number | null;
  per: string;
}

export interface FiltersApi {
  state: FilterState;
  branches: BranchOut[];
  periods: PeriodOut[];
  defaultPeriod: string;
  locked: boolean;
  ready: boolean;
  error: unknown;
  retry(): void;
  branchLabel: string;
  periodLabel: string;
  setBranch(br: number | null): void;
  setPeriod(code: string): void;
  resetAll(): void;
  hrefFor(section: string, page: string | null): string;
}

const NO_BRANCHES: BranchOut[] = [];
const NO_PERIODS: PeriodOut[] = [];

/** TZ 6 «Фильтры»: filial va davr URL'da; noto'g'ri qiymatlar `replace` bilan tuzatiladi. */
export function useFilters(route: SectionRoute, me: MeOut): FiltersApi {
  const branchesQuery = useBranches();
  const periodsQuery = usePeriods();
  const branches = branchesQuery.data ?? NO_BRANCHES;
  const periods = periodsQuery.data?.items ?? NO_PERIODS;
  const defaultPeriod = periodsQuery.data?.default ?? 'ytd';
  const ready = branchesQuery.isSuccess && periodsQuery.isSuccess;
  const lockedBranch = me.branch_ids !== null && me.branch_ids.length === 1 ? (me.branch_ids[0] ?? null) : null;

  const br = lockedBranch ?? (route.br !== null && branches.some((b) => b.id === route.br) ? route.br : null);
  const per = route.per !== null && periods.some((p) => p.code === route.per) ? route.per : defaultPeriod;
  const urlPer = per === defaultPeriod ? null : per;

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!ready) return;
    if (route.br !== br || route.per !== urlPer) navigate({ ...route, br, per: urlPer }, { replace: true });
  }, [ready, route, br, urlPer]);

  const go = useCallback(
    (next: { br: number | null; per: string | null }) => navigate({ ...route, br: lockedBranch ?? next.br, per: next.per }),
    [route, lockedBranch],
  );

  const retry = useCallback(() => {
    if (branchesQuery.isError) void branchesQuery.refetch();
    if (periodsQuery.isError) void periodsQuery.refetch();
  }, [branchesQuery, periodsQuery]);

  return useMemo<FiltersApi>(
    () => ({
      state: { br, per },
      branches,
      periods,
      defaultPeriod,
      locked: lockedBranch !== null,
      ready,
      error: branchesQuery.error ?? periodsQuery.error,
      retry,
      branchLabel: br === null ? t('filters.allBranches') : (branches.find((b) => b.id === br)?.name ?? String(br)),
      periodLabel: periods.find((p) => p.code === per)?.label ?? per,
      setBranch: (next) => go({ br: next, per: urlPer }),
      setPeriod: (code) => go({ br, per: code === defaultPeriod ? null : code }),
      resetAll: () => go({ br: null, per: null }),
      hrefFor: (section, page) => buildHash({ kind: 'section', section, page, br, per: urlPer }),
    }),
    [br, per, urlPer, branches, periods, defaultPeriod, lockedBranch, ready, branchesQuery.error, periodsQuery.error, retry, go],
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/shell/FilterControls.tsx`:
```tsx
import { t } from '../i18n';
import { Select } from '../ui/Select';
import type { FiltersApi } from './useFilters';

export function FilterControls({ filters }: { filters: FiltersApi }) {
  const branchOptions = [
    { value: 'all', label: t('filters.allBranches') },
    ...filters.branches.map((b) => ({ value: String(b.id), label: b.name })),
  ];
  const periodOptions = filters.periods.map((p) => ({ value: p.code, label: p.label }));
  return (
    <>
      <Select
        label={t('filters.branch')}
        value={filters.state.br === null ? 'all' : String(filters.state.br)}
        options={branchOptions}
        disabled={filters.locked || !filters.ready}
        icon={filters.locked ? 'lock' : undefined}
        hint={filters.locked ? t('filters.lockedHint') : undefined}
        onChange={(value) => filters.setBranch(value === 'all' ? null : Number(value))}
      />
      <Select
        label={t('filters.period')}
        value={filters.state.per}
        options={periodOptions}
        disabled={!filters.ready}
        onChange={filters.setPeriod}
      />
    </>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/shell/FilterChips.tsx`:
```tsx
import { t } from '../i18n';
import { Button } from '../ui/Button';
import { Chip } from '../ui/Chip';
import type { FiltersApi } from './useFilters';

export function activeFilterCount(filters: FiltersApi): number {
  return (filters.state.br !== null ? 1 : 0) + (filters.state.per !== filters.defaultPeriod ? 1 : 0);
}

/** Sarlavha ostidagi faol filtrlar: × bilan, 2+ olib tashlanadigan bo'lsa «Сбросить всё» (prototip). */
export function FilterChips({ filters }: { filters: FiltersApi }) {
  if (!filters.ready) return null;
  const chips: Array<{ key: string; label: string; value: string; onRemove?: () => void }> = [];
  if (filters.state.br !== null) {
    chips.push({
      key: 'br',
      label: t('filters.branch'),
      value: filters.branchLabel,
      onRemove: filters.locked ? undefined : () => filters.setBranch(null),
    });
  }
  if (filters.state.per !== filters.defaultPeriod) {
    chips.push({ key: 'per', label: t('filters.period'), value: filters.periodLabel, onRemove: () => filters.setPeriod(filters.defaultPeriod) });
  }
  if (chips.length === 0) return null;
  const removable = chips.filter((chip) => chip.onRemove).length;
  return (
    <div className="chips" role="group" aria-label={t('filters.active')}>
      {chips.map((chip) => (
        <Chip key={chip.key} label={chip.label} value={chip.value} onRemove={chip.onRemove} />
      ))}
      {removable > 1 ? (
        <Button variant="secondary" className="chips__reset" onClick={filters.resetAll}>
          {t('filters.resetAll')}
        </Button>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { forwardRef, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import type { MenuOut } from '../api/types';
import { t } from '../i18n';
import { buildHash } from '../router/hash';
import { SectionNav, type NavQuery } from './SectionNav';

export function LogoMark({ className }: { className?: string }) {
  return (
    <svg className={className} viewBox="0 0 140 140" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false">
      <circle cx="70" cy="70" r="62" fill="none" stroke="#FFFFFF" strokeWidth="8" />
      <circle cx="70" cy="70" r="51" fill="#EEF4FB" />
      <path d="M70 24C70 24 42 58 42 80a28 28 0 0 0 56 0C98 58 70 24 70 24Z" fill="#1565C0" />
      <rect x="56" y="84" width="7" height="14" rx="1.5" fill="#FFFFFF" />
      <rect x="66.5" y="75" width="7" height="23" rx="1.5" fill="#FFFFFF" />
      <rect x="77" y="66" width="7" height="32" rx="1.5" fill="#FFFFFF" />
    </svg>
  );
}

export interface TopBarProps {
  menu: MenuOut;
  activeSection: string | null;
  navQuery?: NavQuery;
  tools?: ReactNode;
}

export const TopBar = forwardRef<HTMLElement, TopBarProps>(function TopBar({ menu, activeSection, navQuery, tools }, ref) {
  return (
    <header ref={ref} className="topbar">
      <a className="logo" href={buildHash({ kind: 'home' })} aria-label={t('shell.logoLabel')}>
        <LogoMark className="logo__mark" />
        <span className="logo__text">
          <b className="logo__word">
            UGE<span>KAZNA</span>
          </b>
          <span className="logo__tag">{t('app.tagline')}</span>
        </span>
      </a>
      <SectionNav menu={menu} activeSection={activeSection} navQuery={navQuery} />
      {tools ? <div className="tools">{tools}</div> : null}
    </header>
  );
});
```

`frontend/src/shell/SectionNav.tsx`:
```tsx
import { Fragment, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import type { MenuOut } from '../api/types';
import { t } from '../i18n';
import { buildHash } from '../router/hash';
import { Icon } from '../ui/icons';
import { sectionIcon } from './sectionMeta';

export interface NavQuery {
  br: number | null;
  per: string | null;
}

/** TZ 6: 9 bo'lim guruhlar bilan (Обзор · Финансы · Обеспечение · Производство · Персонал), filtrlar saqlanadi. */
export function SectionNav({ menu, activeSection, navQuery }: { menu: MenuOut; activeSection: string | null; navQuery?: NavQuery }) {
  const listRef = useRef<HTMLUListElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    listRef.current
      ?.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[aria-current="page"]')
      ?.scrollIntoView({ block: 'nearest', inline: 'center' });
  }, [activeSection]);

  return (
    <nav className="sections-nav" aria-label={t('shell.sections')}>
      <ul ref={listRef} className="sections">
        {menu.groups.map((group, index) => (
          <Fragment key={group.title ?? `group-${index}`}>
            {index > 0 ? <li className="sep" aria-hidden="true" /> : null}
            {group.sections.map((section) => (
              <li key={section.id}>
                <a
                  className="sec"
                  href={buildHash({
                    kind: 'section',
                    section: section.id,
                    page: section.pages[0]?.id ?? null,
                    br: navQuery?.br ?? null,
                    per: navQuery?.per ?? null,
                  })}
                  aria-current={section.id === activeSection ? 'page' : undefined}
                >
                  <Icon name={sectionIcon(section.id)} />
                  <span>{section.title}</span>
                </a>
              </li>
            ))}
          </Fragment>
        ))}
      </ul>
    </nav>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/shell/SideMenu.tsx`:
```tsx
import { t } from '../i18n';
import { Icon, type IconName } from '../ui/icons';

export interface NavItem {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  icon: IconName;
  href: string;
  count?: number;
}

export interface SideModel {
  title: string;
  desc: string;
  icon: IconName;
  items: NavItem[];
  activeId: string | null;
}

export function SideMenu({ model, id, hidden, onNavigate }: { model: SideModel; id?: string; hidden?: boolean; onNavigate?(): void }) {
  return (
    <aside id={id} className="side" aria-label={model.title} hidden={hidden}>
      <div className="side-h">
        <span className="ic">
          <Icon name={model.icon} />
        </span>
        <div>
          <b>{model.title}</b>
          {model.desc ? <span>{model.desc}</span> : null}
        </div>
      </div>
      <ul className="pages">
        {model.items.map((item) => (
          <li key={item.id}>
            <a href={item.href} aria-current={item.id === model.activeId ? 'page' : undefined} onClick={onNavigate}>
              <Icon name={item.icon} />
              <span className="pages__label">{item.label}</span>
              {item.count ? (
                <span className="cnt" aria-label={t('shell.attentionCount', { count: item.count })}>
                  {item.count}
                </span>
              ) : null}
            </a>
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
    </aside>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/shell/AppFrame.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useCallback, useEffect, useLayoutEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import type { MenuOut } from '../api/types';
import { t } from '../i18n';
import { cx } from '../ui/cx';
import { Drawer } from '../ui/Drawer';
import { FrameContext, type FrameState } from './FrameContext';
import type { NavQuery } from './SectionNav';
import { SideMenu, type SideModel } from './SideMenu';
import { TopBar } from './TopBar';
import { MOBILE_QUERY, TABLET_QUERY, useMediaQuery } from './useMediaQuery';
import './shell.css';

export interface AppFrameProps {
  menu: MenuOut;
  activeSection: string | null;
  side: SideModel | null;
  navQuery?: NavQuery;
  tools?: ReactNode;
  children: ReactNode;
}

/** Spec 7.5: ≥1280 — to'liq; 768–1279 — yig'iladigan chap panel; <768 — chap menyu Drawer'da. */
export function AppFrame({ menu, activeSection, side, navQuery, tools, children }: AppFrameProps) {
  const isMobile = useMediaQuery(MOBILE_QUERY);
  const isTablet = useMediaQuery(TABLET_QUERY);
  const [drawerOpen, setDrawerOpen] = useState(false);
  const [collapsed, setCollapsed] = useState(false);
  const topRef = useRef<HTMLElement>(null);
  const hasSide = side !== null;
  const sideVisible = hasSide && !isMobile && !(isTablet && collapsed);
  const activeId = side?.activeId ?? null;

  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    const element = topRef.current;
    if (!element || typeof ResizeObserver === 'undefined') return undefined;
    const apply = () => document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--top-offset', `${element.offsetHeight}px`);
    apply();
    const observer = new ResizeObserver(apply);
    observer.observe(element);
    return () => observer.disconnect();
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    setDrawerOpen(false);
  }, [activeId, activeSection]);

  const toggleSide = useCallback(() => {
    if (isMobile) setDrawerOpen(true);
    else setCollapsed((value) => !value);
  }, [isMobile]);

  const frame = useMemo<FrameState>(
    () => ({ isMobile, isTablet, hasSide, sideVisible, toggleSide }),
    [isMobile, isTablet, hasSide, sideVisible, toggleSide],
  );

  return (
    <FrameContext.Provider value={frame}>
      <button type="button" className="skip" onClick={() => document.getElementById('main')?.focus()}>
        {t('common.skipToContent')}
      </button>
      <TopBar ref={topRef} menu={menu} activeSection={activeSection} navQuery={navQuery} tools={tools} />
      <div className={cx('layout', !sideVisible && 'layout--full')}>
        {side && !isMobile ? <SideMenu model={side} id="side" hidden={!sideVisible} /> : null}
        <main id="main" className="main" tabIndex={-1}>
          {children}
        </main>
      </div>
      {side && isMobile ? (
        <Drawer open={drawerOpen} onClose={() => setDrawerOpen(false)} title={side.title}>
          <SideMenu model={side} onNavigate={() => window.setTimeout(() => setDrawerOpen(false), 0)} />
        </Drawer>
      ) : null}
    </FrameContext.Provider>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/shell/PageHeader.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useState, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import { t } from '../i18n';
import { BottomSheet } from '../ui/BottomSheet';
import { Button } from '../ui/Button';
import { useFrame } from './FrameContext';

export interface PageHeaderProps {
  crumb: string;
  title: string;
  sub?: string;
  filters?: ReactNode;
  filterCount?: number;
}

export function PageHeader({ crumb, title, sub, filters, filterCount = 0 }: PageHeaderProps) {
  const frame = useFrame();
  const [sheetOpen, setSheetOpen] = useState(false);
  const showMenuButton = frame.hasSide && (frame.isMobile || frame.isTablet);
  return (
    <div className="head">
      <div className="head__titles">
        {showMenuButton ? (
          <Button
            variant="secondary"
            icon="panel"
            className="head__menu"
            aria-expanded={frame.isMobile ? undefined : frame.sideVisible}
            aria-controls={frame.isMobile ? undefined : 'side'}
            onClick={frame.toggleSide}
          >
            {t('shell.sectionMenu')}
          </Button>
        ) : null}
        <div className="crumb">{crumb}</div>
        <h1>{title}</h1>
        {sub ? <p>{sub}</p> : null}
      </div>
      {filters && frame.isMobile ? (
        <>
          <Button variant="secondary" icon="filter" onClick={() => setSheetOpen(true)}>
            {filterCount > 0 ? `${t('filters.open')} (${filterCount})` : t('filters.open')}
          </Button>
          <BottomSheet
            open={sheetOpen}
            onClose={() => setSheetOpen(false)}
            title={t('filters.title')}
            footer={
              <Button block onClick={() => setSheetOpen(false)}>
                {t('filters.done')}
              </Button>
            }
          >
            <div className="filters filters--sheet">{filters}</div>
          </BottomSheet>
        </>
      ) : null}
      {filters && !frame.isMobile ? <div className="filters">{filters}</div> : null}
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
import type { MenuOut } from '../api/types';
import { useDocumentTitle } from '../app/useDocumentTitle';
import { t } from '../i18n';
import { navigate } from '../router/store';
import { Button } from '../ui/Button';
import { Card } from '../ui/Card';
import { EmptyState } from '../ui/EmptyState';
import { AppFrame } from './AppFrame';

export function StatusScreen({ kind, menu, tools }: { kind: 'noaccess' | 'notfound'; menu: MenuOut; tools?: ReactNode }) {
  const title = kind === 'noaccess' ? t('status.noaccess.title') : t('status.notfound.title');
  useDocumentTitle(title);
  return (
    <AppFrame menu={menu} activeSection={null} side={null} tools={tools}>
      <Card>
        <EmptyState
          icon={kind === 'noaccess' ? 'lock' : 'search'}
          title={title}
          text={kind === 'noaccess' ? t('status.noaccess.text') : t('status.notfound.text')}
          action={
            <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => navigate({ kind: 'home' })}>
              {t('status.home')}
            </Button>
          }
        />
      </Card>
    </AppFrame>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { useEffect } from 'react';
import type { MenuOut } from '../api/types';
import { navigate } from '../router/store';
import { flattenMenu } from './sectionMeta';
import { ShellSkeleton } from './ShellSkeleton';

/** `#` → rolga ruxsat etilgan birinchi bo'limning birinchi sahifasi. */
export function HomeRedirect({ menu }: { menu: MenuOut }) {
  useEffect(() => {
    const first = flattenMenu(menu)[0];
    if (first) {
      navigate({ kind: 'section', section: first.id, page: first.pages[0]?.id ?? null, br: null, per: null }, { replace: true });
    } else {
      navigate({ kind: 'noaccess' }, { replace: true });
    }
  }, [menu]);
  return <ShellSkeleton />;
}
```

`frontend/src/shell/SectionScreen.tsx`:
```tsx
import { Suspense, useEffect, useMemo, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import type { MeOut, MenuOut } from '../api/types';
import { useDocumentTitle } from '../app/useDocumentTitle';
import { t } from '../i18n';
import type { SectionRoute } from '../router/hash';
import { navigate } from '../router/store';
import { resolveSectionPage } from '../sections/registry';
import { SectionPlaceholder } from '../sections/SectionPlaceholder';
import { ErrorBanner } from '../ui/ErrorBanner';
import { PageSkeleton } from '../ui/Skeleton';
import { AppFrame } from './AppFrame';
import { activeFilterCount, FilterChips } from './FilterChips';
import { FilterControls } from './FilterControls';
import { PageHeader } from './PageHeader';
import { flattenMenu, isKnownSection, pageIcon, sectionDesc, sectionIcon } from './sectionMeta';
import type { SideModel } from './SideMenu';
import { StatusScreen } from './StatusScreen';
import { useFilters } from './useFilters';

export interface SectionScreenProps {
  me: MeOut;
  menu: MenuOut;
  route: SectionRoute;
  tools?: ReactNode;
}

export function SectionScreen({ me, menu, route, tools }: SectionScreenProps) {
  const sections = useMemo(() => flattenMenu(menu), [menu]);
  const section = sections.find((s) => s.id === route.section);
  const page = section ? (section.pages.find((p) => p.id === route.page) ?? section.pages[0]) : undefined;
  const filters = useFilters(route, me);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (section && page && route.page !== page.id) navigate({ ...route, page: page.id }, { replace: true });
  }, [section, page, route]);

  useDocumentTitle(section && page ? `${page.title} | ${section.title}` : null);

  if (!section || !page) {
    return <StatusScreen kind={isKnownSection(route.section) ? 'noaccess' : 'notfound'} menu={menu} tools={tools} />;
  }

  const title = page.id === 'svod' ? t('shell.svodTitle', { section: section.title }) : page.title;
  const side: SideModel = {
    title: section.title,
    desc: sectionDesc(section.id),
    icon: sectionIcon(section.id),
    activeId: page.id,
    items: section.pages.map((p) => ({ id: p.id, label: p.title, icon: pageIcon(p.id), href: filters.hrefFor(section.id, p.id) })),
  };
  const Page = resolveSectionPage(section.id, page.id);
  const navQuery = { br: filters.state.br, per: filters.state.per === filters.defaultPeriod ? null : filters.state.per };

  return (
    <AppFrame menu={menu} activeSection={section.id} side={side} navQuery={navQuery} tools={tools}>
      <PageHeader
        crumb={section.title}
        title={title}
        sub={filters.ready ? `${filters.branchLabel}, ${filters.periodLabel.toLowerCase()}` : undefined}
        filters={<FilterControls filters={filters} />}
        filterCount={activeFilterCount(filters)}
      />
      <FilterChips filters={filters} />
      <div id="view" className="view">
        {filters.error && !filters.ready ? (
          <ErrorBanner error={filters.error} title={t('filters.loadFailed')} onRetry={filters.retry} />
        ) : !filters.ready ? (
          <PageSkeleton />
        ) : Page ? (
          <Suspense fallback={<PageSkeleton />}>
            <Page section={section.id} page={page.id} filters={filters.state} />
          </Suspense>
        ) : (
          <SectionPlaceholder title={page.title} />
        )}
      </div>
    </AppFrame>
  );
}
```

```css
.skip {
  position: absolute;
  top: -10rem;
  left: 1rem;
  z-index: 100;
  padding: 0.6rem 1rem;
  border: 2px solid var(--focus);
  border-radius: 0.5rem;
  background: var(--card);
  color: var(--brand);
  font-weight: 600;
}
.skip:focus { top: 1rem; }

.topbar {
  position: sticky;
  top: 0;
  z-index: 30;
  display: flex;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 0.6rem 1rem;
  min-height: var(--top-h);
  padding: 0.5rem 1.5rem;
  color: #fff;
  background: linear-gradient(120deg, var(--top) 0%, var(--top-end) 100%);
  box-shadow: 0 2px 0 var(--top2), 0 10px 30px -22px rgba(11, 60, 140, 0.9);
}
.topbar :focus-visible { outline-color: var(--focus-on-dark); }
.topbar--skel { position: static; }

.logo {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 0.8rem;

}

.logo__text { display: flex; flex-direction: column; }

.logo__word span { color: #7fe3f0; }
.logo__tag { margin-top: 0.25rem; font-size: 0.76rem; opacity: 0.9; }

.sections-nav { display: flex; flex: 1; min-width: 0; }

}

.sec:hover { background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.12); color: #fff; }
.sec[aria-current='page'] { background: #fff; color: var(--brand); box-shadow: 0 8px 20px -12px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.6); }

.tools { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.4rem; flex: none; margin-left: auto; }

.layout { display: grid; grid-template-columns: var(--side-w) minmax(0, 1fr); align-items: start; }
.layout--full { grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr); }

}

@keyframes ugk-side-in { from { opacity: 0; transform: translateX(-10px); } }
.side-h { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.65rem; margin-bottom: 0.6rem; padding: 0 0.5rem 1rem; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--line); }

.main { display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 1rem; min-width: 0; padding: 1.4rem 1.8rem 2.2rem; outline: none; }
.head { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; align-items: flex-end; justify-content: space-between; gap: 0.8rem 1.5rem; }

}
@media (max-width: 767.98px) {
  .topbar { --top-h: 3.8rem; gap: 0.4rem 0.6rem; padding: 0.4rem 0.6rem 0.3rem 0.8rem; }
  .logo { margin-right: 0; padding-right: 0; border-right: 0; }

  .logo__text { display: none; }
  .sections-nav { order: 3; flex-basis: 100%; }

  .sections::-webkit-scrollbar { display: none; }
  .sec { height: 2.75rem; padding: 0 0.8rem; font-size: 0.98rem; }
  .sec .icon { display: none; }
  .tools { gap: 0.3rem; }
  .main { padding: 1rem; }
  .head h1 { font-size: 1.6rem; }
  .filters .field { width: 100%; min-width: 0; }
  .pages a { min-height: 44px; }
}
@media print {
  .skip, .topbar, .side, .filters, .head__menu, .chips button, .chips__reset { display: none !important; }
  .layout { grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr); }
  .main { padding: 0; }
  .head h1 { background: none; color: var(--ink); -webkit-text-fill-color: var(--ink); }
}
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { BRANCHES, ME_ADMIN, ME_AUDITOR, MENU_AUDITOR, MENU_FULL, PERIODS } from '../test/fixtures';
import { buildPaletteItems, filterPalette } from './paletteItems';

const base = {
  menu: MENU_FULL,
  permissions: ME_ADMIN.permissions,
  branches: BRANCHES,
  periods: PERIODS.items,
  defaultPeriod: 'ytd',
  locked: false,
  route: { kind: 'section', section: 'kazna', page: 'platezhi', br: 2, per: 'q1' } as const,
};

describe('buildPaletteItems', () => {
  it('lists pages with current filters, admin pages, account, branches and periods', () => {
    const items = buildPaletteItems(base);
    expect(items.find((i) => i.label === 'Кредиты: Реестр кредитов')?.target).toEqual({
      kind: 'section', section: 'kredity', page: 'reestr', br: 2, per: 'q1',
    });
    expect(items.find((i) => i.label === 'Андижанская ГЭС')?.target).toEqual({
      kind: 'section', section: 'kazna', page: 'platezhi', br: 4, per: 'q1',
    });
    expect(items.find((i) => i.label === 'Все филиалы')?.target).toMatchObject({ br: null });
    expect(items.find((i) => i.label === 'Январь – сентябрь 2026')?.target).toMatchObject({ per: null, br: 2 });
    expect(items.find((i) => i.label === 'Администрирование: Журнал аудита')?.target).toEqual({ kind: 'admin', page: 'audit' });
    expect(items.find((i) => i.label === 'Мои сеансы')?.target).toEqual({ kind: 'sessions' });
  });

  it('hides branches for a locked user and admin pages without permissions', () => {
    const items = buildPaletteItems({ ...base, menu: MENU_AUDITOR, permissions: ['section:obzor:view'], locked: true, route: { kind: 'admin', page: null } });
    expect(items.some((i) => i.kind === 'branch')).toBe(false);
    expect(items.some((i) => i.kind === 'admin')).toBe(false);
    expect(items.find((i) => i.kind === 'period')?.target).toMatchObject({ kind: 'section', section: 'obzor', page: 'svod' });
  });

  it('shows only the audit page to internal control', () => {
    const items = buildPaletteItems({ ...base, menu: MENU_AUDITOR, permissions: ME_AUDITOR.permissions });
    expect(items.filter((i) => i.kind === 'admin').map((i) => i.label)).toEqual(['Администрирование: Журнал аудита']);
  });
});

it('filters case-insensitively and treats ё as е', () => {
  const items = buildPaletteItems(base);
  expect(filterPalette(items, 'ПЛАТЁЖНЫЙ').map((i) => i.label)).toEqual(['Казначейство: Платёжный календарь']);
  expect(filterPalette(items, '').length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(50);
});
```

```tsx
import { screen, waitFor, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import type { MeOut, MenuOut } from '../api/types';
import { useRoute } from '../router/store';
import { expectNoAxeViolations } from '../test/axe';

function Harness({ me = ME_ADMIN, menu = MENU_FULL }: { me?: MeOut; menu?: MenuOut }) {
  const route = useRoute();
  if (route.kind !== 'section') return <p>route:{route.kind}</p>;
  return <SectionScreen me={me} menu={menu} route={route} tools={<ShellTools me={me} menu={menu} route={route} />} />;
}

describe('command palette', () => {

    mockApi(shellRoutes());

    await screen.findByText('Раздел в разработке');
    await user.keyboard('{Control>}k{/Control}');
    const dialog = screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Быстрый переход' });
    const input = within(dialog).getByRole('combobox');
    expect(input).toHaveFocus();
    await user.type(input, 'календ');
    expect(within(dialog).getAllByRole('option')[0]).toHaveTextContent('Казначейство: Платёжный календарь');
    await user.keyboard('{Enter}');
    expect(window.location.hash).toBe('#kazna/calendar?br=2');
    expect(screen.queryByRole('dialog')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('applies a branch from the palette', async () => {
    mockApi(shellRoutes());
    const { user } = renderWithProviders(<Harness />, { hash: '#obzor/svod' });
    await screen.findByText('Раздел в разработке');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Поиск' }));
    await user.type(screen.getByRole('combobox'), 'Андижан');

    expect(window.location.hash).toBe('#obzor/svod?br=4');
  });

  it('hides branch items for a locked user', async () => {
    mockApi(shellRoutes(ME_BRANCH, MENU_BRANCH));
    const { user } = renderWithProviders(<Harness me={ME_BRANCH} menu={MENU_BRANCH} />, { hash: '#obzor/svod' });
    await screen.findByText('Раздел в разработке');

    await user.type(screen.getByRole('combobox'), 'Каскад');
    expect(screen.getByText('Ничего не найдено')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});

describe('toolbar', () => {
  it('copies the link with filters and prints', async () => {
    mockApi(shellRoutes());

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('menuitem', { name: 'Администрирование' }));
    expect(window.location.hash).toBe('#admin/users');
  });
});

describe('section hotkeys', () => {

});
```

```ts
import type { MessageKey } from '../i18n';
import type { IconName } from '../ui/icons';

export type AdminPageId = 'users' | 'roles' | 'alerts' | 'finmodel' | 'etl' | 'builds' | 'audit' | 'keys';

export interface AdminPageDef {
  id: AdminPageId;
  perm: string;
  titleKey: MessageKey;
  icon: IconName;
}

/** Spec 7.6 admin sahifalari va ularni ochuvchi ruxsatlar (tekshiruv baribir serverda). */
export const ADMIN_PAGES: readonly AdminPageDef[] = [
  { id: 'users', perm: 'admin:users', titleKey: 'admin.page.users', icon: 'people' },
  { id: 'roles', perm: 'admin:roles', titleKey: 'admin.page.roles', icon: 'shield' },
  { id: 'alerts', perm: 'admin:settings', titleKey: 'admin.page.alerts', icon: 'alert' },
  { id: 'finmodel', perm: 'admin:settings', titleKey: 'admin.page.finmodel', icon: 'net' },
  { id: 'etl', perm: 'admin:etl', titleKey: 'admin.page.etl', icon: 'refresh' },
  { id: 'builds', perm: 'admin:etl', titleKey: 'admin.page.builds', icon: 'layers' },
  { id: 'audit', perm: 'audit:read', titleKey: 'admin.page.audit', icon: 'history' },
  { id: 'keys', perm: 'admin:keys', titleKey: 'admin.page.keys', icon: 'key' },
];

export function allowedAdminPages(permissions: readonly string[]): AdminPageDef[] {
  return ADMIN_PAGES.filter((page) => permissions.includes(page.perm));
}
```

`frontend/src/shell/useSectionHotkeys.ts`:
```ts
import { useEffect } from 'react';

export function isTypingTarget(target: EventTarget | null): boolean {
  if (!(target instanceof HTMLElement)) return false;
  if (target.isContentEditable) return true;
  return ['INPUT', 'TEXTAREA', 'SELECT'].includes(target.tagName) || target.getAttribute('role') === 'combobox';
}

/** TZ 6: ← → bo'limlar orasida, agar fokus kiritish maydonida, menyuda yoki skroll jadvalda bo'lmasa. */
export function useSectionHotkeys(ids: readonly string[], active: string | null, go: (id: string) => void): void {
  useEffect(() => {
    const onKeyDown = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (event.key !== 'ArrowLeft' && event.key !== 'ArrowRight') return;
      if (event.defaultPrevented || event.altKey || event.ctrlKey || event.metaKey || event.shiftKey) return;
      if (isTypingTarget(event.target) || document.body.classList.contains('no-scroll')) return;
      if (event.target instanceof HTMLElement && event.target.closest('[role="tablist"],[role="menu"],[role="listbox"],.tw')) return;
      if (ids.length === 0) return;
      const index = active ? ids.indexOf(active) : -1;
      const step = event.key === 'ArrowRight' ? 1 : -1;
      const nextIndex = index < 0 ? (step > 0 ? 0 : ids.length - 1) : (index + step + ids.length) % ids.length;
      const next = ids[nextIndex];
      if (!next) return;
      event.preventDefault();
      go(next);
    };
    window.addEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
  }, [ids, active, go]);
}
```

`frontend/src/shell/paletteItems.ts`:
```ts
import { allowedAdminPages } from '../admin/access';
import type { BranchOut, MenuOut, PeriodOut } from '../api/types';
import { t } from '../i18n';
import type { Route, SectionRoute } from '../router/hash';
import { flattenMenu } from './sectionMeta';

export type PaletteKind = 'section' | 'admin' | 'account' | 'branch' | 'period';

export interface PaletteItem {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  kind: PaletteKind;
  kindLabel: string;
  target: Route;
}

export interface PaletteInput {
  menu: MenuOut;
  permissions: readonly string[];
  branches: readonly BranchOut[];
  periods: readonly PeriodOut[];
  defaultPeriod: string;
  locked: boolean;
  route: Route;
}

/** TZ 6 «Быстрый переход»: bo'limlar, sahifalar, filiallar va davrlar (+ admin/akkaunt). */
export function buildPaletteItems(input: PaletteInput): PaletteItem[] {
  const sections = flattenMenu(input.menu);
  const current: SectionRoute | null = input.route.kind === 'section' ? input.route : null;
  const first = sections[0];
  const base: SectionRoute | null =
    current ?? (first ? { kind: 'section', section: first.id, page: first.pages[0]?.id ?? null, br: null, per: null } : null);
  const items: PaletteItem[] = [];

  for (const section of sections) {
    for (const page of section.pages) {
      items.push({
        id: `page:${section.id}/${page.id}`,
        label: `${section.title}: ${page.title}`,
        kind: 'section',
        kindLabel: t('palette.kind.section'),
        target: { kind: 'section', section: section.id, page: page.id, br: current?.br ?? null, per: current?.per ?? null },
      });
    }
  }
  for (const page of allowedAdminPages(input.permissions)) {
    items.push({
      id: `admin:${page.id}`,
      label: `${t('admin.title')}: ${t(page.titleKey)}`,
      kind: 'admin',
      kindLabel: t('palette.kind.admin'),
      target: { kind: 'admin', page: page.id },
    });
  }
  items.push(
    { id: 'account:sessions', label: t('user.sessions'), kind: 'account', kindLabel: t('palette.kind.account'), target: { kind: 'sessions' } },
    { id: 'account:password', label: t('user.password'), kind: 'account', kindLabel: t('palette.kind.account'), target: { kind: 'password' } },
  );
  if (base) {
    if (!input.locked) {
      items.push({ id: 'branch:all', label: t('filters.allBranches'), kind: 'branch', kindLabel: t('palette.kind.branch'), target: { ...base, br: null } });
      for (const branch of input.branches) {
        items.push({ id: `branch:${branch.id}`, label: branch.name, kind: 'branch', kindLabel: t('palette.kind.branch'), target: { ...base, br: branch.id } });
      }
    }
    for (const period of input.periods) {
      items.push({
        id: `period:${period.code}`,
        label: period.label,
        kind: 'period',
        kindLabel: t('palette.kind.period'),
        target: { ...base, per: period.code === input.defaultPeriod ? null : period.code },
      });
    }
  }
  return items;
}

function normalize(value: string): string {
  return value.toLocaleLowerCase('ru').replace(/ё/g, 'е').trim();
}

export function filterPalette(items: readonly PaletteItem[], query: string, limit = 50): PaletteItem[] {
  const q = normalize(query);
  const found = q ? items.filter((item) => normalize(item.label).includes(q)) : items;
  return found.slice(0, limit);
}
```

`frontend/src/shell/CommandPalette.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useEffect, useId, useMemo, useRef, useState, type KeyboardEvent } from 'react';
import type { MeOut, MenuOut } from '../api/types';
import { t } from '../i18n';
import { useBranches, usePeriods } from '../meta/api';
import type { Route } from '../router/hash';
import { navigate } from '../router/store';
import { cx } from '../ui/cx';
import { Overlay } from '../ui/Overlay';
import { buildPaletteItems, filterPalette, type PaletteItem } from './paletteItems';

export interface CommandPaletteProps {
  open: boolean;
  onClose(): void;
  me: MeOut;
  menu: MenuOut;
  route: Route;
}

export function CommandPalette({ open, onClose, me, menu, route }: CommandPaletteProps) {
  const branches = useBranches();
  const periods = usePeriods();
  const [query, setQuery] = useState('');
  const [active, setActive] = useState(0);
  const inputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  const listId = useId();

  useEffect(() => {
    if (open) {
      setQuery('');
      setActive(0);
    }
  }, [open]);

  const items = useMemo(
    () =>
      buildPaletteItems({
        menu,
        permissions: me.permissions,
        branches: branches.data ?? [],
        periods: periods.data?.items ?? [],
        defaultPeriod: periods.data?.default ?? 'ytd',
        locked: me.branch_ids !== null && me.branch_ids.length === 1,
        route,
      }),
    [menu, me, branches.data, periods.data, route],
  );
  const results = useMemo(() => filterPalette(items, query), [items, query]);

  const choose = (item: PaletteItem) => {
    onClose();
    navigate(item.target);
  };

  const onKeyDown = (event: KeyboardEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    if (event.key === 'ArrowDown') {
      event.preventDefault();
      setActive((i) => Math.min(i + 1, results.length - 1));
    } else if (event.key === 'ArrowUp') {
      event.preventDefault();
      setActive((i) => Math.max(i - 1, 0));
    } else if (event.key === 'Enter') {
      const item = results[active];
      if (item) {
        event.preventDefault();
        choose(item);
      }
    }
  };

  return (
    <Overlay open={open} onClose={onClose} variant="palette" label={t('palette.label')} initialFocusRef={inputRef}>
      <input
        ref={inputRef}
        className="palette__input"
        role="combobox"
        aria-expanded="true"
        aria-controls={listId}
        aria-autocomplete="list"
        aria-activedescendant={results[active] ? `${listId}-${active}` : undefined}
        aria-label={t('palette.label')}
        placeholder={t('palette.placeholder')}
        autoComplete="off"
        value={query}
        onChange={(e) => {
          setQuery(e.target.value);
          setActive(0);
        }}
        onKeyDown={onKeyDown}
      />
      <ul id={listId} role="listbox" aria-label={t('palette.results')} className="palette__list">
        {results.length === 0 ? (
          <li className="palette__empty" role="presentation">
            {t('palette.empty')}
          </li>
        ) : (
          results.map((item, index) => (
            <li
              key={item.id}
              id={`${listId}-${index}`}
              role="option"
              tabIndex={-1}
              aria-selected={index === active}
              className={cx('palette__opt', index === active && 'is-active')}
              onMouseDown={(e) => {
                e.preventDefault();
                choose(item);
              }}
              onMouseEnter={() => setActive(index)}
            >
              <span>{item.label}</span>
              <span className="palette__kind">{item.kindLabel}</span>
            </li>
          ))
        )}
      </ul>
      <div className="palette__hint" aria-hidden="true">
        <span>
          <kbd>↑</kbd> <kbd>↓</kbd> {t('palette.hintMove')}
        </span>
        <span>
          <kbd>Enter</kbd> {t('palette.hintOpen')}
        </span>
        <span>
          <kbd>Esc</kbd> {t('palette.hintClose')}
        </span>
      </div>
    </Overlay>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { t } from '../i18n';
import { formatDateTime } from '../i18n/format';
import { useFreshness } from '../meta/api';
import { cx } from '../ui/cx';
import { Icon } from '../ui/icons';

/** TZ 6 «Данные устарели»: oxirgi yuklash vaqti; chegaradan (6 soat) oshsa — ogohlantirish. */
export function Freshness() {
  const query = useFreshness();
  const data = query.data;
  if (!data) return null;
  const text = data.last_success_at ? t('fresh.at', { time: formatDateTime(data.last_success_at) }) : t('fresh.never');
  return (
    <div
      className={cx('fresh', data.stale && 'fresh--stale')}
      role="status"
      title={data.stale ? t('fresh.staleTitle', { hours: data.max_age_hours }) : undefined}
    >
      <Icon name={data.stale ? 'alert' : 'clock'} />
      <span className="fresh__text">{text}</span>
      {data.stale ? <span className="fresh__warn">{t('fresh.stale')}</span> : null}
    </div>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/shell/UserMenu.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useEffect, useId, useRef, useState, type KeyboardEvent } from 'react';
import { allowedAdminPages } from '../admin/access';
import type { MeOut } from '../api/types';
import { useLogout } from '../auth/api';
import { t } from '../i18n';
import { navigate } from '../router/store';
import { Icon, type IconName } from '../ui/icons';

interface MenuAction {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  icon: IconName;
  run(): void;
}

function initials(fullName: string): string {
  return fullName
    .split(/\s+/)
    .filter(Boolean)
    .slice(0, 2)
    .map((part) => part[0]?.toUpperCase() ?? '')
    .join('');
}

/** WAI-ARIA menu button: Enter/Space/↓ ochadi, ↑↓ Home End, Esc/Tab yopadi. */
export function UserMenu({ me }: { me: MeOut }) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const buttonRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null);
  const menuRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const menuId = useId();
  const logout = useLogout();
  const admin = allowedAdminPages(me.permissions);

  const actions: MenuAction[] = [
    { id: 'sessions', label: t('user.sessions'), icon: 'history', run: () => navigate({ kind: 'sessions' }) },
    { id: 'password', label: t('user.password'), icon: 'key', run: () => navigate({ kind: 'password' }) },
    ...(admin[0] ? [{ id: 'admin', label: t('user.admin'), icon: 'shield' as const, run: () => navigate({ kind: 'admin', page: admin[0]?.id ?? null }) }] : []),
    { id: 'logout', label: t('user.logout'), icon: 'logout', run: () => logout.mutate() },
  ];

  const items = () => Array.from(menuRef.current?.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('[role="menuitem"]') ?? []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return undefined;
    menuRef.current?.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[role="menuitem"]')?.focus();
    const onPointer = (event: MouseEvent) => {
      const target = event.target as Node;
      if (!menuRef.current?.contains(target) && !buttonRef.current?.contains(target)) setOpen(false);
    };
    document.addEventListener('mousedown', onPointer);
    return () => document.removeEventListener('mousedown', onPointer);
  }, [open]);

  const close = (focusButton: boolean) => {
    setOpen(false);
    if (focusButton) buttonRef.current?.focus();
  };

  const onMenuKeyDown = (event: KeyboardEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
    const list = items();
    const index = list.indexOf(document.activeElement as HTMLElement);
    if (event.key === 'ArrowDown') list[(index + 1) % list.length]?.focus();
    else if (event.key === 'ArrowUp') list[(index - 1 + list.length) % list.length]?.focus();
    else if (event.key === 'Home') list[0]?.focus();
    else if (event.key === 'End') list[list.length - 1]?.focus();
    else if (event.key === 'Escape') close(true);
    else if (event.key === 'Tab') close(false);
    else return;
    if (event.key !== 'Tab') event.preventDefault();
  };

  return (
    <div className="usermenu">
      <button
        ref={buttonRef}
        type="button"
        className="tool usermenu__btn"
        aria-haspopup="menu"
        aria-expanded={open}
        aria-controls={open ? menuId : undefined}
        aria-label={t('user.menu', { name: me.full_name })}
        onClick={() => setOpen((value) => !value)}
        onKeyDown={(event) => {
          if (event.key === 'ArrowDown') {
            event.preventDefault();
            setOpen(true);
          }
        }}
      >
        <span className="avatar" aria-hidden="true">
          {initials(me.full_name)}
        </span>
        <span className="usermenu__name" aria-hidden="true">
          {me.full_name}
        </span>
        <Icon name="chevronDown" />
      </button>
      {open ? (
        <div
          ref={menuRef}
          id={menuId}
          role="menu"
          tabIndex={-1}
          aria-label={me.full_name}
          className="usermenu__list"
          onKeyDown={onMenuKeyDown}
        >
          <div className="usermenu__head" aria-hidden="true">
            <b>{me.full_name}</b>
            <span>{me.username}</span>
          </div>
          {actions.map((action) => (
            <button
              key={action.id}
              type="button"
              role="menuitem"
              tabIndex={-1}
              className="usermenu__item"
              onClick={() => {
                close(false);
                action.run();
              }}
            >
              <Icon name={action.icon} />
              {action.label}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/shell/ShellTools.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import type { MeOut, MenuOut } from '../api/types';
import { t } from '../i18n';
import type { Route } from '../router/hash';
import { navigate } from '../router/store';
import { Icon } from '../ui/icons';
import { CommandPalette } from './CommandPalette';
import { Freshness } from './Freshness';
import { flattenMenu } from './sectionMeta';
import { UserMenu } from './UserMenu';
import { useSectionHotkeys } from './useSectionHotkeys';

export interface ShellToolsProps {
  me: MeOut;
  menu: MenuOut;
  route: Route;
}

export function ShellTools({ me, menu, route }: ShellToolsProps) {
  const [paletteOpen, setPaletteOpen] = useState(false);
  const [copy, setCopy] = useState<'idle' | 'ok' | 'fail'>('idle');
  const sections = useMemo(() => flattenMenu(menu), [menu]);
  const ids = useMemo(() => sections.map((s) => s.id), [sections]);
  const activeSection = route.kind === 'section' ? route.section : null;

  const goSection = useCallback(
    (id: string) => {
      const target = sections.find((s) => s.id === id);
      if (!target) return;
      const fromNav = document.activeElement instanceof HTMLElement && document.activeElement.closest('.sections') !== null;
      navigate({
        kind: 'section',
        section: id,
        page: target.pages[0]?.id ?? null,
        br: route.kind === 'section' ? route.br : null,
        per: route.kind === 'section' ? route.per : null,
      });
      if (fromNav) {
        window.requestAnimationFrame(() => document.querySelector<HTMLElement>('.sections [aria-current="page"]')?.focus());
      }
    },
    [sections, route],
  );

  useSectionHotkeys(ids, activeSection, goSection);

  useEffect(() => {
    const onKeyDown = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if ((event.ctrlKey || event.metaKey) && event.key.toLowerCase() === 'k') {
        event.preventDefault();
        setPaletteOpen(true);
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
  }, []);

  const copyLink = async () => {
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(window.location.href);
      setCopy('ok');
    } catch {
      setCopy('fail');
    }
    window.setTimeout(() => setCopy('idle'), 1500);
  };

  const linkLabel = copy === 'ok' ? t('common.copied') : copy === 'fail' ? t('common.copyFailed') : t('tools.link');

  return (
    <>
      <button type="button" className="tool" title={t('tools.searchTitle')} aria-keyshortcuts="Control+K Meta+K" onClick={() => setPaletteOpen(true)}>
        <Icon name="search" />
        <span className="tool__label">{t('tools.search')}</span>
      </button>
      <button type="button" className="tool" title={t('tools.linkTitle')} onClick={() => void copyLink()}>
        <Icon name="link" />
        <span className="tool__label" aria-live="polite">
          {linkLabel}
        </span>
      </button>
      <button type="button" className="tool" title={t('tools.printTitle')} onClick={() => window.print()}>
        <Icon name="print" />
        <span className="tool__label">{t('tools.print')}</span>
      </button>
      <Freshness />
      <UserMenu me={me} />
      <CommandPalette open={paletteOpen} onClose={() => setPaletteOpen(false)} me={me} menu={menu} route={route} />
    </>
  );
}
```

```css
kbd { padding: 0 0.35rem; border: 1px solid var(--line); border-bottom-width: 2px; border-radius: 0.3rem; background: var(--soft); font: inherit; font-size: 0.8rem; }

@media (max-width: 1279.98px) {

}
@media print {
```

`frontend/src/auth/passwordRules.test.ts`:
```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { characterClasses, checkPassword, passwordProblemText } from './passwordRules';

describe('password rules', () => {
  it('counts character classes including Cyrillic letters', () => {
    expect(characterClasses('abc')).toBe(1);
    expect(characterClasses('Abc1')).toBe(3);
    expect(characterClasses('Пароль-2026')).toBe(4);
  });

  it('checks length, classes and the username', () => {
    expect(checkPassword('Short-1', 'admin')).toEqual({ length: false, classes: true, noUsername: true });
    expect(checkPassword('Yangi-Mustahkam-77', 'admin')).toEqual({ length: true, classes: true, noUsername: true });
    expect(checkPassword('MyAdmin-Parol-2026', 'admin').noUsername).toBe(false);
  });

  it('maps server policy codes to Russian texts', () => {
    expect(passwordProblemText('password_common')).toBe('Пароль слишком распространён или содержит запрещённое слово');
    expect(passwordProblemText('something_new')).toBe('Пароль не соответствует политике безопасности');
  });
});
```

```tsx
import { screen, waitFor } from '@testing-library/react';
import { useEffect, useRef, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { expectNoAxeViolations } from '../test/axe';
import { ME_ADMIN } from '../test/fixtures';
import { apiError, json, mockApi } from '../test/mockApi';
import { renderWithProviders } from '../test/render';
import { useAuthFlow, type Challenge } from './AuthFlow';
import { LoginPage } from './LoginPage';
import { MfaVerifyPage } from './MfaVerifyPage';
import { PasswordChangePage } from './PasswordChangePage';

const TOKEN = 't'.repeat(32);

function WithChallenge({ step = 'mfa_verify', returnTo = null, children }: { step?: Challenge['step']; returnTo?: string | null; children: ReactNode }) {
  const flow = useAuthFlow();
  const started = useRef(false);
  const [ready, setReady] = useState(false);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (started.current) return;
    started.current = true;
    flow.begin({ mfaToken: TOKEN, step, username: 'admin', returnTo });
    setReady(true);
  }, [flow, step, returnTo]);
  return ready ? <>{children}</> : null;
}

describe('LoginPage', () => {
  it('validates empty fields without calling the API', async () => {
    const server = mockApi({});
    const { user } = renderWithProviders(<LoginPage next={null} />, { hash: '#login' });
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Войти' }));
    expect(screen.getByText('Введите логин')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Введите пароль')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(server.calls).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it('shows the server message on bad credentials and clears the password', async () => {
    mockApi({ 'POST /auth/login': apiError(401, 'invalid_credentials', 'Неверный логин или пароль') });
    const { user } = renderWithProviders(<LoginPage next={null} />, { hash: '#login' });
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Логин'), 'admin');
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Пароль'), 'wrong-password');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Войти' }));
    expect(await screen.findByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent('Неверный логин или пароль');
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Пароль')).toHaveValue('');
    expect(window.location.hash).toBe('#login');
  });

  it('shows retry time on 429', async () => {
    mockApi({
      'POST /auth/login': apiError(429, 'rate_limited', 'Слишком много запросов', { retry_after: 42 }, { 'Retry-After': '42' }),
    });
    const { user } = renderWithProviders(<LoginPage next={null} />, { hash: '#login' });
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Логин'), 'admin');
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Пароль'), 'x');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Войти' }));
    expect(await screen.findByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent('Слишком много запросов. Повторите через 42 с.');
    expect(window.location.hash).toBe('#login');
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Пароль')).toHaveValue('');
  });

  it('trims the username and goes to 2FA enrollment when required', async () => {
    const server = mockApi({ 'POST /auth/login': json({ next: 'mfa_enroll', mfa_token: TOKEN }) });
    const { user } = renderWithProviders(<LoginPage next="kazna/svod" />, { hash: '#login?next=kazna%2Fsvod' });
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Логин'), '  Admin  ');
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Пароль'), 'Secret-Parol-1');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Войти' }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(window.location.hash).toBe('#2fa/setup'));
    expect(server.calls[0]?.body).toEqual({ username: 'Admin', password: 'Secret-Parol-1' });
  });

  it('has no axe violations', async () => {
    const { container } = renderWithProviders(<LoginPage next={null} />, { hash: '#login' });
    await expectNoAxeViolations(container);
  });
});

describe('MfaVerifyPage', () => {
  it('sends the user back to login without a challenge', async () => {
    renderWithProviders(<MfaVerifyPage />, { hash: '#2fa' });
    await waitFor(() => expect(window.location.hash).toBe('#login'));
  });

  it('submits the code only once', async () => {
    let release: () => void = () => undefined;
    const gate = new Promise<void>((resolve) => {
      release = resolve;
    });
    const server = mockApi({
      'POST /auth/2fa/verify': async () => {
        await gate;
        return json(ME_ADMIN);
      },
    });
    const { user } = renderWithProviders(
      <WithChallenge returnTo="kazna/svod?br=3">
        <MfaVerifyPage />
      </WithChallenge>,
      { hash: '#2fa' },
    );
    const input = await screen.findByLabelText('Код из приложения');
    expect(input).toHaveFocus();
    await user.type(input, '123456');
    await user.keyboard('{Enter}');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Подтвердить' }));
    release();
    await waitFor(() => expect(window.location.hash).toBe('#kazna/svod?br=3'));
    expect(server.calls.filter((c) => c.path === '/auth/2fa/verify')).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(server.calls[0]?.body).toEqual({ mfa_token: TOKEN, code: '123456' });
  });

  it('shows an error on a wrong code, clears it and allows another try', async () => {
    let attempts = 0;
    mockApi({
      'POST /auth/2fa/verify': () => {
        attempts += 1;
        return attempts === 1 ? apiError(401, 'invalid_mfa_code', 'Неверный код подтверждения') : json(ME_ADMIN);
      },
    });
    const { user } = renderWithProviders(
      <WithChallenge>
        <MfaVerifyPage />
      </WithChallenge>,
      { hash: '#2fa' },
    );
    const input = await screen.findByLabelText('Код из приложения');
    await user.type(input, '111111');
    expect(await screen.findByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent('Неверный код подтверждения');
    expect(input).toHaveValue('');
    await user.type(input, '222222');
    // returnTo yo'q → bosh sahifa ('#' → location.hash === '')
    await waitFor(() => expect(window.location.hash).toBe(''));
  });

  it('accepts a backup code', async () => {
    const server = mockApi({ 'POST /auth/2fa/verify': json({ ...ME_ADMIN, must_change_password: true }) });
    const { user } = renderWithProviders(
      <WithChallenge>
        <MfaVerifyPage />
      </WithChallenge>,
      { hash: '#2fa' },
    );
    await user.click(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'Использовать резервный код' }));
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Резервный код'), 'abcd-efgh-jkmn');
    await user.keyboard('{Enter}');
    await waitFor(() => expect(window.location.hash).toBe('#password'));
    expect(server.calls[0]?.body).toEqual({ mfa_token: TOKEN, code: 'ABCD-EFGH-JKMN' });
  });

  it('offers a new sign-in when the challenge expired', async () => {
    mockApi({ 'POST /auth/2fa/verify': apiError(401, 'mfa_expired', 'Время подтверждения истекло, войдите снова') });
    const { user } = renderWithProviders(
      <WithChallenge returnTo="obzor/svod">
        <MfaVerifyPage />
      </WithChallenge>,
      { hash: '#2fa' },
    );
    await user.type(await screen.findByLabelText('Код из приложения'), '123456');
    expect(await screen.findByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent('Время подтверждения истекло');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Войти заново' }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(window.location.hash).toBe('#login?next=obzor%2Fsvod'));
  });
});

describe('PasswordChangePage', () => {
  const forcedMe = { ...ME_ADMIN, must_change_password: true };

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Сохранить пароль' }));

});
```

```tsx
import { render, screen, waitFor } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { ME_ADMIN, shellRoutes } from '../test/fixtures';
import { apiError, json, mockApi, type MockHandler } from '../test/mockApi';
import { App } from './App';

const TOKEN = 't'.repeat(32);

function withMe(handler: MockHandler): Record<string, MockHandler> {
  return { ...shellRoutes(), 'GET /auth/me': handler };
}

describe('App routing', () => {
  it('redirects an anonymous visitor to login with the return path', async () => {
    mockApi(withMe(apiError(401, 'unauthenticated', 'Требуется вход в систему')));
    window.history.replaceState(null, '', '#kazna/svod?br=3');
    render(<App />);
    await waitFor(() => expect(window.location.hash).toBe('#login?next=kazna%2Fsvod%3Fbr%3D3'));
    expect(await screen.findByRole('heading', { name: 'Вход в систему' })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('returns to the original page with filters after re-login', async () => {
    let signedIn = false;
    mockApi({
      ...withMe(() => (signedIn ? json(ME_ADMIN) : apiError(401, 'session_expired', 'Сеанс завершён, войдите снова'))),
      'POST /auth/login': json({ next: 'mfa_verify', mfa_token: TOKEN }),
      'POST /auth/2fa/verify': () => {
        signedIn = true;
        return json(ME_ADMIN);
      },
    });
    window.history.replaceState(null, '', '#kazna/svod?br=3');
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    render(<App />);
    await user.type(await screen.findByLabelText('Логин'), 'admin');
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Пароль'), 'Secret-Parol-1');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Войти' }));
    await user.type(await screen.findByLabelText('Код из приложения'), '123456');
    expect(await screen.findByRole('heading', { level: 1, name: 'Казначейство: сводка' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(window.location.hash).toBe('#kazna/svod?br=3');
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Меню пользователя: Азимов Бахтиёр' })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('forces the password change before anything else', async () => {
    let changed = false;
    mockApi({
      ...withMe(() => json({ ...ME_ADMIN, must_change_password: !changed })),
      'POST /auth/password/change': () => {
        changed = true;
        return { status: 204 };
      },
    });
    window.history.replaceState(null, '', '#obzor/svod');
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    render(<App />);
    await waitFor(() => expect(window.location.hash).toBe('#password'));
    await user.type(await screen.findByLabelText('Текущий пароль'), 'Temp-Parol-2026!');
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Новый пароль'), 'Yangi-Mustahkam-77');
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Повторите новый пароль'), 'Yangi-Mustahkam-77');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Сохранить пароль' }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(window.location.hash).toBe('#obzor/svod'));
    expect(await screen.findByRole('heading', { level: 1, name: 'Обзор: сводка' })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('sends password_change_required from any request to the password page', async () => {
    mockApi({
      ...withMe(json(ME_ADMIN)),
      'GET /meta/menu': apiError(403, 'password_change_required', 'Необходимо сменить пароль'),
    });
    window.history.replaceState(null, '', '#obzor/svod');
    render(<App />);
    await waitFor(() => expect(window.location.hash).toBe('#password'));
    expect(await screen.findByRole('heading', { name: 'Смена пароля' })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('opens the first allowed page from the root', async () => {
    mockApi(withMe(json(ME_ADMIN)));
    window.history.replaceState(null, '', '/');
    render(<App />);
    await waitFor(() => expect(window.location.hash).toBe('#obzor/svod'));
  });
});
```

`frontend/src/auth/passwordRules.ts`:
```ts
import { t } from '../i18n';

/** Backend siyosati (1-reja Global Constraints): ≥12 belgi, 4 turdan kamida 3 tasi, loginsiz. */
export const MIN_PASSWORD_LENGTH = 12;

export interface PasswordRules {
  length: boolean;
  classes: boolean;
  noUsername: boolean;
}

export function characterClasses(password: string): number {
  let count = 0;
  if (/\p{Ll}/u.test(password)) count += 1;
  if (/\p{Lu}/u.test(password)) count += 1;
  if (/\p{Nd}/u.test(password)) count += 1;
  if (/[^\p{L}\p{Nd}]/u.test(password)) count += 1;
  return count;
}

export function checkPassword(password: string, username: string): PasswordRules {
  const folded = password.toLocaleLowerCase();
  return {
    length: password.length >= MIN_PASSWORD_LENGTH,
    classes: characterClasses(password) >= 3,
    noUsername: !username || !folded.includes(username.toLocaleLowerCase()),
  };
}

export function passwordProblemText(code: string): string {
  switch (code) {
    case 'password_too_short':
      return t('password.problem.too_short');
    case 'password_too_simple':
      return t('password.problem.too_simple');
    case 'password_contains_username':
      return t('password.problem.contains_username');
    case 'password_common':
      return t('password.problem.common');
    case 'password_reused':
      return t('password.problem.reused');
    default:
      return t('password.problem.unknown');
  }
}
```

`frontend/src/auth/errors.ts`:
```ts
import { ApiError } from '../api/client';
import { t } from '../i18n';

/** Auth formalari uchun: 429 — kutish vaqti, qolgani — server xabari (ruscha) yoki umumiy matn. */
export function authErrorText(error: unknown): string {
  if (error instanceof ApiError) {
    if (error.status === 429) return t('error.rateLimited', { seconds: error.retryAfter ?? 60 });
    return error.message;
  }
  return t('error.generic');
}
```

```tsx
import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
```
