`frontend/src/auth/LoginPage.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react';
import { useDocumentTitle } from '../app/useDocumentTitle';
import { t } from '../i18n';
import { navigate } from '../router/store';
import { Button } from '../ui/Button';
import { PasswordInput, TextInput } from '../ui/Field';
import { useLogin } from './api';
import { AuthAlert, AuthLayout } from './AuthLayout';
import { useAuthFlow } from './AuthFlow';
import { authErrorText } from './errors';

export function LoginPage({ next }: { next: string | null }) {
  const login = useLogin();
  const flow = useAuthFlow();
  const [username, setUsername] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [errors, setErrors] = useState<{ username?: string; password?: string }>({});
  useDocumentTitle(t('login.docTitle'));

  const onSubmit = (event: FormEvent) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    const name = username.trim();
    const found: { username?: string; password?: string } = {};
    if (!name) found.username = t('login.required.username');
    if (!password) found.password = t('login.required.password');
    setErrors(found);
    if (found.username || found.password) return;
    login.mutate(
      { username: name, password },
      {
        onSuccess: (result) => {
          flow.begin({ mfaToken: result.mfa_token, step: result.next, username: name, returnTo: next });
          setPassword('');
          navigate({ kind: result.next === 'mfa_enroll' ? 'mfaEnroll' : 'mfa' });
        },
        onError: () => setPassword(''),
      },
    );
  };

  return (
    <AuthLayout>
      <h1>{t('login.title')}</h1>
      <p className="auth__lead">{t('login.lead')}</p>
      <form className="auth__form" noValidate onSubmit={onSubmit}>
        {login.error ? <AuthAlert message={authErrorText(login.error)} /> : null}
        <TextInput
          label={t('login.username')}
          name="username"
          autoComplete="username"
          autoCapitalize="none"
          spellCheck={false}
          value={username}
          error={errors.username}
          onChange={(e) => setUsername(e.target.value)}
        />
        <PasswordInput
          label={t('login.password')}
          name="password"
          autoComplete="current-password"
          value={password}
          error={errors.password}
          onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
        />
        <Button type="submit" block loading={login.isPending}>
          {t('login.submit')}
        </Button>
      </form>
      <p className="auth__note">{t('login.forgot')}</p>
    </AuthLayout>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/auth/MfaVerifyPage.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useEffect, useRef, useState, type FormEvent } from 'react';
import { isApiError } from '../api/client';
import { useDocumentTitle } from '../app/useDocumentTitle';
import { t } from '../i18n';
import { navigate } from '../router/store';
import { Button } from '../ui/Button';
import { OtpInput } from '../ui/OtpInput';
import { useVerifyMfa } from './api';
import { AuthAlert, AuthLayout } from './AuthLayout';
import { useAuthFlow } from './AuthFlow';
import { destinationAfterSignIn } from './destination';
import { authErrorText } from './errors';

type Mode = 'totp' | 'backup';

function isComplete(code: string, mode: Mode): boolean {
  return mode === 'totp' ? /^\d{6}$/.test(code) : /^[A-Z0-9]{4}-?[A-Z0-9]{4}-?[A-Z0-9]{4}$/.test(code);
}

export function MfaVerifyPage() {
  const flow = useAuthFlow();
  const verify = useVerifyMfa();
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<Mode>('totp');
  const [code, setCode] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const inFlight = useRef(false);
  /** true — sahifa o'zi boshqa joyga o'tkazdi; challenge tozalangani uchun login'ga qaytarmaymiz. */
  const leaving = useRef(false);
  const challenge = flow.challenge;
  const valid = challenge !== null && challenge.step === 'mfa_verify';
  useDocumentTitle(t('mfa.docTitle'));

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!valid && !leaving.current) navigate({ kind: 'login', next: flow.returnTo }, { replace: true });
  }, [valid, flow.returnTo]);

  if (!challenge || !valid) return null;

  /** Bitta so'rov: avto-yuborish, Enter va tugma bir vaqtda kelsa ham (TOTP replay himoyasi). */
  const submit = (value: string) => {
    if (inFlight.current) return;
    if (!isComplete(value, mode)) {
      setError(t('mfa.codeIncomplete'));
      return;
    }
    inFlight.current = true;
    setError(null);
    verify.mutate(
      { mfa_token: challenge.mfaToken, code: value },
      {
        onSuccess: (me) => {
          const returnTo = challenge.returnTo;
          leaving.current = true;
          navigate(destinationAfterSignIn(me, returnTo), { replace: true });
          flow.finish();
        },
        onError: (err) => {
          setCode('');
          setError(authErrorText(err));
        },
        onSettled: () => {
          inFlight.current = false;
        },
      },
    );
  };

  const restart = () => {
    const returnTo = challenge.returnTo;
    leaving.current = true;
    navigate({ kind: 'login', next: returnTo });
    flow.clear();
  };

  const onSubmit = (event: FormEvent) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    submit(code);
  };

  const expired = isApiError(verify.error, 401, 'mfa_expired');

  return (
    <AuthLayout>
      <h1>{t('mfa.title')}</h1>
      <p className="auth__lead">{mode === 'totp' ? t('mfa.lead') : t('mfa.leadBackup')}</p>
      <form className="auth__form" noValidate onSubmit={onSubmit}>
        {error ? <AuthAlert message={error} /> : null}
        <OtpInput
          key={mode}
          label={mode === 'totp' ? t('mfa.codeLabel') : t('mfa.backupLabel')}
          mode={mode}
          value={code}
          onChange={setCode}
          onComplete={submit}
          focusOnMount
        />
        <Button type="submit" block loading={verify.isPending} disabled={expired}>
          {t('mfa.submit')}
        </Button>
      </form>
      <div className="auth__links">
        {!expired ? (
          <button
            type="button"
            className="linkbtn"
            onClick={() => {
              setMode((m) => (m === 'totp' ? 'backup' : 'totp'));
              setCode('');
              setError(null);
            }}
          >
            {mode === 'totp' ? t('mfa.useBackup') : t('mfa.useTotp')}
          </button>
        ) : null}
        <button type="button" className="linkbtn" onClick={restart}>
          {t('mfa.backToLogin')}
        </button>
      </div>
    </AuthLayout>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/auth/PasswordChangePage.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react';
import { isApiError } from '../api/client';
import type { MeOut } from '../api/types';
import { useDocumentTitle } from '../app/useDocumentTitle';
import { t } from '../i18n';
import { navigate } from '../router/store';
import { Button } from '../ui/Button';
import { FormError, PasswordInput } from '../ui/Field';
import { Icon } from '../ui/icons';
import { useToast } from '../ui/Toast';
import { useChangePassword, useLogout } from './api';
import { AuthLayout } from './AuthLayout';
import { useAuthFlow } from './AuthFlow';
import { destinationAfterSignIn } from './destination';
import { checkPassword, passwordProblemText } from './passwordRules';

interface Errors {
  current?: string;
  next?: string;
  repeat?: string;
}

function Rule({ id, ok, text }: { id: string; ok: boolean; text: string }) {
  return (
    <li data-rule={id} data-ok={ok}>
      <Icon name={ok ? 'check' : 'info'} />
      <span>{text}</span>
      <span className="visually-hidden">{` — ${ok ? t('password.rule.ok') : t('password.rule.notOk')}`}</span>
    </li>
  );
}

export function PasswordChangePage({ me }: { me: MeOut }) {
  const forced = me.must_change_password;
  const change = useChangePassword();
  const logout = useLogout();
  const flow = useAuthFlow();
  const toast = useToast();
  const [current, setCurrent] = useState('');
  const [next, setNext] = useState('');
  const [repeat, setRepeat] = useState('');
  const [errors, setErrors] = useState<Errors>({});
  const [problems, setProblems] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const rules = checkPassword(next, me.username);
  useDocumentTitle(t('password.docTitle'));

  const onSubmit = (event: FormEvent) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    const found: Errors = {};
    if (!current) found.current = t('field.required');
    if (!rules.length || !rules.classes || !rules.noUsername) found.next = t('password.rulesNotMet');
    else if (next === current) found.next = t('password.sameAsCurrent');
    if (next !== repeat) found.repeat = t('password.mismatch');
    setErrors(found);
    setProblems([]);
    if (found.current || found.next || found.repeat) return;
    change.mutate(
      { current_password: current, new_password: next },
      {
        onSuccess: () => {
          toast.show(t('password.changed'), 'success');
          const returnTo = flow.returnTo;
          flow.clear();
          navigate(destinationAfterSignIn({ ...me, must_change_password: false }, returnTo), { replace: true });
        },
        onError: (error) => {
          if (isApiError(error, 422, 'invalid_current_password')) {
            setErrors({ current: error.message });
          } else if (isApiError(error, 422, 'password_policy')) {
            const raw = error.params.problems;
            const list = Array.isArray(raw) ? raw.filter((p): p is string => typeof p === 'string') : [];
            setProblems(list.length > 0 ? list : ['unknown']);
          }
        },
      },
    );
  };

  const otherError = change.error && !isApiError(change.error, 422) ? change.error : null;

  return (
    <AuthLayout>
      <h1>{forced ? t('password.forcedTitle') : t('password.title')}</h1>
      <p className="auth__lead">{forced ? t('password.forcedLead') : t('password.lead')}</p>
      <form className="auth__form" noValidate onSubmit={onSubmit}>
        {problems.length > 0 ? (
          <div className="form-error" role="alert">
            <Icon name="alert" />
            <div>
              <b>{t('password.policyFailed')}</b>
              <ul className="form-error__list">
                {problems.map((code) => (
                  <li key={code}>{passwordProblemText(code)}</li>
                ))}
              </ul>
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : null}
        <FormError error={otherError} />
        <PasswordInput
          label={t('password.current')}
          autoComplete="current-password"
          value={current}
          error={errors.current}
          onChange={(e) => setCurrent(e.target.value)}
        />
        <PasswordInput
          label={t('password.new')}
          autoComplete="new-password"
          value={next}
          error={errors.next}
          onChange={(e) => setNext(e.target.value)}
        />
        <ul className="rules" aria-label={t('password.rulesTitle')}>
          <Rule id="length" ok={rules.length} text={t('password.rule.length')} />
          <Rule id="classes" ok={rules.classes} text={t('password.rule.classes')} />
          <Rule id="username" ok={next.length > 0 && rules.noUsername} text={t('password.rule.username')} />
          <Rule id="match" ok={next.length > 0 && next === repeat} text={t('password.rule.match')} />
        </ul>
        <PasswordInput
          label={t('password.repeat')}
          autoComplete="new-password"
          value={repeat}
          error={errors.repeat}
          onChange={(e) => setRepeat(e.target.value)}
        />
        <Button type="submit" block loading={change.isPending}>
          {t('password.submit')}
        </Button>
      </form>
      <div className="auth__links">
        {forced ? (
          <button type="button" className="linkbtn" onClick={() => logout.mutate()}>
            {t('user.logout')}
          </button>
        ) : (
          <button type="button" className="linkbtn" onClick={() => navigate({ kind: 'home' })}>
            {t('common.back')}
          </button>
        )}
      </div>
    </AuthLayout>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/app/queryClient.ts`:
```ts
import type { QueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { createQueryClient } from '../api/queryClient';
import { isPublicRoute, parseHash, safeNext } from '../router/hash';
import { currentPath, navigate } from '../router/store';

/** Spec 8 xato siyosatini router bilan bog'laydi. */
export function makeQueryClient(notify: (message: string) => void): QueryClient {
  const client: QueryClient = createQueryClient({
    onUnauthenticated: () => {
      if (isPublicRoute(parseHash(window.location.hash))) return;
      navigate({ kind: 'login', next: safeNext(currentPath()) }, { replace: true });
      queueMicrotask(() => client.clear());
    },
    onPasswordChange: () => navigate({ kind: 'password' }, { replace: true }),
    onForbidden: () => navigate({ kind: 'noaccess' }, { replace: true }),
    notify,
  });
  return client;
}
```

`frontend/src/app/RequireAuth.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useEffect } from 'react';
import { isApiError } from '../api/client';
import type { MeOut, MenuOut } from '../api/types';
import { useMe } from '../auth/api';
import { PasswordChangePage } from '../auth/PasswordChangePage';
import { useMenu } from '../meta/api';
import type { Route } from '../router/hash';
import { navigate } from '../router/store';
import { HomeRedirect } from '../shell/HomeRedirect';
import { SectionScreen } from '../shell/SectionScreen';
import { ShellSkeleton } from '../shell/ShellSkeleton';
import { ShellTools } from '../shell/ShellTools';
import { StatusScreen } from '../shell/StatusScreen';
import { FullPageError } from './FullPageError';

/** Himoyalangan marshrutlar: me → (majburiy parol) → menyu → ekran. Tekshiruv baribir serverda. */
export function RequireAuth({ route }: { route: Route }) {
  const me = useMe();
  const mustChange = me.data?.must_change_password ?? false;
  const menu = useMenu(me.isSuccess && !mustChange);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (mustChange && route.kind !== 'password') navigate({ kind: 'password' }, { replace: true });
  }, [mustChange, route.kind]);

  if (me.isPending) return <ShellSkeleton />;
  if (me.isError) {
    return isApiError(me.error, 401) ? <ShellSkeleton /> : <FullPageError error={me.error} onRetry={() => void me.refetch()} />;
  }
  if (route.kind === 'password') return <PasswordChangePage me={me.data} />;
  if (mustChange || menu.isPending) return <ShellSkeleton />;
  if (menu.isError) return <FullPageError error={menu.error} onRetry={() => void menu.refetch()} />;
  return <Screen me={me.data} menu={menu.data} route={route} />;
}

function Screen({ me, menu, route }: { me: MeOut; menu: MenuOut; route: Route }) {
  const tools = <ShellTools me={me} menu={menu} route={route} />;
  switch (route.kind) {
    case 'home':
      return <HomeRedirect menu={menu} />;
    case 'section':
      return <SectionScreen me={me} menu={menu} route={route} tools={tools} />;
    case 'noaccess':
      return <StatusScreen kind="noaccess" menu={menu} tools={tools} />;
    default:
      return <StatusScreen kind="notfound" menu={menu} tools={tools} />;
  }
}
```

`frontend/src/app/Routes.tsx`:
```tsx
import { LoginPage } from '../auth/LoginPage';
import { MfaVerifyPage } from '../auth/MfaVerifyPage';
import { useRoute } from '../router/store';
import { RequireAuth } from './RequireAuth';

export function Routes() {
  const route = useRoute();
  switch (route.kind) {
    case 'login':
      return <LoginPage next={route.next} />;
    case 'mfa':
      return <MfaVerifyPage />;
    default:
      return <RequireAuth route={route} />;
  }
}
```

`frontend/src/app/App.tsx` (to'liq almashtiring):
```tsx
import { QueryClientProvider } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { useState, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import { AuthFlowProvider } from '../auth/AuthFlow';
import { ToastProvider, useToast } from '../ui/Toast';
import { makeQueryClient } from './queryClient';
import { Routes } from './Routes';

function ClientBoundary({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const toast = useToast();
  const [client] = useState(() => makeQueryClient((message) => toast.show(message, 'error')));
  return <QueryClientProvider client={client}>{children}</QueryClientProvider>;
}

export function App() {
  return (
    <ToastProvider>
      <ClientBoundary>
        <AuthFlowProvider>
          <Routes />
        </AuthFlowProvider>
      </ClientBoundary>
    </ToastProvider>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/auth/enroll.test.tsx`:
```tsx
import { screen, waitFor, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import { StrictMode, useEffect, useRef, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { expectNoAxeViolations } from '../test/axe';
import { ME_ADMIN } from '../test/fixtures';
import { apiError, json, mockApi } from '../test/mockApi';
import { renderWithProviders } from '../test/render';
import { useAuthFlow } from './AuthFlow';
import { backupCodesFilename, backupCodesText } from './BackupCodes';
import { MfaEnrollPage } from './MfaEnrollPage';

const TOKEN = 't'.repeat(32);
const SECRET = 'JBSWY3DPEHPK3PXPJBSWY3DPEHPK3PXP';
const CODES = ['ABCD-EFGH-JKMN', 'BCDE-FGHJ-KMNP', 'CDEF-GHJK-MNPQ', 'DEFG-HJKM-NPQR', 'EFGH-JKMN-PQRS', 'FGHJ-KMNP-QRST', 'GHJK-MNPQ-RSTU', 'HJKM-NPQR-STUV', 'JKMN-PQRS-TUVW', 'KMNP-QRST-UVWX'];

function WithEnrollChallenge({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const flow = useAuthFlow();
  const started = useRef(false);
  const [ready, setReady] = useState(false);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (started.current) return;
    started.current = true;
    flow.begin({ mfaToken: TOKEN, step: 'mfa_enroll', username: 'novikov', returnTo: 'kazna/svod' });
    setReady(true);
  }, [flow]);
  return ready ? <>{children}</> : null;
}

function enrollRoutes() {
  let attempts = 0;
  return mockApi({
    'POST /auth/2fa/enroll/start': json({
      otpauth_uri: `otpauth://totp/UGEKAZNA:novikov?secret=${SECRET}&issuer=UGEKAZNA`,
      qr_svg_data_uri: 'data:image/svg+xml;base64,PHN2Zy8+',
      secret: SECRET,
    }),
    'POST /auth/2fa/enroll/confirm': () => {
      attempts += 1;
      return attempts === 1
        ? apiError(401, 'invalid_mfa_code', 'Неверный код подтверждения')
        : json({ user: { ...ME_ADMIN, username: 'novikov', must_change_password: true }, backup_codes: CODES });
    },
  });
}

describe('MfaEnrollPage', () => {
  it('redirects to login without an enrollment challenge', async () => {
    renderWithProviders(<MfaEnrollPage />, { hash: '#2fa/setup' });
    await waitFor(() => expect(window.location.hash).toBe('#login'));
  });

  it('starts once (even in StrictMode) and shows the QR code and grouped key', async () => {
    const server = enrollRoutes();
    const { user } = renderWithProviders(
      <StrictMode>
        <WithEnrollChallenge>
          <MfaEnrollPage />
        </WithEnrollChallenge>
      </StrictMode>,
      { hash: '#2fa/setup' },
    );
    const qr = await screen.findByRole('img', { name: 'QR-код для подключения приложения-аутентификатора' });
    expect(qr).toHaveAttribute('src', 'data:image/svg+xml;base64,PHN2Zy8+');
    expect(screen.getByTestId('totp-secret')).toHaveTextContent('JBSW Y3DP EHPK 3PXP JBSW Y3DP EHPK 3PXP');
    expect(screen.getByText('Шаг 1 из 2')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(server.calls.filter((c) => c.path === '/auth/2fa/enroll/start')).toHaveLength(1);
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Скопировать' }));
    expect(navigator.clipboard.writeText).toHaveBeenCalledWith(SECRET);
  });

  it('confirms the code, shows backup codes and continues to the password step', async () => {
    const click = vi.spyOn(HTMLAnchorElement.prototype, 'click').mockImplementation(() => undefined);
    const server = enrollRoutes();
    const { user } = renderWithProviders(
      <WithEnrollChallenge>
        <MfaEnrollPage />
      </WithEnrollChallenge>,
      { hash: '#2fa/setup' },
    );
    await screen.findByTestId('totp-secret');
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Код из приложения'), '000000');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Подтвердить' }));
    expect(await screen.findByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent('Неверный код подтверждения');
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Код из приложения'), '123456');
    await user.keyboard('{Enter}');
    expect(await screen.findByRole('heading', { name: 'Резервные коды' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    const list = screen.getByTestId('backup-codes');
    expect(within(list).getAllByRole('listitem')).toHaveLength(10);
    expect(server.calls.find((c) => c.path === '/auth/2fa/enroll/confirm' && (c.body as { code: string }).code === '123456')).toBeTruthy();

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Скачать' }));
    expect(URL.createObjectURL).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
    const anchor = click.mock.contexts[0] as HTMLAnchorElement;
    expect(anchor.download).toBe('ugekazna-backup-codes-novikov.txt');

    const next = screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Продолжить' });
    expect(next).toBeDisabled();
    await user.click(screen.getByLabelText('Я сохранил резервные коды в надёжном месте'));
    expect(next).toBeEnabled();
    await user.click(next);
    await waitFor(() => expect(window.location.hash).toBe('#password'));
  });

  it('has no axe violations on the backup codes step', async () => {
    enrollRoutes();
    const { container, user } = renderWithProviders(
      <WithEnrollChallenge>
        <MfaEnrollPage />
      </WithEnrollChallenge>,
      { hash: '#2fa/setup' },
    );
    await screen.findByTestId('totp-secret');
    await expectNoAxeViolations(container);
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Код из приложения'), '000000');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Подтвердить' }));
    await screen.findByRole('alert');
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Код из приложения'), '123456');
    await user.keyboard('{Enter}');
    await screen.findByTestId('backup-codes');
    await expectNoAxeViolations(container);
  });
});

describe('backup codes file', () => {
  it('lists every code with a header', () => {
    const text = backupCodesText(CODES, 'novikov', new Date('2026-09-24T05:00:00Z'));
    const lines = text.split(String.fromCharCode(13, 10));
    expect(lines[0]).toBe('UGEKAZNA — резервные коды двухфакторной аутентификации');
    expect(lines).toContain('Пользователь: novikov');
    expect(lines).toContain('Создано: 24.09.2026 10:00');
    expect(lines).toContain(' 1. ABCD-EFGH-JKMN');
    expect(lines).toContain('10. KMNP-QRST-UVWX');
    expect(backupCodesFilename('novikov')).toBe('ugekazna-backup-codes-novikov.txt');
  });
});
```

```tsx
import { screen, waitFor, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import { expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { expectNoAxeViolations } from '../test/axe';
import { ME_ADMIN, MENU_FULL, shellRoutes } from '../test/fixtures';
import { json, mockApi } from '../test/mockApi';
import { renderWithProviders } from '../test/render';
import { SessionsScreen } from './SessionsScreen';

const SESSIONS = [
  { id: 'a1', created_at: '2026-09-24T04:00:00Z', last_seen_at: '2026-09-24T05:30:00Z', ua_summary: 'Chrome · Windows', ip: '10.0.0.5', current: true },
  { id: 'b2', created_at: '2026-09-20T04:00:00Z', last_seen_at: '2026-09-21T06:00:00Z', ua_summary: 'Firefox · Linux', ip: null, current: false },
];

it('lists sessions, marks the current one and ends another after confirmation', async () => {
  let list = SESSIONS;
  const server = mockApi({
    ...shellRoutes(),
    'GET /auth/sessions': () => json(list),
    'DELETE /auth/sessions/b2': () => {
      list = SESSIONS.slice(0, 1);
      return { status: 204 };
    },
  });
  const { user } = renderWithProviders(<SessionsScreen me={ME_ADMIN} menu={MENU_FULL} />, { hash: '#account/sessions' });
  const table = await screen.findByRole('table', { name: 'Мои сеансы' });
  const rows = within(table).getAllByRole('row').slice(1);
  expect(rows).toHaveLength(2);
  expect(rows[0]).toHaveTextContent('Chrome · Windows');
  expect(rows[0]).toHaveTextContent('Текущий сеанс');
  expect(within(rows[0] as HTMLElement).queryByRole('button', { name: 'Завершить' })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  expect(rows[1]).toHaveTextContent('—');
  await user.click(within(rows[1] as HTMLElement).getByRole('button', { name: 'Завершить' }));
  const dialog = screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Завершить сеанс?' });
  expect(dialog).toHaveTextContent('Firefox · Linux');
  await user.click(within(dialog).getByRole('button', { name: 'Завершить' }));
  expect(await screen.findByText('Сеанс завершён')).toBeInTheDocument();
  await waitFor(() => expect(within(table).getAllByRole('row')).toHaveLength(2));
  expect(server.calls.some((c) => c.method === 'DELETE' && c.path === '/auth/sessions/b2')).toBe(true);
  expect(screen.getByRole('link', { name: 'Сменить пароль' })).toHaveAttribute('href', '#password');
  await expectNoAxeViolations(document.body);
});
```

`frontend/src/auth/BackupCodes.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { t } from '../i18n';
import { formatDateTime } from '../i18n/format';
import { Button } from '../ui/Button';
import { Checkbox } from '../ui/Field';
import { Icon } from '../ui/icons';
import { useToast } from '../ui/Toast';

const EOL = String.fromCharCode(13, 10);

export function backupCodesFilename(username: string): string {
  return `ugekazna-backup-codes-${username}.txt`;
}

export function backupCodesText(codes: readonly string[], username: string, now: Date): string {
  const lines = [
    t('backup.fileTitle'),
    `${t('backup.fileUser')}: ${username}`,
    `${t('backup.fileCreated')}: ${formatDateTime(now)}`,
    t('backup.fileNote'),
    '',
    ...codes.map((code, index) => `${String(index + 1).padStart(2, ' ')}. ${code}`),
    '',
  ];
  return lines.join(EOL);
}

/** Mapbox/Chargetrip naqshi: ogohlantirish, 10 kod, yuklab olish/nusxalash, "saqladim" belgisi. */
export function BackupCodes({ codes, username, onContinue }: { codes: string[]; username: string; onContinue(): void }) {
  const [saved, setSaved] = useState(false);
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);
  const toast = useToast();

  useEffect(() => {
    if (saved) return undefined;
    const onBeforeUnload = (event: BeforeUnloadEvent) => {
      event.preventDefault();
      event.returnValue = '';
    };
    window.addEventListener('beforeunload', onBeforeUnload);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('beforeunload', onBeforeUnload);
  }, [saved]);

  const text = () => backupCodesText(codes, username, new Date());

  const download = () => {
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([text()], { type: 'text/plain;charset=utf-8' }));
    const anchor = document.createElement('a');
    anchor.href = url;
    anchor.download = backupCodesFilename(username);
    document.body.appendChild(anchor);
    anchor.click();
    anchor.remove();
    window.setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 1000);
  };

  const copy = async () => {
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text());
      setCopied(true);
    } catch {
      toast.show(t('common.copyFailed'), 'error');
    }
  };

  return (
    <>
      <p className="auth__step">{t('enroll.step', { n: 2, total: 2 })}</p>
      <h1>{t('backup.title')}</h1>
      <p className="notice">
        <Icon name="alert" />
        <span>{t('backup.warning')}</span>
      </p>
      <ol className="codes" data-testid="backup-codes" aria-label={t('backup.listLabel')}>
        {codes.map((code) => (
          <li key={code}>{code}</li>
        ))}
      </ol>
      <div className="row-actions">
        <Button variant="secondary" icon="download" onClick={download}>
          {t('backup.download')}
        </Button>
        <Button variant="secondary" icon="copy" onClick={() => void copy()}>
          {copied ? t('common.copied') : t('common.copy')}
        </Button>
      </div>
      <Checkbox label={t('backup.confirm')} checked={saved} onChange={setSaved} />
      <Button block disabled={!saved} onClick={onContinue}>
        {t('backup.continue')}
      </Button>
    </>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/auth/MfaEnrollPage.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useEffect, useRef, useState, type FormEvent } from 'react';
import { isApiError } from '../api/client';
import type { EnrollConfirmOut } from '../api/types';
import { useDocumentTitle } from '../app/useDocumentTitle';
import { t } from '../i18n';
import { navigate } from '../router/store';
import { Button } from '../ui/Button';
import { OtpInput } from '../ui/OtpInput';
import { Skeleton } from '../ui/Skeleton';
import { useEnrollConfirm, useEnrollStart } from './api';
import { AuthAlert, AuthLayout } from './AuthLayout';
import { useAuthFlow } from './AuthFlow';
import { BackupCodes } from './BackupCodes';
import { destinationAfterSignIn } from './destination';
import { authErrorText } from './errors';

function groupSecret(secret: string): string {
  return secret.replace(/(.{4})/g, '$1 ').trim();
}

export function MfaEnrollPage() {
  const flow = useAuthFlow();
  const start = useEnrollStart();
  const confirm = useEnrollConfirm();
  const [code, setCode] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);
  const [result, setResult] = useState<EnrollConfirmOut | null>(null);
  const started = useRef(false);
  const leaving = useRef(false);
  const challenge = flow.challenge;
  const valid = challenge !== null && challenge.step === 'mfa_enroll';
  useDocumentTitle(t('enroll.docTitle'));

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!valid && !leaving.current && result === null) navigate({ kind: 'login', next: flow.returnTo }, { replace: true });
  }, [valid, result, flow.returnTo]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!challenge || !valid || started.current) return;
    started.current = true;
    start.mutate({ mfa_token: challenge.mfaToken });
  }, [challenge, valid, start]);

  if (result) {
    return (
      <AuthLayout wide>
        <BackupCodes
          codes={result.backup_codes}
          username={result.user.username}
          onContinue={() => navigate(destinationAfterSignIn(result.user, flow.returnTo), { replace: true })}
        />
      </AuthLayout>
    );
  }
  if (!challenge || !valid) return null;

  const restart = () => {
    const returnTo = challenge.returnTo;
    leaving.current = true;
    navigate({ kind: 'login', next: returnTo });
    flow.clear();
  };

  const onSubmit = (event: FormEvent) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    if (confirm.isPending) return;
    if (!/^\d{6}$/.test(code)) {
      setError(t('mfa.codeIncomplete'));
      return;
    }
    setError(null);
    confirm.mutate(
      { mfa_token: challenge.mfaToken, code },
      {
        onSuccess: (data) => {
          setResult(data);
          flow.finish();
        },
        onError: (err) => {
          setCode('');
          setError(authErrorText(err));
        },
      },
    );
  };

  const copySecret = async (secret: string) => {
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(secret);
      setCopied(true);
    } catch {
      setCopied(false);
    }
  };

  const expired = isApiError(confirm.error, 401, 'mfa_expired') || isApiError(start.error, 401, 'mfa_expired');
  const enrollment = start.data;

  return (
    <AuthLayout wide>
      <p className="auth__step">{t('enroll.step', { n: 1, total: 2 })}</p>
      <h1>{t('enroll.title')}</h1>
      <p className="auth__lead">{t('enroll.lead')}</p>
      {start.isPending ? <Skeleton variant="card" /> : null}
      {start.error ? <AuthAlert message={authErrorText(start.error)} /> : null}
      {enrollment ? (
        <div className="enroll">
          <img className="enroll__qr" src={enrollment.qr_svg_data_uri} alt={t('enroll.qrAlt')} width={176} height={176} />
          <div className="enroll__body">
            <ol className="enroll__steps">
              <li>{t('enroll.step1')}</li>
              <li>{t('enroll.step2')}</li>
              <li>{t('enroll.step3')}</li>
            </ol>
            <p className="field__label">{t('enroll.manualKey')}</p>
            <div className="secret">
              <code data-testid="totp-secret">{groupSecret(enrollment.secret)}</code>
              <Button variant="secondary" icon="copy" onClick={() => void copySecret(enrollment.secret)}>
                {copied ? t('common.copied') : t('common.copy')}
              </Button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : null}
      <form className="auth__form" noValidate onSubmit={onSubmit}>
        {error ? <AuthAlert message={error} /> : null}
        <OtpInput label={t('mfa.codeLabel')} value={code} onChange={setCode} disabled={!enrollment || expired} />
        <Button type="submit" block loading={confirm.isPending} disabled={!enrollment || expired}>
          {t('enroll.confirm')}
        </Button>
      </form>
      <div className="auth__links">
        <button type="button" className="linkbtn" onClick={restart}>
          {t('mfa.backToLogin')}
        </button>
      </div>
    </AuthLayout>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/auth/SessionsScreen.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useState, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import type { MeOut, MenuOut, SessionOut } from '../api/types';
import { useDocumentTitle } from '../app/useDocumentTitle';
import { t } from '../i18n';
import { formatDateTime } from '../i18n/format';
import { buildHash } from '../router/hash';
import { AppFrame } from '../shell/AppFrame';
import { PageHeader } from '../shell/PageHeader';
import type { SideModel } from '../shell/SideMenu';
import { Badge } from '../ui/Badge';
import { Button } from '../ui/Button';
import { Card } from '../ui/Card';
import { ConfirmDialog } from '../ui/ConfirmDialog';
import { ErrorBanner } from '../ui/ErrorBanner';
import { PageSkeleton } from '../ui/Skeleton';
import { Table, type Column } from '../ui/Table';
import { useToast } from '../ui/Toast';
import { useRevokeSession, useSessions } from './api';

export function SessionsScreen({ me, menu, tools }: { me: MeOut; menu: MenuOut; tools?: ReactNode }) {
  const sessions = useSessions();
  const revoke = useRevokeSession();
  const toast = useToast();
  const [target, setTarget] = useState<SessionOut | null>(null);
  useDocumentTitle(t('sessions.title'));

  const side: SideModel = {
    title: t('account.title'),
    desc: me.full_name,
    icon: 'user',
    activeId: 'sessions',
    items: [
      { id: 'sessions', label: t('user.sessions'), icon: 'history', href: buildHash({ kind: 'sessions' }) },
      { id: 'password', label: t('user.password'), icon: 'key', href: buildHash({ kind: 'password' }) },
    ],
  };

  const columns: Column<SessionOut>[] = [
    {
      key: 'device',
      header: t('sessions.device'),
      align: 'left',
      cell: (s) => (
        <span className="cell-main">
          <b>{s.ua_summary || t('sessions.unknownDevice')}</b>
          {s.current ? <Badge tone="ok">{t('sessions.current')}</Badge> : null}
        </span>
      ),
    },
    { key: 'ip', header: t('sessions.ip'), align: 'left', cell: (s) => s.ip ?? t('common.dash') },
    { key: 'created', header: t('sessions.created'), cell: (s) => formatDateTime(s.created_at), sortValue: (s) => s.created_at },
    { key: 'seen', header: t('sessions.lastSeen'), cell: (s) => formatDateTime(s.last_seen_at), sortValue: (s) => s.last_seen_at },
    {
      key: 'action',
      header: t('sessions.actions'),
      cell: (s) =>
        s.current ? (
          <span className="muted">{t('sessions.thisDevice')}</span>
        ) : (
          <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => setTarget(s)}>
            {t('sessions.end')}
          </Button>
        ),
    },
  ];

  const close = () => {
    setTarget(null);
    revoke.reset();
  };

  return (
    <AppFrame menu={menu} activeSection={null} side={side} tools={tools}>
      <PageHeader crumb={t('account.title')} title={t('sessions.title')} sub={t('sessions.lead')} />
      <div className="view">
        {sessions.isPending ? (
          <PageSkeleton />
        ) : sessions.isError ? (
          <ErrorBanner error={sessions.error} lastSuccessAt={sessions.dataUpdatedAt} onRetry={() => void sessions.refetch()} />
        ) : (
          <Card>
            <Table
              caption={t('sessions.title')}
              columns={columns}
              rows={sessions.data}
              rowKey={(s) => s.id}
              initialSort={{ key: 'seen', dir: 'desc' }}
              free
            />
          </Card>
        )}
      </div>
      <ConfirmDialog
        open={target !== null}
        title={t('sessions.confirmTitle')}
        message={t('sessions.confirmText', { device: target?.ua_summary || t('sessions.unknownDevice') })}
        confirmLabel={t('sessions.end')}
        danger
        busy={revoke.isPending}
        error={revoke.error}
        onCancel={close}
        onConfirm={() => {
          if (!target) return;
          revoke.mutate(target.id, {
            onSuccess: () => {
              toast.show(t('sessions.ended'), 'success');
              close();
            },
          });
        }}
      />
    </AppFrame>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { screen, waitFor, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import type { PositionOut, UserOut } from '../api/types';
import { expectNoAxeViolations } from '../test/axe';
import { ME_ADMIN } from '../test/fixtures';
import { apiError, json, mockApi, type MockHandler } from '../test/mockApi';
import { renderWithProviders } from '../test/render';
import UsersPage from './UsersPage';

const USERS: UserOut[] = [
  {
    id: 1, username: 'admin', full_name: 'Азимов Бахтиёр', position: { id: 6, code: 'admin', name: 'Администратор' },
    role_code: 'admin', is_active: true, totp_enabled: true, locked_until: null,
    last_login_at: '2026-09-24T04:00:00Z', created_at: '2026-09-01T00:00:00Z',
  },
  {
    id: 2, username: 'karimova', full_name: 'Каримова Нилуфар', position: { id: 4, code: 'economist_filiala', name: 'Экономист филиала' },
    role_code: 'economist_filiala', is_active: false, totp_enabled: false, locked_until: null,
    last_login_at: null, created_at: '2026-09-02T00:00:00Z',
  },
  {
    id: 3, username: 'rakhimov', full_name: 'Рахимов Ильхом', position: { id: 7, code: 'vnutr_kontrol', name: 'Служба внутреннего контроля' },
    role_code: 'vnutr_kontrol', is_active: true, totp_enabled: true, locked_until: '2099-01-01T00:00:00Z',
    last_login_at: '2026-09-20T04:00:00Z', created_at: '2026-09-03T00:00:00Z',
  },
];

const POSITIONS: PositionOut[] = [
  { id: 6, code: 'admin', name: 'Администратор', role_id: 6, role_code: 'admin', is_active: true, branch_ids: [] },
  { id: 4, code: 'economist_filiala', name: 'Экономист филиала', role_id: 4, role_code: 'economist_filiala', is_active: true, branch_ids: [3] },
  { id: 3, code: 'spec_kazna', name: 'Специалист казначейства', role_id: 3, role_code: 'spec_kazna', is_active: true, branch_ids: [] },
  { id: 7, code: 'vnutr_kontrol', name: 'Служба внутреннего контроля', role_id: 7, role_code: 'vnutr_kontrol', is_active: true, branch_ids: [] },
];

function routes(extra: Record<string, MockHandler> = {}) {
  return mockApi({
    'GET /admin/users': (req) => {
      const q = req.url.searchParams.get('q') ?? '';
      const items = USERS.filter((u) => !q || u.username.includes(q) || u.full_name.toLowerCase().includes(q));
      return json({ items, total: items.length, page: 1, page_size: 50 });
    },
    'GET /admin/positions': json(POSITIONS),
    ...extra,
  });
}

async function openUser(user: ReturnType<typeof renderWithProviders>['user'], name: string) {
  await user.click(await screen.findByText(name));
  return screen.getByRole('dialog', { name });
}

describe('UsersPage', () => {
  it('lists users with 2FA and status badges', async () => {
    routes();
    renderWithProviders(<UsersPage me={ME_ADMIN} />);
    const table = await screen.findByRole('table', { name: 'Пользователи' });
    const rows = within(table).getAllByRole('row').slice(1);
    expect(rows).toHaveLength(3);
    const karimova = rows.find((r) => r.textContent?.includes('karimova')) as HTMLElement;
    expect(karimova).toHaveTextContent('Заблокирован');
    expect(karimova).toHaveTextContent('Не подключена');
    const rakhimov = rows.find((r) => r.textContent?.includes('rakhimov')) as HTMLElement;
    expect(rakhimov).toHaveTextContent('Временно заблокирован до 01.01.2099');
    expect(screen.getByRole('navigation', { name: 'Страницы' })).toHaveTextContent('1–3 из 3');
  });

  it('searches on the server after a short pause', async () => {
    const server = routes();
    const { user } = renderWithProviders(<UsersPage me={ME_ADMIN} />);
    await screen.findByRole('table', { name: 'Пользователи' });
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Поиск'), 'kar');
    await waitFor(() => expect(server.calls.some((c) => c.url.searchParams.get('q') === 'kar')).toBe(true));
    await waitFor(() => expect(within(screen.getByRole('table')).getAllByRole('row')).toHaveLength(2));
  });

  it('validates and creates a user, then shows the temporary password once', async () => {
    const server = routes({
      'POST /admin/users': () =>
        json({ user: { ...USERS[0], id: 9, username: 'novikov', full_name: 'Новиков Сотрудник' }, temporary_password: 'Tmp-Parol-1234567' }, 201),
    });
    const { user } = renderWithProviders(<UsersPage me={ME_ADMIN} />);
    await user.click(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'Добавить пользователя' }));
    const dialog = screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Новый пользователь' });
    await user.type(within(dialog).getByLabelText('Логин'), 'A b');
    await user.click(within(dialog).getByRole('button', { name: 'Создать' }));
    expect(within(dialog).getAllByText('3–64 символа: латинские строчные буквы, цифры, точка, дефис, подчёркивание').length).toBeGreaterThan(1);
    expect(within(dialog).getAllByText('Обязательное поле')).toHaveLength(2);
    await user.clear(within(dialog).getByLabelText('Логин'));
    await user.type(within(dialog).getByLabelText('Логин'), 'Novikov');
    await user.type(within(dialog).getByLabelText('ФИО'), 'Новиков Сотрудник');
    await user.type(within(dialog).getByRole('combobox', { name: 'Должность' }), 'Спец');
    await user.keyboard('{ArrowDown}{Enter}');
    await user.click(within(dialog).getByRole('button', { name: 'Создать' }));
    const secret = await screen.findByRole('dialog', { name: 'Временный пароль' });
    expect(within(secret).getByTestId('temp-password')).toHaveTextContent('Tmp-Parol-1234567');
    expect(server.calls.find((c) => c.method === 'POST')?.body).toEqual({ username: 'novikov', full_name: 'Новиков Сотрудник', position_id: 3 });
    await user.click(within(secret).getByRole('button', { name: 'Скопировать' }));
    expect(navigator.clipboard.writeText).toHaveBeenCalledWith('Tmp-Parol-1234567');
  });

  it('shows a taken username next to the field', async () => {
    routes({ 'POST /admin/users': apiError(409, 'username_taken', 'Логин уже занят') });
    const { user } = renderWithProviders(<UsersPage me={ME_ADMIN} />);
    await user.click(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'Добавить пользователя' }));
    const dialog = screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Новый пользователь' });
    await user.type(within(dialog).getByLabelText('Логин'), 'admin');
    await user.type(within(dialog).getByLabelText('ФИО'), 'Дубликат');
    await user.type(within(dialog).getByRole('combobox', { name: 'Должность' }), 'Админ');
    await user.keyboard('{ArrowDown}{Enter}');
    await user.click(within(dialog).getByRole('button', { name: 'Создать' }));
    expect(await within(dialog).findByText('Логин уже занят')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(dialog).getByLabelText('Логин')).toHaveAttribute('aria-invalid', 'true');
  });

  it('unblocks a user after confirmation', async () => {
    const server = routes({ 'POST /admin/users/2/unblock': json({ ...USERS[1], is_active: true }) });
    const { user } = renderWithProviders(<UsersPage me={ME_ADMIN} />);
    const dialog = await openUser(user, 'Каримова Нилуфар');
    await user.click(within(dialog).getByRole('button', { name: 'Разблокировать' }));
    const confirm = screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Разблокировать пользователя?' });

    expect(await screen.findByText('Пользователь разблокирован')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(server.calls.some((c) => c.path === '/admin/users/2/unblock')).toBe(true);
  });

  it('resets a password and shows the new temporary password', async () => {

    const { user } = renderWithProviders(<UsersPage me={ME_ADMIN} />);
    const dialog = await openUser(user, 'Рахимов Ильхом');
    await user.click(within(dialog).getByRole('button', { name: 'Сбросить пароль' }));
    await user.click(within(screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Сбросить пароль?' })).getByRole('button', { name: 'Сбросить пароль' }));
    expect(await screen.findByTestId('temp-password')).toHaveTextContent('New-Tmp-Parol-99');
  });

  it('keeps the confirmation open with the server reason on 409', async () => {
    routes({ 'POST /admin/users/3/block': apiError(409, 'last_admin', 'Нельзя оставить систему без администратора') });
    const { user } = renderWithProviders(<UsersPage me={ME_ADMIN} />);
    const dialog = await openUser(user, 'Рахимов Ильхом');
    await user.click(within(dialog).getByRole('button', { name: 'Заблокировать' }));
    const confirm = screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Заблокировать пользователя?' });
    await user.click(within(confirm).getByRole('button', { name: 'Заблокировать' }));
    expect(await within(confirm).findByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent('Нельзя оставить систему без администратора');
  });

  it('saves profile changes', async () => {
    const server = routes({ 'PATCH /admin/users/3': (req) => json({ ...USERS[2], ...(req.body as object) }) });
    const { user } = renderWithProviders(<UsersPage me={ME_ADMIN} />);
    const dialog = await openUser(user, 'Рахимов Ильхом');
    const name = within(dialog).getByLabelText('ФИО');
    await user.clear(name);
    await user.type(name, 'Рахимов Ильхом Каримович');
    await user.click(within(dialog).getByRole('button', { name: 'Сохранить' }));
    expect(await screen.findByText('Изменения сохранены')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(server.calls.find((c) => c.method === 'PATCH')?.body).toEqual({ full_name: 'Рахимов Ильхом Каримович', position_id: 7 });
  });

  it('disables dangerous actions on the own account', async () => {
    routes();
    const { user } = renderWithProviders(<UsersPage me={ME_ADMIN} />);
    const dialog = await openUser(user, 'Азимов Бахтиёр');
    expect(within(dialog).getByText('Эти действия нельзя выполнить со своей учётной записью.')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(dialog).getByRole('button', { name: 'Заблокировать' })).toBeDisabled();
    expect(within(dialog).getByRole('button', { name: 'Сбросить 2FA' })).toBeDisabled();
  });

  it('has no axe violations', async () => {
    routes();
    const { container } = renderWithProviders(<UsersPage me={ME_ADMIN} />);
    await screen.findByRole('table', { name: 'Пользователи' });
    await expectNoAxeViolations(container);
  });
});
```

```tsx
import { screen, waitFor, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import type { MeOut, MenuOut } from '../api/types';
import { useRoute } from '../router/store';
import { ME_ADMIN, ME_AUDITOR, MENU_AUDITOR, MENU_FULL, shellRoutes } from '../test/fixtures';
import { json, mockApi } from '../test/mockApi';
import { renderWithProviders } from '../test/render';
import { AdminScreen } from './AdminScreen';

function Harness({ me, menu }: { me: MeOut; menu: MenuOut }) {
  const route = useRoute();
  if (route.kind !== 'admin') return <p>route:{route.kind}</p>;
  return <AdminScreen me={me} menu={menu} route={route} />;
}

describe('AdminScreen', () => {
  it('shows the admin side menu filtered by permissions and the users page', async () => {
    mockApi({
      ...shellRoutes(),
      'GET /admin/users': json({ items: [], total: 0, page: 1, page_size: 50 }),
      'GET /admin/positions': json([]),
    });
    renderWithProviders(<Harness me={ME_ADMIN} menu={MENU_FULL} />, { hash: '#admin/users' });
    const side = await screen.findByRole('complementary', { name: 'Администрирование' });
    expect(within(side).getAllByRole('link')).toHaveLength(8);
    expect(within(side).getByRole('link', { name: 'Пользователи' })).toHaveAttribute('aria-current', 'page');
    expect(await screen.findByRole('heading', { level: 1, name: 'Пользователи' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(document.title).toBe('Пользователи | Администрирование | UGEKAZNA');
  });

  it('opens the first allowed page for #admin', async () => {
    mockApi(shellRoutes(ME_AUDITOR, MENU_AUDITOR));
    renderWithProviders(<Harness me={ME_AUDITOR} menu={MENU_AUDITOR} />, { hash: '#admin' });
    await waitFor(() => expect(window.location.hash).toBe('#admin/audit'));
    const side = await screen.findByRole('complementary', { name: 'Администрирование' });
    expect(within(side).getAllByRole('link').map((l) => l.textContent)).toEqual(['Журнал аудита']);
  });

  it('shows no access for a known admin page without permission', async () => {
    mockApi(shellRoutes(ME_AUDITOR, MENU_AUDITOR));
    renderWithProviders(<Harness me={ME_AUDITOR} menu={MENU_AUDITOR} />, { hash: '#admin/users' });
    expect(await screen.findByRole('heading', { name: 'Нет доступа' })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});
```

`frontend/src/ui/useDebouncedValue.ts`:
```ts
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';

export function useDebouncedValue<T>(value: T, delay: number): T {
  const [debounced, setDebounced] = useState(value);
  useEffect(() => {
    const id = window.setTimeout(() => setDebounced(value), delay);
    return () => window.clearTimeout(id);
  }, [value, delay]);
  return debounced;
}
```

`frontend/src/admin/AdminScreen.tsx`:
```tsx
import { Suspense, useEffect, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import type { MeOut, MenuOut } from '../api/types';
import { useDocumentTitle } from '../app/useDocumentTitle';
import { t } from '../i18n';
import { buildHash, type Route } from '../router/hash';
import { navigate } from '../router/store';
import { SectionPlaceholder } from '../sections/SectionPlaceholder';
import { AppFrame } from '../shell/AppFrame';
import { PageHeader } from '../shell/PageHeader';
import type { SideModel } from '../shell/SideMenu';
import { StatusScreen } from '../shell/StatusScreen';
import { PageSkeleton } from '../ui/Skeleton';
import { ADMIN_PAGES, allowedAdminPages } from './access';
import { ADMIN_COMPONENTS } from './registry';
import './admin.css';

export type AdminRoute = Extract<Route, { kind: 'admin' }>;

export function AdminScreen({ me, menu, route, tools }: { me: MeOut; menu: MenuOut; route: AdminRoute; tools?: ReactNode }) {
  const allowed = allowedAdminPages(me.permissions);
  const known = ADMIN_PAGES.some((page) => page.id === route.page);
  const requested = allowed.find((page) => page.id === route.page);
  const forbidden = known && !requested;
  const current = requested ?? (forbidden ? undefined : allowed[0]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (current && route.page !== current.id) navigate({ kind: 'admin', page: current.id }, { replace: true });
  }, [current, route.page]);

  useDocumentTitle(current ? `${t(current.titleKey)} | ${t('admin.title')}` : null);

  if (!current) return <StatusScreen kind="noaccess" menu={menu} tools={tools} />;

  const side: SideModel = {
    title: t('admin.title'),
    desc: t('admin.desc'),
    icon: 'shield',
    activeId: current.id,
    items: allowed.map((page) => ({
      id: page.id,
      label: t(page.titleKey),
      icon: page.icon,
      href: buildHash({ kind: 'admin', page: page.id }),
    })),
  };
  const Page = ADMIN_COMPONENTS[current.id];

  return (
    <AppFrame menu={menu} activeSection={null} side={side} tools={tools}>
      <PageHeader crumb={t('admin.title')} title={t(current.titleKey)} />
      <div className="view">
        {Page ? (
          <Suspense fallback={<PageSkeleton />}>
            <Page me={me} />
          </Suspense>
        ) : (
          <SectionPlaceholder title={t(current.titleKey)} />
        )}
      </div>
    </AppFrame>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/admin/api.ts`:
```ts
import { keepPreviousData, useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { api } from '../api/client';
import type {
  Page,
  PositionOut,
  TemporaryPassword,
  UserCreate,
  UserCreated,
  UserOut,
  UserUpdate,
} from '../api/types';

export const USERS_PAGE_SIZE = 50;

export const adminKeys = {
  users: ['admin', 'users'] as const,
  positions: ['admin', 'positions'] as const,
  roles: ['admin', 'roles'] as const,
  permissions: ['admin', 'permissions'] as const,
  alertRules: ['admin', 'alert-rules'] as const,
  finAssumptions: ['admin', 'fin-assumptions'] as const,
  etlRuns: ['admin', 'etl-runs'] as const,
  builds: ['admin', 'builds'] as const,
  audit: ['admin', 'audit'] as const,
  keys: ['admin', 'keys'] as const,
};

export function useUsers(q: string, page: number) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: [...adminKeys.users, q, page],
    queryFn: ({ signal }) =>
      api<Page<UserOut>>('/admin/users', { signal, query: { q: q || undefined, page, page_size: USERS_PAGE_SIZE } }),
    placeholderData: keepPreviousData,
    staleTime: 0,
  });
}

export function usePositions() {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: adminKeys.positions,
    queryFn: ({ signal }) => api<PositionOut[]>('/admin/positions', { signal }),
    staleTime: 0,
  });
}

function useInvalidate(key: readonly string[]) {
  const client = useQueryClient();
  return () => client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: key });
}

export function useCreateUser() {
  const invalidate = useInvalidate(adminKeys.users);
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (body: UserCreate) => api<UserCreated>('/admin/users', { method: 'POST', body }),
    onSuccess: invalidate,
  });
}

export function useUpdateUser() {
  const invalidate = useInvalidate(adminKeys.users);
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: ({ id, body }: { id: number; body: UserUpdate }) =>
      api<UserOut>(`/admin/users/${id}`, { method: 'PATCH', body }),
    onSuccess: invalidate,
  });
}

export function useSetUserActive() {
  const invalidate = useInvalidate(adminKeys.users);
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: ({ id, active }: { id: number; active: boolean }) =>
      api<UserOut>(`/admin/users/${id}/${active ? 'unblock' : 'block'}`, { method: 'POST' }),
    onSuccess: invalidate,
  });
}

export function useResetUserPassword() {
  const invalidate = useInvalidate(adminKeys.users);
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (id: number) => api<TemporaryPassword>(`/admin/users/${id}/reset-password`, { method: 'POST' }),
    onSuccess: invalidate,
  });
}

export function useResetUserMfa() {
  const invalidate = useInvalidate(adminKeys.users);
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (id: number) => api<void>(`/admin/users/${id}/reset-2fa`, { method: 'POST' }),
    onSuccess: invalidate,
  });
}
```

`frontend/src/admin/labels.ts`:
```ts
import type { UserOut } from '../api/types';
import { t } from '../i18n';
import { formatDateTime } from '../i18n/format';
import type { BadgeTone } from '../ui/Badge';

export function userStatus(user: UserOut, now = Date.now()): { tone: BadgeTone; label: string } {
  if (!user.is_active) return { tone: 'neg', label: t('users.status.blocked') };
  if (user.locked_until && Date.parse(user.locked_until) > now) {
    return { tone: 'warn', label: t('users.status.locked', { time: formatDateTime(user.locked_until) }) };
  }
  return { tone: 'ok', label: t('users.status.active') };
}
```

```tsx
import { isApiError } from '../api/client';
import type { MeOut, PositionOut, UserOut } from '../api/types';
import { t } from '../i18n';
import { Button } from '../ui/Button';

import { useToast } from '../ui/Toast';
import { useCreateUser, useResetUserMfa, useResetUserPassword, useSetUserActive, useUpdateUser } from './api';

const USERNAME_RE = /^[a-z0-9._-]{3,64}$/;

function positionOptions(positions: readonly PositionOut[], keepId?: number) {
  return positions
    .filter((p) => p.is_active || p.id === keepId)
    .map((p) => ({ value: String(p.id), label: p.name, hint: p.role_code }));
}

interface CreateProps {
  positions: readonly PositionOut[];
  onClose(): void;
  onCreated(user: UserOut, temporaryPassword: string): void;
}

export function UserCreateDialog({ positions, onClose, onCreated }: CreateProps) {
  const formId = useId();
  const create = useCreateUser();
  const [username, setUsername] = useState('');
  const [fullName, setFullName] = useState('');
  const [positionId, setPositionId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [errors, setErrors] = useState<{ username?: string; fullName?: string; position?: string }>({});

  const submit = (event: FormEvent) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    const login = username.trim().toLowerCase();
    const found: typeof errors = {};
    if (!USERNAME_RE.test(login)) found.username = t('users.usernameRule');
    if (!fullName.trim()) found.fullName = t('field.required');
    if (!positionId) found.position = t('field.required');
    setErrors(found);
    if (found.username || found.fullName || found.position) return;
    create.mutate(
      { username: login, full_name: fullName.trim(), position_id: Number(positionId) },
      {
        onSuccess: (result) => onCreated(result.user, result.temporary_password),
        onError: (error) => {
          if (isApiError(error, 409, 'username_taken')) setErrors({ username: error.message });
        },
      },
    );
  };

  const otherError = create.error && !isApiError(create.error, 409, 'username_taken') ? create.error : null;

  return (
    <Modal
      open
      onClose={onClose}
      title={t('users.createTitle')}
      footer={
        <>
          <Button variant="secondary" onClick={onClose}>
            {t('common.cancel')}
          </Button>
          <Button type="submit" form={formId} loading={create.isPending}>
            {t('common.create')}
          </Button>
        </>
      }
    >
      <form id={formId} className="form-grid" noValidate onSubmit={submit}>
        <div className="span-2">
          <FormError error={otherError} />
        </div>
        <TextInput
          label={t('users.username')}
          hint={t('users.usernameRule')}
          autoComplete="off"
          autoCapitalize="none"
          spellCheck={false}
          required
          value={username}
          error={errors.username}
          onChange={(e) => setUsername(e.target.value)}
        />
        <TextInput label={t('users.fullName')} required value={fullName} error={errors.fullName} onChange={(e) => setFullName(e.target.value)} />
        <div className="span-2">
          <Combobox
            label={t('users.position')}
            options={positionOptions(positions)}
            value={positionId}
            onChange={setPositionId}
            error={errors.position}
            required
          />
        </div>
        <p className="span-2 note">{t('users.createNote')}</p>
      </form>
    </Modal>
  );
}

type Danger = 'block' | 'unblock' | 'password' | 'mfa';

interface EditProps {
  me: MeOut;
  user: UserOut;
  positions: readonly PositionOut[];
  onClose(): void;
  onTemporaryPassword(username: string, password: string): void;
}

export function UserEditDialog({ me, user, positions, onClose, onTemporaryPassword }: EditProps) {
  const formId = useId();
  const toast = useToast();
  const update = useUpdateUser();
  const setActive = useSetUserActive();
  const resetPassword = useResetUserPassword();
  const resetMfa = useResetUserMfa();
  const [fullName, setFullName] = useState(user.full_name);
  const [positionId, setPositionId] = useState<string | null>(String(user.position.id));
  const [nameError, setNameError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [danger, setDanger] = useState<Danger | null>(null);
  const self = user.id === me.id;

  const save = (event: FormEvent) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    if (!fullName.trim()) {
      setNameError(t('field.required'));
      return;
    }
    setNameError(null);
    update.mutate(
      { id: user.id, body: { full_name: fullName.trim(), position_id: Number(positionId ?? user.position.id) } },
      {
        onSuccess: () => {
          toast.show(t('users.saved'), 'success');
          onClose();
        },
      },
    );
  };

  const busy = setActive.isPending || resetPassword.isPending || resetMfa.isPending;
  const dangerError = setActive.error ?? resetPassword.error ?? resetMfa.error;

  const closeDanger = () => {
    setDanger(null);
    setActive.reset();
    resetPassword.reset();
    resetMfa.reset();
  };

  const runDanger = () => {
    if (danger === 'block' || danger === 'unblock') {
      setActive.mutate(
        { id: user.id, active: danger === 'unblock' },
        {
          onSuccess: () => {
            toast.show(danger === 'block' ? t('users.blocked') : t('users.unblocked'), 'success');
            closeDanger();
            onClose();
          },
        },
      );
    } else if (danger === 'password') {
      resetPassword.mutate(user.id, {
        onSuccess: (result) => {
          closeDanger();
          onTemporaryPassword(user.username, result.temporary_password);
        },
      });
    } else if (danger === 'mfa') {
      resetMfa.mutate(user.id, {
        onSuccess: () => {
          toast.show(t('users.mfaReset'), 'success');
          closeDanger();
          onClose();
        },
      });
    }
  };

  const confirmTexts: Record<Danger, { title: string; text: string; label: string }> = {
    block: { title: t('users.confirm.block.title'), text: t('users.confirm.block.text', { name: user.full_name }), label: t('users.block') },
    unblock: { title: t('users.confirm.unblock.title'), text: t('users.confirm.unblock.text', { name: user.full_name }), label: t('users.unblock') },
    password: { title: t('users.confirm.password.title'), text: t('users.confirm.password.text', { name: user.full_name }), label: t('users.resetPassword') },
    mfa: { title: t('users.confirm.mfa.title'), text: t('users.confirm.mfa.text', { name: user.full_name }), label: t('users.resetMfa') },
  };
  const current = danger ? confirmTexts[danger] : null;

  return (
    <>
      <Modal
        open
        onClose={onClose}
        title={user.full_name}
        note={t('users.editNote', { username: user.username, role: user.role_code })}
        footer={
          <>
            <Button variant="secondary" onClick={onClose}>
              {t('common.cancel')}
            </Button>
            <Button type="submit" form={formId} loading={update.isPending}>
              {t('common.save')}
            </Button>
          </>
        }
      >
        <form id={formId} className="form-grid" noValidate onSubmit={save}>
          <div className="span-2">
            <FormError error={update.error} />
          </div>
          <div className="span-2">
            <TextInput label={t('users.fullName')} value={fullName} error={nameError} onChange={(e) => setFullName(e.target.value)} />
          </div>
          <div className="span-2">
            <Combobox label={t('users.position')} options={positionOptions(positions, user.position.id)} value={positionId} onChange={setPositionId} />
          </div>
        </form>
        <section className="danger-zone" aria-labelledby={`${formId}-danger`}>
          <h3 id={`${formId}-danger`}>{t('users.actions')}</h3>
          {self ? (
            <p className="note">
              <Icon name="info" /> {t('users.selfNote')}
            </p>
          ) : null}
          <div className="row-actions">
            {user.is_active ? (
              <Button variant="danger" icon="lock" disabled={self} onClick={() => setDanger('block')}>
                {t('users.block')}
              </Button>
            ) : (
              <Button variant="secondary" icon="check" disabled={self} onClick={() => setDanger('unblock')}>
                {t('users.unblock')}
              </Button>
            )}
            <Button variant="secondary" icon="key" disabled={self} onClick={() => setDanger('password')}>
              {t('users.resetPassword')}
            </Button>
            <Button variant="secondary" icon="shield" disabled={self} onClick={() => setDanger('mfa')}>
              {t('users.resetMfa')}
            </Button>
          </div>
        </section>
      </Modal>
      <ConfirmDialog
        open={current !== null}
        title={current?.title ?? ''}
        message={current?.text ?? ''}
        confirmLabel={current?.label ?? ''}
        danger={danger !== 'unblock'}
        busy={busy}
        error={dangerError}
        onCancel={closeDanger}
        onConfirm={runDanger}
      />
    </>
  );
}

export function TemporaryPasswordDialog({ username, password, onClose }: { username: string; password: string; onClose(): void }) {
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);
  const copy = async () => {
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(password);
      setCopied(true);
    } catch {
      setCopied(false);
    }
  };
  return (
    <Modal
      open
      onClose={onClose}
      title={t('users.tempTitle')}
      note={t('users.tempFor', { username })}
      size="sm"
      footer={<Button onClick={onClose}>{t('common.close')}</Button>}
    >
      <p className="notice">
        <Icon name="alert" />
        <span>{t('users.tempNote')}</span>
      </p>
      <div className="secret-box">
        <code className="mono" data-testid="temp-password">
          {password}
        </code>
        <Button variant="secondary" icon="copy" onClick={() => void copy()}>
          {copied ? t('common.copied') : t('common.copy')}
        </Button>
      </div>
    </Modal>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/admin/UsersPage.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import type { UserOut } from '../api/types';
import { t } from '../i18n';
import { formatDateTime } from '../i18n/format';
import { Badge } from '../ui/Badge';
import { Button } from '../ui/Button';
import { Card } from '../ui/Card';
import { ErrorBanner } from '../ui/ErrorBanner';
import { TextInput } from '../ui/Field';
import { Pagination } from '../ui/Pagination';
import { PageSkeleton } from '../ui/Skeleton';
import { Table, type Column } from '../ui/Table';
import { useDebouncedValue } from '../ui/useDebouncedValue';
import { USERS_PAGE_SIZE, usePositions, useUsers } from './api';
import { userStatus } from './labels';
import type { AdminPageProps } from './registry';
import { TemporaryPasswordDialog, UserCreateDialog, UserEditDialog } from './UserDialogs';

type DialogState = { kind: 'create' } | { kind: 'edit'; user: UserOut } | null;

export default function UsersPage({ me }: AdminPageProps) {
  const [search, setSearch] = useState('');
  const q = useDebouncedValue(search.trim().toLowerCase(), 300);
  const [page, setPage] = useState(1);
  const users = useUsers(q, page);
  const positions = usePositions();
  const [dialog, setDialog] = useState<DialogState>(null);
  const [secret, setSecret] = useState<{ username: string; password: string } | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    setPage(1);
  }, [q]);

  const columns: Column<UserOut>[] = [
    {
      key: 'user',
      header: t('users.col.user'),
      align: 'left',
      sortValue: (u) => u.full_name,
      cell: (u) => (
        <span className="cell-main">
          <b>{u.full_name}</b>
          <small className="mono">{u.username}</small>
        </span>
      ),
    },
    { key: 'position', header: t('users.col.position'), align: 'left', wrap: true, sortValue: (u) => u.position.name, cell: (u) => u.position.name },
    { key: 'role', header: t('users.col.role'), align: 'left', cell: (u) => <span className="mono">{u.role_code}</span> },
    {
      key: 'mfa',
      header: t('users.col.mfa'),
      align: 'left',
      cell: (u) => <Badge tone={u.totp_enabled ? 'ok' : 'warn'}>{u.totp_enabled ? t('users.mfaOn') : t('users.mfaOff')}</Badge>,
    },
    {
      key: 'status',
      header: t('users.col.status'),
      align: 'left',
      cell: (u) => {
        const status = userStatus(u);
        return <Badge tone={status.tone}>{status.label}</Badge>;
      },
    },
    {
      key: 'login',
      header: t('users.col.lastLogin'),
      sortValue: (u) => u.last_login_at,
      cell: (u) => (u.last_login_at ? formatDateTime(u.last_login_at) : t('common.dash')),
    },
  ];

  return (
    <>
      <Card>
        <div className="toolbar">
          <TextInput
            type="search"
            label={t('users.search')}
            placeholder={t('users.searchPlaceholder')}
            value={search}
            onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
          />
          <span className="toolbar__grow" />
          <Button icon="plus" onClick={() => setDialog({ kind: 'create' })}>
            {t('users.add')}
          </Button>
        </div>
        {users.isError ? (
          <ErrorBanner error={users.error} lastSuccessAt={users.dataUpdatedAt} onRetry={() => void users.refetch()} />
        ) : users.isPending ? (
          <PageSkeleton />
        ) : (
          <>
            <Table
              caption={t('admin.page.users')}
              columns={columns}
              rows={users.data.items}
              rowKey={(u) => u.id}
              onRowClick={(u) => setDialog({ kind: 'edit', user: u })}
              empty={q ? t('users.notFound') : undefined}
            />
            <Pagination page={page} pageSize={USERS_PAGE_SIZE} total={users.data.total} onPage={setPage} />
          </>
        )}
      </Card>
      {dialog?.kind === 'create' ? (
        <UserCreateDialog
          positions={positions.data ?? []}
          onClose={() => setDialog(null)}
          onCreated={(user, password) => {
            setDialog(null);
            setSecret({ username: user.username, password });
          }}
        />
      ) : null}
      {dialog?.kind === 'edit' ? (
        <UserEditDialog
          me={me}
          user={dialog.user}
          positions={positions.data ?? []}
          onClose={() => setDialog(null)}
          onTemporaryPassword={(username, password) => {
            setDialog(null);
            setSecret({ username, password });
          }}
        />
      ) : null}
      {secret ? <TemporaryPasswordDialog username={secret.username} password={secret.password} onClose={() => setSecret(null)} /> : null}
    </>
  );
}
```

```css
.toolbar { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; align-items: flex-end; gap: 0.7rem; margin-bottom: 0.9rem; }
.toolbar .field { min-width: 14rem; }
.toolbar__grow { flex: 1; }
.form-grid { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr)); gap: 1rem; }
.span-2 { grid-column: 1 / -1; }
.span-2:empty { display: none; }
.note { margin: 0; font-size: 0.9rem; color: var(--muted); }
.note .icon { width: 1rem; height: 1rem; vertical-align: -0.15em; }
.fieldset { min-width: 0; margin: 0; padding: 0.8rem 1rem 1rem; border: 1px solid var(--line); border-radius: 0.7rem; }
.fieldset legend { padding: 0 0.3rem; font-weight: 600; }
.checklist { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fill, minmax(15rem, 1fr)); gap: 0.5rem 1rem; margin-top: 0.5rem; }
.danger-zone { display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 0.6rem; padding-top: 1rem; border-top: 1px solid var(--line); }
.danger-zone h3 { margin: 0; font-size: 1rem; }
.row-actions { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 0.6rem; }
.notice { display: flex; align-items: flex-start; gap: 0.6rem; margin: 0; padding: 0.8rem 0.9rem; border-radius: 0.6rem; background: var(--warn-bg); color: var(--warn); font-weight: 500; }
.notice .icon { width: 1.2rem; height: 1.2rem; flex: none; margin-top: 0.1rem; }
.secret-box {
  display: flex;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  gap: 1rem;
  padding: 0.8rem 1rem;
  border-radius: 0.6rem;
  background: var(--soft);
  font-size: 1.2rem;
}
.matrix input { width: 6.5rem; text-align: right; }
.matrix .field { min-width: 0; }
.result { display: flex; align-items: flex-start; gap: 0.6rem; margin: 0; padding: 0.8rem 1rem; border-radius: 0.7rem; font-weight: 500; }
.result .icon { width: 1.2rem; height: 1.2rem; flex: none; margin-top: 0.1rem; }
.result--ok { background: var(--ok-bg); color: var(--ok); }
.result--bad { background: var(--neg-bg); color: var(--neg); }
.details {
  max-height: 20rem;
  margin: 0;
  padding: 0.8rem;
  overflow: auto;
  border-radius: 0.6rem;
  background: var(--soft);
  font-size: 0.85rem;
  white-space: pre-wrap;
  word-break: break-word;
}
.dl { display: grid; grid-template-columns: max-content minmax(0, 1fr); gap: 0.4rem 1rem; margin: 0; }
.dl dt { color: var(--muted); }
.dl dd { margin: 0; }
@media (max-width: 767.98px) {
  .form-grid { grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr); }
  .toolbar .field { width: 100%; min-width: 0; }
  .toolbar__grow { display: none; }
}
```

`frontend/src/admin/numbers.test.ts`:
```ts
import { expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { formatDecimal, parseDecimal } from './numbers';

it('parses decimals with comma or dot and rejects garbage', () => {
  expect(parseDecimal('0,08')).toBe(0.08);
  expect(parseDecimal(' 1.5 ')).toBe(1.5);
  expect(parseDecimal('−0,2')).toBe(-0.2);
  expect(parseDecimal('')).toBeNull();
  expect(parseDecimal('1,2,3')).toBeNull();
  expect(parseDecimal('abc')).toBeNull();
});

it('formats decimals with a comma and no trailing zeros', () => {
  expect(formatDecimal(0.08)).toBe('0,08');
  expect(formatDecimal(1)).toBe('1');
  expect(formatDecimal(-0.25)).toBe('-0,25');
});
```

```tsx
import { screen, within } from '@testing-library/react';

  { id: 4, code: 'economist_filiala', name: 'Экономист филиала', scope_all: false, is_system: true, permissions: ['section:obzor:view', 'export:csv'] },
];

const POSITIONS: PositionOut[] = [

  { id: 4, code: 'economist_filiala', name: 'Экономист филиала', role_id: 4, role_code: 'economist_filiala', is_active: true, branch_ids: [3] },
];

const PERMISSIONS: PermissionOut[] = [

  { code: 'section:kazna:view', description: 'Раздел «Казначейство»: просмотр' },
  { code: 'export:csv', description: 'Выгрузка таблиц в CSV' },
  { code: 'admin:users', description: 'Администрирование: пользователи' },
  { code: 'admin:roles', description: 'Администрирование: должности и роли' },

];

  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implementatsiya — API va yordamchilar**

`frontend/src/admin/api.ts` — fayl oxiriga qo'shing (import qatoriga `useQueryClient` allaqachon bor; tiplar import'iga `AlertRuleOut, AlertRuleUpdate, FinAssumptionItem, FinAssumptionOut, PermissionOut, PositionIn, PositionUpdate, RoleIn, RoleOut, RoleUpdate` ni qo'shing):
```ts
export function useRoles() {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: adminKeys.roles,
    queryFn: ({ signal }) => api<RoleOut[]>('/admin/roles', { signal }),
    staleTime: 0,
  });
}

export function usePermissions() {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: adminKeys.permissions,
    queryFn: ({ signal }) => api<PermissionOut[]>('/admin/permissions', { signal }),
    staleTime: 30 * 60_000,
  });
}

export type SaveRoleInput = { id: null; body: RoleIn } | { id: number; body: RoleUpdate };

export function useSaveRole() {
  const client = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (input: SaveRoleInput) =>
      input.id === null
        ? api<RoleOut>('/admin/roles', { method: 'POST', body: input.body })
        : api<RoleOut>(`/admin/roles/${input.id}`, { method: 'PATCH', body: input.body }),
    onSuccess: () =>
      Promise.all([
        client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: adminKeys.roles }),
        client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: adminKeys.positions }),
      ]),
  });
}

export type SavePositionInput = { id: null; body: PositionIn } | { id: number; body: PositionUpdate };

export function useSavePosition() {
  const invalidate = useInvalidate(adminKeys.positions);
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (input: SavePositionInput) =>
      input.id === null
        ? api<PositionOut>('/admin/positions', { method: 'POST', body: input.body })
        : api<PositionOut>(`/admin/positions/${input.id}`, { method: 'PATCH', body: input.body }),
    onSuccess: invalidate,
  });
}

export function useAlertRules() {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: adminKeys.alertRules,
    queryFn: ({ signal }) => api<AlertRuleOut[]>('/admin/alert-rules', { signal }),
    staleTime: 0,
  });
}

export function useAlertRuleHistory(code: string | null) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: [...adminKeys.alertRules, 'history', code],
    queryFn: ({ signal }) => api<AlertRuleOut[]>(`/admin/alert-rules/${encodeURIComponent(code ?? '')}/history`, { signal }),
    enabled: code !== null,
    staleTime: 0,
  });
}

export function useUpdateAlertRule() {
  const invalidate = useInvalidate(adminKeys.alertRules);
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: ({ code, body }: { code: string; body: AlertRuleUpdate }) =>
      api<AlertRuleOut>(`/admin/alert-rules/${encodeURIComponent(code)}`, { method: 'PATCH', body }),
    onSuccess: invalidate,
  });
}

export function useFinAssumptions() {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: adminKeys.finAssumptions,
    queryFn: ({ signal }) => api<FinAssumptionOut[]>('/admin/fin-assumptions', { signal }),
    staleTime: 0,
  });
}

export function useUpdateFinAssumptions() {
  const invalidate = useInvalidate(adminKeys.finAssumptions);
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (items: FinAssumptionItem[]) =>
      api<FinAssumptionOut[]>('/admin/fin-assumptions', { method: 'PUT', body: { items } }),
    onSuccess: invalidate,
  });
}
```

`frontend/src/admin/numbers.ts`:
```ts
/** Admin formalarida o'nlik sonlar: "0,08" va "0.08" ikkalasi qabul qilinadi. */
export function parseDecimal(text: string): number | null {
  const normalized = text.trim().replace(/\s/g, '').replace('−', '-').replace(',', '.');
  if (!/^-?\d+(\.\d+)?$/.test(normalized)) return null;
  const value = Number(normalized);
  return Number.isFinite(value) ? value : null;
}

export function formatDecimal(value: number): string {
  return String(Number(value.toPrecision(12))).replace('.', ',');
}
```
