`frontend/src/admin/PositionDialog.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useId, useState, type FormEvent } from 'react';
import { isApiError } from '../api/client';
import type { BranchOut, PositionOut, RoleOut } from '../api/types';
import { t } from '../i18n';
import { Button } from '../ui/Button';
import { Checkbox, FormError, TextInput } from '../ui/Field';
import { Modal } from '../ui/Modal';
import { Select } from '../ui/Select';
import { useToast } from '../ui/Toast';
import { useSavePosition } from './api';

export const CODE_RE = /^[a-z][a-z0-9_]{1,63}$/;

interface Props {
  position: PositionOut | null;
  roles: readonly RoleOut[];
  branches: readonly BranchOut[];
  onClose(): void;
}

export function PositionDialog({ position, roles, branches, onClose }: Props) {
  const formId = useId();
  const toast = useToast();
  const save = useSavePosition();
  const [code, setCode] = useState(position?.code ?? '');
  const [name, setName] = useState(position?.name ?? '');
  const [roleId, setRoleId] = useState(String(position?.role_id ?? roles[0]?.id ?? ''));
  const [branchIds, setBranchIds] = useState<number[]>(position?.branch_ids ?? []);
  const [active, setActive] = useState(position?.is_active ?? true);
  const [errors, setErrors] = useState<{ code?: string; name?: string }>({});
  const role = roles.find((r) => String(r.id) === roleId);
  const scopeAll = role?.scope_all ?? false;

  const toggleBranch = (id: number, checked: boolean) =>
    setBranchIds((list) => (checked ? [...list, id].sort((a, b) => a - b) : list.filter((b) => b !== id)));

  const submit = (event: FormEvent) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    const found: typeof errors = {};
    if (!position && !CODE_RE.test(code.trim())) found.code = t('roles.codeRule');
    if (!name.trim()) found.name = t('field.required');
    setErrors(found);
    if (found.code || found.name) return;
    const input =
      position === null
        ? ({ id: null, body: { code: code.trim(), name: name.trim(), role_id: Number(roleId), branch_ids: branchIds } } as const)
        : ({ id: position.id, body: { name: name.trim(), role_id: Number(roleId), branch_ids: branchIds, is_active: active } } as const);
    save.mutate(input, {
      onSuccess: () => {
        toast.show(t('roles.saved'), 'success');
        onClose();
      },
      onError: (error) => {
        if (isApiError(error, 409, 'code_taken')) setErrors({ code: error.message });
      },
    });
  };

  const otherError = save.error && !isApiError(save.error, 409, 'code_taken') ? save.error : null;

  return (
    <Modal
      open
      onClose={onClose}
      title={position ? t('roles.positionEdit', { name: position.name }) : t('roles.positionCreate')}
      footer={
        <>
          <Button variant="secondary" onClick={onClose}>
            {t('common.cancel')}
          </Button>
          <Button type="submit" form={formId} loading={save.isPending}>
            {position ? t('common.save') : t('common.create')}
          </Button>
        </>
      }
    >
      <form id={formId} className="form-grid" noValidate onSubmit={submit}>
        <div className="span-2">
          <FormError error={otherError} />
        </div>
        {position ? null : (
          <TextInput
            label={t('roles.code')}
            hint={t('roles.codeRule')}
            className="mono"
            autoCapitalize="none"
            spellCheck={false}
            required
            value={code}
            error={errors.code}
            onChange={(e) => setCode(e.target.value)}
          />
        )}
        <TextInput label={t('roles.name')} required value={name} error={errors.name} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} />
        <div className="span-2">
          <Select
            label={t('roles.role')}
            value={roleId}
            options={roles.map((r) => ({ value: String(r.id), label: r.name }))}
            onChange={setRoleId}
          />
        </div>
        <fieldset className="fieldset span-2">
          <legend>{t('roles.branches')}</legend>
          {scopeAll ? <p className="note">{t('roles.branchesByRole')}</p> : null}
          {!scopeAll && branchIds.length === 0 ? <p className="note">{t('roles.noBranchesWarn')}</p> : null}
          <div className="checklist">
            {branches.map((branch) => (
              <Checkbox
                key={branch.id}
                label={branch.name}
                checked={branchIds.includes(branch.id)}
                disabled={scopeAll}
                onChange={(checked) => toggleBranch(branch.id, checked)}
              />
            ))}
          </div>
        </fieldset>
        {position ? (
          <div className="span-2">
            <Checkbox label={t('roles.positionActive')} checked={active} onChange={setActive} />
          </div>
        ) : null}
      </form>
    </Modal>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/admin/RoleDialog.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useId, useState, type FormEvent } from 'react';
import { isApiError } from '../api/client';
import type { PermissionOut, RoleOut } from '../api/types';
import { t, type MessageKey } from '../i18n';
import { Button } from '../ui/Button';
import { Checkbox, FormError, TextInput } from '../ui/Field';
import { Modal } from '../ui/Modal';
import { useToast } from '../ui/Toast';
import { useSaveRole } from './api';
import { CODE_RE } from './PositionDialog';

type Group = 'sections' | 'export' | 'admin';
const GROUPS: ReadonlyArray<{ id: Group; titleKey: MessageKey }> = [
  { id: 'sections', titleKey: 'roles.permGroup.sections' },
  { id: 'export', titleKey: 'roles.permGroup.export' },
  { id: 'admin', titleKey: 'roles.permGroup.admin' },
];

function groupOf(code: string): Group {
  if (code.startsWith('section:')) return 'sections';
  if (code.startsWith('export:')) return 'export';
  return 'admin';
}

interface Props {
  role: RoleOut | null;
  permissions: readonly PermissionOut[];
  onClose(): void;
}

export function RoleDialog({ role, permissions, onClose }: Props) {
  const formId = useId();
  const toast = useToast();
  const save = useSaveRole();
  const [code, setCode] = useState(role?.code ?? '');
  const [name, setName] = useState(role?.name ?? '');
  const [scopeAll, setScopeAll] = useState(role?.scope_all ?? true);
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<string[]>(role?.permissions ?? []);
  const [errors, setErrors] = useState<{ code?: string; name?: string }>({});

  const toggle = (perm: string, checked: boolean) =>
    setSelected((list) => (checked ? [...list, perm] : list.filter((p) => p !== perm)));

  const submit = (event: FormEvent) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    const found: typeof errors = {};
    if (!role && !CODE_RE.test(code.trim())) found.code = t('roles.codeRule');
    if (!name.trim()) found.name = t('field.required');
    setErrors(found);
    if (found.code || found.name) return;
    const permissionsSorted = [...selected].sort();
    const input =
      role === null
        ? ({ id: null, body: { code: code.trim(), name: name.trim(), scope_all: scopeAll, permissions: permissionsSorted } } as const)
        : ({ id: role.id, body: { name: name.trim(), scope_all: scopeAll, permissions: permissionsSorted } } as const);
    save.mutate(input, {
      onSuccess: () => {
        toast.show(t('roles.saved'), 'success');
        onClose();
      },
      onError: (error) => {
        if (isApiError(error, 409, 'code_taken')) setErrors({ code: error.message });
      },
    });
  };

  const otherError = save.error && !isApiError(save.error, 409, 'code_taken') ? save.error : null;

  return (
    <Modal
      open
      onClose={onClose}
      title={role ? t('roles.roleEdit', { name: role.name }) : t('roles.roleCreate')}
      footer={
        <>
          <Button variant="secondary" onClick={onClose}>
            {t('common.cancel')}
          </Button>
          <Button type="submit" form={formId} loading={save.isPending}>
            {role ? t('common.save') : t('common.create')}
          </Button>
        </>
      }
    >
      <form id={formId} className="form-grid" noValidate onSubmit={submit}>
        <div className="span-2">
          <FormError error={otherError} />
        </div>
        {role ? (
          role.is_system ? (
            <p className="span-2 note">{t('roles.systemNote')}</p>
          ) : null
        ) : (
          <TextInput
            label={t('roles.code')}
            hint={t('roles.codeRule')}
            autoCapitalize="none"
            spellCheck={false}
            required
            value={code}
            error={errors.code}
            onChange={(e) => setCode(e.target.value)}
          />
        )}
        <TextInput label={t('roles.name')} required value={name} error={errors.name} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} />
        <div className="span-2">
          <Checkbox label={t('roles.scopeAllLabel')} hint={t('roles.scopeAllHint')} checked={scopeAll} onChange={setScopeAll} />
        </div>
        {GROUPS.map((group) => {
          const items = permissions.filter((p) => groupOf(p.code) === group.id);
          if (items.length === 0) return null;
          return (
            <fieldset key={group.id} className="fieldset span-2">
              <legend>{t(group.titleKey)}</legend>
              <div className="checklist">
                {items.map((perm) => (
                  <Checkbox
                    key={perm.code}
                    label={perm.description}
                    checked={selected.includes(perm.code)}
                    onChange={(checked) => toggle(perm.code, checked)}
                  />
                ))}
              </div>
            </fieldset>
          );
        })}
      </form>
    </Modal>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/admin/RolesPage.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useState } from 'react';
import type { PositionOut, RoleOut } from '../api/types';
import { t } from '../i18n';
import { useBranches } from '../meta/api';
import { Badge } from '../ui/Badge';
import { Button } from '../ui/Button';
import { Card } from '../ui/Card';
import { ErrorBanner } from '../ui/ErrorBanner';
import { PageSkeleton } from '../ui/Skeleton';
import { Table, type Column } from '../ui/Table';
import { Tabs } from '../ui/Tabs';
import { usePermissions, usePositions, useRoles } from './api';
import { PositionDialog } from './PositionDialog';
import { RoleDialog } from './RoleDialog';

type Tab = 'positions' | 'roles';

export default function RolesPage() {
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<Tab>('positions');
  return (
    <Card>
      <Tabs
        label={t('roles.tabs')}
        tabs={[
          { id: 'positions', label: t('roles.tab.positions') },
          { id: 'roles', label: t('roles.tab.roles') },
        ]}
        value={tab}
        onChange={(id) => setTab(id === 'roles' ? 'roles' : 'positions')}
      >
        {tab === 'positions' ? <PositionsPanel /> : <RolesPanel />}
      </Tabs>
    </Card>
  );
}

function PositionsPanel() {
  const positions = usePositions();
  const roles = useRoles();
  const branches = useBranches();
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState<PositionOut | 'new' | null>(null);
  const roleById = new Map((roles.data ?? []).map((r) => [r.id, r]));
  const branchName = new Map((branches.data ?? []).map((b) => [b.id, b.short_name]));

  const columns: Column<PositionOut>[] = [
    { key: 'code', header: t('roles.col.code'), align: 'left', sortValue: (p) => p.code, cell: (p) => <span className="mono">{p.code}</span> },
    { key: 'name', header: t('roles.col.name'), align: 'left', wrap: true, sortValue: (p) => p.name, cell: (p) => p.name },
    { key: 'role', header: t('roles.col.role'), align: 'left', cell: (p) => roleById.get(p.role_id)?.name ?? p.role_code },
    {
      key: 'branches',
      header: t('roles.col.branches'),
      align: 'left',
      wrap: true,
      cell: (p) =>
        roleById.get(p.role_id)?.scope_all
          ? t('roles.allByRole')
          : p.branch_ids.length > 0
            ? p.branch_ids.map((id) => branchName.get(id) ?? String(id)).join(', ')
            : t('common.dash'),
    },
    {
      key: 'status',
      header: t('roles.col.status'),
      align: 'left',
      cell: (p) => <Badge tone={p.is_active ? 'ok' : 'neutral'}>{p.is_active ? t('roles.active') : t('roles.inactive')}</Badge>,
    },
  ];

  if (positions.isError) return <ErrorBanner error={positions.error} onRetry={() => void positions.refetch()} />;
  if (positions.isPending || roles.isPending) return <PageSkeleton />;

  return (
    <>
      <div className="toolbar">
        <span className="toolbar__grow" />
        <Button icon="plus" onClick={() => setEditing('new')}>
          {t('roles.addPosition')}
        </Button>
      </div>
      <Table
        caption={t('roles.tab.positions')}
        columns={columns}
        rows={positions.data}
        rowKey={(p) => p.id}
        onRowClick={(p) => setEditing(p)}
        initialSort={{ key: 'name', dir: 'asc' }}
      />
      {editing !== null ? (
        <PositionDialog
          position={editing === 'new' ? null : editing}
          roles={roles.data ?? []}
          branches={branches.data ?? []}
          onClose={() => setEditing(null)}
        />
      ) : null}
    </>
  );
}

function RolesPanel() {
  const roles = useRoles();
  const permissions = usePermissions();
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState<RoleOut | 'new' | null>(null);
  const total = permissions.data?.length ?? 0;

  const columns: Column<RoleOut>[] = [
    { key: 'code', header: t('roles.col.code'), align: 'left', sortValue: (r) => r.code, cell: (r) => <span className="mono">{r.code}</span> },
    { key: 'name', header: t('roles.col.name'), align: 'left', wrap: true, sortValue: (r) => r.name, cell: (r) => r.name },
    { key: 'scope', header: t('roles.col.scope'), align: 'left', cell: (r) => (r.scope_all ? t('roles.scopeAll') : t('roles.scopePosition')) },
    { key: 'perms', header: t('roles.col.perms'), cell: (r) => t('roles.permCount', { count: r.permissions.length, total }) },
    {
      key: 'type',
      header: t('roles.col.type'),
      align: 'left',
      cell: (r) => <Badge tone={r.is_system ? 'blue' : 'neutral'}>{r.is_system ? t('roles.system') : t('roles.custom')}</Badge>,
    },
  ];

  if (roles.isError) return <ErrorBanner error={roles.error} onRetry={() => void roles.refetch()} />;
  if (roles.isPending || permissions.isPending) return <PageSkeleton />;

  return (
    <>
      <div className="toolbar">
        <span className="toolbar__grow" />
        <Button icon="plus" onClick={() => setEditing('new')}>
          {t('roles.addRole')}
        </Button>
      </div>
      <Table caption={t('roles.tab.roles')} columns={columns} rows={roles.data} rowKey={(r) => r.id} onRowClick={(r) => setEditing(r)} />
      {editing !== null ? (
        <RoleDialog role={editing === 'new' ? null : editing} permissions={permissions.data ?? []} onClose={() => setEditing(null)} />
      ) : null}
    </>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { useId, useState, type FormEvent } from 'react';
import type { AlertRuleOut, AlertRuleUpdate } from '../api/types';
import { t } from '../i18n';
import { formatDateTime } from '../i18n/format';
import { sectionTitle } from '../shell/sectionMeta';
import { Badge } from '../ui/Badge';
import { Button } from '../ui/Button';
import { Card } from '../ui/Card';
import { ErrorBanner } from '../ui/ErrorBanner';
import { Checkbox, FormError, TextInput } from '../ui/Field';
import { Modal } from '../ui/Modal';
import { Select } from '../ui/Select';
import { PageSkeleton } from '../ui/Skeleton';
import { Table, type Column } from '../ui/Table';
import { useToast } from '../ui/Toast';
import { useAlertRuleHistory, useAlertRules, useUpdateAlertRule } from './api';
import { formatDecimal, parseDecimal } from './numbers';

type Op = '<' | '<=' | '>' | '>=';
type Level = 'critical' | 'warning';
const OPS: ReadonlyArray<{ value: Op; label: string }> = [
  { value: '<', label: t('alerts.op.lt') },
  { value: '<=', label: t('alerts.op.le') },
  { value: '>', label: t('alerts.op.gt') },
  { value: '>=', label: t('alerts.op.ge') },
];
const OP_SIGN: Record<Op, string> = { '<': '<', '<=': '≤', '>': '>', '>=': '≥' };

interface Threshold {
  metric: string;
  op: Op;
  value: number | null;
}

function readThreshold(rule: AlertRuleOut): Threshold {
  const raw = rule.threshold as Record<string, unknown>;
  const op = OPS.some((o) => o.value === raw.op) ? (raw.op as Op) : '<';
  return {
    metric: typeof raw.metric === 'string' ? raw.metric : rule.code,
    op,
    value: typeof raw.value === 'number' ? raw.value : null,
  };
}

function condition(rule: AlertRuleOut): string {
  const th = readThreshold(rule);
  return `${th.metric} ${OP_SIGN[th.op]} ${th.value === null ? '—' : formatDecimal(th.value)}`;
}

function levelBadge(level: string) {
  return level === 'critical' ? <Badge tone="neg">{t('alerts.level.critical')}</Badge> : <Badge tone="warn">{t('alerts.level.warning')}</Badge>;
}

function enabledBadge(enabled: boolean) {
  return <Badge tone={enabled ? 'ok' : 'neutral'}>{enabled ? t('common.yes') : t('common.no')}</Badge>;
}

export default function AlertRulesPage() {
  const rules = useAlertRules();
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState<AlertRuleOut | null>(null);

  const columns: Column<AlertRuleOut>[] = [
    {
      key: 'rule',
      header: t('alerts.col.rule'),
      align: 'left',
      wrap: true,
      sortValue: (r) => r.title,
      cell: (r) => (
        <span className="cell-main">
          <b>{r.title}</b>
          <small className="mono">{r.code}</small>
        </span>
      ),
    },
    { key: 'where', header: t('alerts.col.where'), align: 'left', sortValue: (r) => r.section, cell: (r) => `${sectionTitle(r.section)} / ${r.page}` },
    { key: 'condition', header: t('alerts.col.condition'), align: 'left', cell: (r) => <span className="mono">{condition(r)}</span> },
    { key: 'level', header: t('alerts.col.level'), align: 'left', sortValue: (r) => r.level, cell: (r) => levelBadge(r.level) },
    { key: 'enabled', header: t('alerts.col.enabled'), align: 'left', cell: (r) => enabledBadge(r.enabled) },
    { key: 'version', header: t('alerts.col.version'), sortValue: (r) => r.version, cell: (r) => r.version },
    { key: 'updated', header: t('alerts.col.updated'), sortValue: (r) => r.updated_at, cell: (r) => formatDateTime(r.updated_at) },
  ];

  if (rules.isError) return <ErrorBanner error={rules.error} lastSuccessAt={rules.dataUpdatedAt} onRetry={() => void rules.refetch()} />;
  if (rules.isPending) return <PageSkeleton />;

  return (
    <Card>
      <Table
        caption={t('admin.page.alerts')}
        columns={columns}
        rows={rules.data}
        rowKey={(r) => r.code}
        onRowClick={setEditing}
        initialSort={{ key: 'where', dir: 'asc' }}
        free
      />
      {editing ? <AlertRuleDialog rule={editing} onClose={() => setEditing(null)} /> : null}
    </Card>
  );
}

function AlertRuleDialog({ rule, onClose }: { rule: AlertRuleOut; onClose(): void }) {
  const formId = useId();
  const toast = useToast();
  const update = useUpdateAlertRule();
  const history = useAlertRuleHistory(rule.code);
  const initial = readThreshold(rule);
  const [value, setValue] = useState(initial.value === null ? '' : formatDecimal(initial.value));
  const [op, setOp] = useState<Op>(initial.op);
  const [level, setLevel] = useState<Level>(rule.level === 'critical' ? 'critical' : 'warning');
  const [enabled, setEnabled] = useState(rule.enabled);
  const [valueError, setValueError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const submit = (event: FormEvent) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    const parsed = parseDecimal(value);
    if (parsed === null) {
      setValueError(t('alerts.valueInvalid'));
      return;
    }
    setValueError(null);
    const body: AlertRuleUpdate = { value: parsed, op, level, enabled };
    update.mutate(
      { code: rule.code, body },
      {
        onSuccess: () => {
          toast.show(t('alerts.saved'), 'success');
          onClose();
        },
      },
    );
  };

  const historyColumns: Column<AlertRuleOut>[] = [
    { key: 'version', header: t('alerts.col.version'), cell: (r) => r.version },
    { key: 'condition', header: t('alerts.col.condition'), align: 'left', cell: (r) => <span className="mono">{condition(r)}</span> },
    { key: 'level', header: t('alerts.col.level'), align: 'left', cell: (r) => levelBadge(r.level) },
    { key: 'enabled', header: t('alerts.col.enabled'), align: 'left', cell: (r) => enabledBadge(r.enabled) },
    { key: 'updated', header: t('alerts.col.updated'), cell: (r) => formatDateTime(r.updated_at) },
  ];

  return (
    <Modal
      open
      onClose={onClose}
      title={t('alerts.editTitle', { title: rule.title })}
      note={`${sectionTitle(rule.section)} / ${rule.page} · ${initial.metric}`}
      footer={
        <>
          <Button variant="secondary" onClick={onClose}>
            {t('common.cancel')}
          </Button>
          <Button type="submit" form={formId} loading={update.isPending}>
            {t('common.save')}
          </Button>
        </>
      }
    >
      <form id={formId} className="form-grid" noValidate onSubmit={submit}>
        <div className="span-2">
          <FormError error={update.error} />
        </div>
        <Select label={t('alerts.op')} value={op} options={OPS} onChange={(v) => setOp(OPS.find((o) => o.value === v)?.value ?? '<')} />
        <TextInput
          label={t('alerts.value')}
          hint={t('alerts.valueHint')}
          inputMode="decimal"
          value={value}
          error={valueError}
          onChange={(e) => setValue(e.target.value)}
        />
        <Select
          label={t('alerts.level')}
          value={level}
          options={[
            { value: 'critical', label: t('alerts.level.critical') },
            { value: 'warning', label: t('alerts.level.warning') },
          ]}
          onChange={(v) => setLevel(v === 'critical' ? 'critical' : 'warning')}
        />
        <div className="span-2">
          <Checkbox label={t('alerts.enabled')} checked={enabled} onChange={setEnabled} />
        </div>
      </form>
      <section aria-label={t('alerts.history')}>
        {history.data ? (
          <Table
            caption={t('alerts.history')}
            columns={historyColumns}
            rows={history.data}
            rowKey={(r) => r.version}
            initialSort={{ key: 'version', dir: 'desc' }}
            mobile="scroll"
          />
        ) : null}
      </section>
    </Modal>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/admin/FinAssumptionsPage.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import type { FinAssumptionItem, FinAssumptionOut } from '../api/types';
import { t, type MessageKey } from '../i18n';
import { Button } from '../ui/Button';
import { Card } from '../ui/Card';
import { ErrorBanner } from '../ui/ErrorBanner';
import { FormError } from '../ui/Field';
import { Icon } from '../ui/icons';
import { PageSkeleton } from '../ui/Skeleton';
import { Tabs } from '../ui/Tabs';
import { useToast } from '../ui/Toast';
import { useFinAssumptions, useUpdateFinAssumptions } from './api';
import { formatDecimal, parseDecimal } from './numbers';

type Scenario = FinAssumptionItem['scenario'];

const SCENARIOS: ReadonlyArray<{ id: Scenario; titleKey: MessageKey }> = [
  { id: 'base', titleKey: 'fin.scenario.base' },
  { id: 'water', titleKey: 'fin.scenario.water' },
  { id: 'tariff', titleKey: 'fin.scenario.tariff' },
  { id: 'fx', titleKey: 'fin.scenario.fx' },
];

const KEY_TITLES: Record<string, MessageKey> = {
  tariff_growth: 'fin.key.tariff_growth',
  generation_factor: 'fin.key.generation_factor',
  fx_rate_factor: 'fin.key.fx_rate_factor',
  opex_index: 'fin.key.opex_index',
  tax_rate: 'fin.key.tax_rate',
  kium: 'fin.key.kium',
};

function keyTitle(key: string): string {
  const titleKey = KEY_TITLES[key];
  return titleKey ? t(titleKey) : key;
}

function cellId(scenario: string, year: number, key: string): string {
  return `${scenario}|${year}|${key}`;
}

export default function FinAssumptionsPage() {
  const data = useFinAssumptions();
  const update = useUpdateFinAssumptions();
  const toast = useToast();
  const [scenario, setScenario] = useState<Scenario>('base');
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [invalid, setInvalid] = useState<string[]>([]);

  const index = useMemo(() => {
    const map = new Map<string, FinAssumptionOut>();
    for (const row of data.data ?? []) map.set(cellId(row.scenario, row.year, row.key), row);
    return map;
  }, [data.data]);
  const years = useMemo(() => [...new Set((data.data ?? []).map((r) => r.year))].sort((a, b) => a - b), [data.data]);
  const keys = useMemo(() => {
    const present = new Set((data.data ?? []).map((r) => r.key));
    return [...Object.keys(KEY_TITLES).filter((k) => present.has(k)), ...[...present].filter((k) => !(k in KEY_TITLES))];
  }, [data.data]);

  const changed = Object.entries(draft).filter(([id, text]) => {
    const original = index.get(id);
    const parsed = parseDecimal(text);
    return original === undefined || parsed === null || parsed !== original.value;
  });

  const scenarioTitle = (id: string) => t(SCENARIOS.find((s) => s.id === id)?.titleKey ?? 'fin.scenario.base');

  const save = () => {
    const items: FinAssumptionItem[] = [];
    const bad: string[] = [];
    for (const [id, text] of changed) {
      const [sc, yearText, key] = id.split('|') as [Scenario, string, string];
      const value = parseDecimal(text);
      if (value === null || value < -10 || value > 10) bad.push(`${scenarioTitle(sc)} · ${keyTitle(key)} · ${yearText}`);
      else items.push({ scenario: sc, year: Number(yearText), key, value });
    }
    setInvalid(bad);
    if (bad.length > 0 || items.length === 0) return;
    update.mutate(items, {
      onSuccess: () => {
        toast.show(t('fin.saved'), 'success');
        setDraft({});
      },
    });
  };

  if (data.isError) return <ErrorBanner error={data.error} lastSuccessAt={data.dataUpdatedAt} onRetry={() => void data.refetch()} />;
  if (data.isPending) return <PageSkeleton />;

  return (
    <Card>
      <Tabs
        label={t('fin.scenarios')}
        tabs={SCENARIOS.map((s) => ({ id: s.id, label: t(s.titleKey) }))}
        value={scenario}
        onChange={(id) => setScenario(SCENARIOS.find((s) => s.id === id)?.id ?? 'base')}
      >
        <p className="note">{t('fin.hint')}</p>
        <div className="tw tw--free">
          <table className="table matrix">
            <caption className="visually-hidden">{t(SCENARIOS.find((s) => s.id === scenario)?.titleKey ?? 'fin.scenario.base')}</caption>
            <thead>
              <tr>
                <th scope="col" className="l">
                  {t('fin.col.key')}
                </th>
                {years.map((year) => (
                  <th key={year} scope="col">
                    {year}
                  </th>
                ))}
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {keys.map((key) => (
                <tr key={key}>
                  <th scope="row" className="l">
                    {keyTitle(key)}
                  </th>
                  {years.map((year) => {
                    const id = cellId(scenario, year, key);
                    const original = index.get(id);
                    if (!original) return <td key={year}>{t('common.dash')}</td>;
                    return (
                      <td key={year}>
                        <input
                          className="input"
                          inputMode="decimal"
                          aria-label={`${keyTitle(key)}, ${year}`}
                          value={draft[id] ?? formatDecimal(original.value)}
                          onChange={(e) => setDraft((d) => ({ ...d, [id]: e.target.value }))}
                        />
                      </td>
                    );
                  })}
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </Tabs>
      {invalid.length > 0 ? (
        <p className="form-error" role="alert">
          <Icon name="alert" />
          <span>{t('fin.invalid', { cells: invalid.join('; ') })}</span>
        </p>
      ) : null}
      <FormError error={update.error} />
      <div className="toolbar">
        <span className="note">{t('fin.changed', { count: changed.length })}</span>
        <span className="toolbar__grow" />
        <Button variant="secondary" disabled={changed.length === 0} onClick={() => setDraft({})}>
          {t('fin.reset')}
        </Button>
        <Button disabled={changed.length === 0} loading={update.isPending} onClick={save}>
          {t('fin.save')}
        </Button>
      </div>
    </Card>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/admin/ops.test.tsx`:
```tsx
import { screen, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import type { BuildOut, EtlRunDetail, EtlRunOut, KeyOut } from '../api/types';
import { expectNoAxeViolations } from '../test/axe';
import { apiError, json, mockApi } from '../test/mockApi';
import { renderWithProviders } from '../test/render';
import BuildsPage from './BuildsPage';
import EtlPage from './EtlPage';
import KeysPage from './KeysPage';

const RUNS: EtlRunOut[] = [
  {
    id: 3, source: 'synthetic', mode: 'full', status: 'failed_quality',
    started_at: '2026-09-24T02:00:00Z', finished_at: '2026-09-24T02:04:05Z',
    window_from: '2024-01-01', window_to: '2026-09-24', rows_in: 120000, rows_upserted: 119500,
    build_id: null, triggered_by: null, error: 'Проверка качества не пройдена',
  },
  {
    id: 2, source: 'file', mode: 'incremental', status: 'success',
    started_at: '2026-09-23T10:00:00Z', finished_at: '2026-09-23T10:00:45Z',
    window_from: '2026-08-09', window_to: '2026-09-23', rows_in: 3000, rows_upserted: 2990,
    build_id: 11, triggered_by: 1, error: null,
  },
];

const DETAIL: EtlRunDetail = {
  run: RUNS[0] as EtlRunOut,
  steps: [
    { name: 'extract', status: 'success', started_at: '2026-09-24T02:00:00Z', finished_at: '2026-09-24T02:02:00Z', rows: 120000, detail: {} },
    { name: 'quality', status: 'failed', started_at: '2026-09-24T02:03:00Z', finished_at: '2026-09-24T02:04:05Z', rows: 0, detail: {} },
  ],
  errors: [
    { step: 'quality', severity: 'error', target: 'fact_balance', src_id: null, code: 'balance_mismatch', message: 'Остаток на конец месяца не сходится', created_at: '2026-09-24T02:04:00Z' },
  ],
};

describe('EtlPage', () => {
  it('lists runs with statuses and durations and opens the details', async () => {
    mockApi({ 'GET /admin/etl/runs': json({ items: RUNS, total: 2, page: 1, page_size: 50 }), 'GET /admin/etl/runs/3': json(DETAIL) });
    const { user } = renderWithProviders(<EtlPage />);
    const table = await screen.findByRole('table', { name: 'История загрузок' });
    expect(table).toHaveTextContent('Ошибка проверки качества');
    expect(table).toHaveTextContent('4 мин 05 с');
    expect(table).toHaveTextContent('Синтетические данные');
    await user.click(within(table).getByText('Ошибка проверки качества'));
    const dialog = screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Загрузка №3' });
    expect(await within(dialog).findByText('Остаток на конец месяца не сходится')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(dialog).getByRole('table', { name: 'Шаги' })).toHaveTextContent('Проверки качества');
  });

  it('queues a run and shows server refusals', async () => {
    let call = 0;
    const server = mockApi({
      'GET /admin/etl/runs': json({ items: [], total: 0, page: 1, page_size: 50 }),
      'POST /admin/etl/run': () => {
        call += 1;
        return call === 1
          ? json({ request_id: 5, status: 'pending' }, 202)
          : apiError(422, 'source_forbidden', 'Этот источник недоступен в рабочей среде');
      },
    });
    const { user } = renderWithProviders(<EtlPage />);
    await user.selectOptions(await screen.findByLabelText('Источник'), 'file');
    await user.selectOptions(screen.getByLabelText('Режим'), 'full');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Запустить' }));
    expect(await screen.findByText('Запрос №5 поставлен в очередь')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(server.calls.find((c) => c.method === 'POST')?.body).toEqual({ source: 'file', mode: 'full' });
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Запустить' }));
    expect(await screen.findByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent('Этот источник недоступен в рабочей среде');
  });
});

const BUILDS: BuildOut[] = [
  { build_id: 12, created_at: '2026-09-24T02:05:00Z', status: 'published', load_id: 40, published_at: '2026-09-24T02:06:00Z', active: true },
  { build_id: 11, created_at: '2026-09-23T10:01:00Z', status: 'superseded', load_id: 39, published_at: '2026-09-23T10:02:00Z', active: false },
  { build_id: 10, created_at: '2026-09-22T02:05:00Z', status: 'failed', load_id: 38, published_at: null, active: false },
];

describe('BuildsPage', () => {
  it('makes an older build active after confirmation', async () => {
    const server = mockApi({
      'GET /admin/builds': json(BUILDS),
      'POST /admin/builds/rollback': json({ ...BUILDS[1], status: 'published', active: true }),
    });
    const { user } = renderWithProviders(<BuildsPage />);
    const table = await screen.findByRole('table', { name: 'Сборки витрины' });
    expect(within(table).getAllByRole('button', { name: 'Сделать активной' })).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(table).toHaveTextContent('Активная');
    await user.click(within(table).getByRole('button', { name: 'Сделать активной' }));
    const dialog = screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Сменить активную сборку?' });
    expect(dialog).toHaveTextContent('№11');
    await user.click(within(dialog).getByRole('button', { name: 'Сделать активной' }));
    expect(await screen.findByText('Активная сборка: №11')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(server.calls.find((c) => c.method === 'POST')?.body).toEqual({ build_id: 11 });
  });
});

const KEYS: KeyOut[] = [
  { purpose: 'mfa', version: 2, status: 'active', created_at: '2026-09-20T00:00:00Z', retired_at: null },
  { purpose: 'mfa', version: 1, status: 'decrypt_only', created_at: '2026-09-01T00:00:00Z', retired_at: null },
  { purpose: 'pii', version: 1, status: 'active', created_at: '2026-09-01T00:00:00Z', retired_at: null },
];

describe('KeysPage', () => {
  it('shows key versions and rotates a purpose', async () => {
    const server = mockApi({
      'GET /admin/keys': json(KEYS),
      'POST /admin/keys/mfa/rotate': json({ purpose: 'mfa', version: 3, reencrypted: 42 }),
    });
    const { user } = renderWithProviders(<KeysPage />);
    const table = await screen.findByRole('table', { name: 'Ключи шифрования' });
    expect(table).toHaveTextContent('Только расшифровка');
    expect(screen.getByText(/python -m app.cli retire-keys/)).toBeInTheDocument();
    const mfaRow = within(table).getAllByRole('row').find((r) => r.textContent?.includes('Секреты 2FA') && r.textContent.includes('Активный')) as HTMLElement;
    await user.click(within(mfaRow).getByRole('button', { name: 'Ротировать' }));
    const dialog = screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Ротировать ключ «Секреты 2FA»?' });
    await user.click(within(dialog).getByRole('button', { name: 'Ротировать' }));
    expect(await screen.findByText('«Секреты 2FA»: новая версия 3, перешифровано записей: 42')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(server.calls.some((c) => c.method === 'POST' && c.path === '/admin/keys/mfa/rotate')).toBe(true);
  });
});

it('has no axe violations on ETL, builds and keys pages', async () => {
  mockApi({
    'GET /admin/etl/runs': json({ items: RUNS, total: 2, page: 1, page_size: 50 }),
    'GET /admin/builds': json(BUILDS),
    'GET /admin/keys': json(KEYS),
  });
  const { container } = renderWithProviders(
    <>
      <EtlPage />
      <BuildsPage />
      <KeysPage />
    </>,
  );
  await screen.findByRole('table', { name: 'История загрузок' });
  await screen.findByRole('table', { name: 'Сборки витрины' });
  await screen.findByRole('table', { name: 'Ключи шифрования' });
  await expectNoAxeViolations(container);
});
```

`frontend/src/admin/audit.test.tsx`:
```tsx
import { fireEvent, screen, waitFor, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import type { AuditOut } from '../api/types';
import { expectNoAxeViolations } from '../test/axe';
import { json, mockApi, type MockHandler } from '../test/mockApi';
import { renderWithProviders } from '../test/render';
import AuditPage from './AuditPage';

const ROWS: AuditOut[] = [
  {
    id: 101, ts: '2026-09-24T05:07:00Z', user_id: 1, username: 'admin', action: 'auth.login.success', result: 'ok',
    object_type: null, object_id: null, request_id: 'r1', details: { method: 'totp' }, ip: '10.0.0.5',
  },
  {
    id: 100, ts: '2026-09-24T05:06:00Z', user_id: null, username: null, action: 'auth.login.fail', result: 'fail',
    object_type: 'user', object_id: 'ghost', request_id: 'r0', details: { reason: 'bad_password' }, ip: null,
  },
];

function routes(extra: Record<string, MockHandler> = {}) {
  return mockApi({ 'GET /audit': json({ items: ROWS, total: 2, page: 1, page_size: 100 }), ...extra });
}

describe('AuditPage', () => {
  it('lists records and shows details', async () => {
    routes();
    const { user } = renderWithProviders(<AuditPage />);
    const table = await screen.findByRole('table', { name: 'Журнал аудита' });
    expect(table).toHaveTextContent('24.09.2026 10:07');
    expect(table).toHaveTextContent('auth.login.fail');
    expect(table).toHaveTextContent('Ошибка');
    expect(table).toHaveTextContent('user:ghost');
    await user.click(within(table).getByText('auth.login.success'));
    const dialog = screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Запись журнала №101' });
    expect(dialog).toHaveTextContent('"method": "totp"');
  });

  it('applies filters to the query and the CSV link', async () => {
    const server = routes();
    const { user } = renderWithProviders(<AuditPage />);
    await screen.findByRole('table', { name: 'Журнал аудита' });
    fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText('С'), { target: { value: '2026-09-01' } });
    fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText('По'), { target: { value: '2026-09-24' } });
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Действие (начало кода)'), 'auth.login');
    await user.selectOptions(screen.getByLabelText('Результат'), 'fail');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Применить' }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(server.calls.some((c) => c.url.searchParams.get('action') === 'auth.login')).toBe(true));
    const call = server.calls.filter((c) => c.path === '/audit').at(-1);
    expect(call?.url.searchParams.get('from_ts')).toBe('2026-08-31T19:00:00.000Z');
    expect(call?.url.searchParams.get('to_ts')).toBe('2026-09-24T19:00:00.000Z');
    expect(call?.url.searchParams.get('result')).toBe('fail');
    const link = screen.getByRole('link', { name: 'Выгрузить CSV' });
    expect(link.getAttribute('href')).toContain('/api/v1/audit/export.csv?');
    expect(link.getAttribute('href')).toContain('action=auth.login');
    expect(link.getAttribute('href')).not.toContain('page=');
  });

  it('validates filters before querying', async () => {
    const server = routes();
    const { user } = renderWithProviders(<AuditPage />);
    await screen.findByRole('table', { name: 'Журнал аудита' });
    const before = server.calls.length;
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Действие (начало кода)'), 'Auth Login');
    fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText('С'), { target: { value: '2026-09-24' } });
    fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText('По'), { target: { value: '2026-09-01' } });
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Применить' }));
    expect(screen.getByText('Латинские строчные буквы, точка и «_»', { selector: '.field__error' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Дата «С» позже даты «По»')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(server.calls.length).toBe(before);
  });

  it('verifies the hash chain', async () => {
    let call = 0;
    routes({
      'POST /audit/verify': () => {
        call += 1;
        return call === 1
          ? json({ ok: true, checked: 2, first_bad_id: null, bad_ids: [] })
          : json({ ok: false, checked: 10, first_bad_id: 7, bad_ids: [7, 8] });
      },
    });
    const { user } = renderWithProviders(<AuditPage />);
    await user.click(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'Проверить целостность' }));
    expect(await screen.findByText('Цепочка записей не нарушена. Проверено записей: 2.')).toBeInTheDocument();
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Проверить целостность' }));
    const alert = await screen.findByRole('alert');
    expect(alert).toHaveTextContent('первая изменённая запись №7');
    expect(alert).toHaveTextContent('Записи с нарушениями: 7, 8');
  });

  it('has no axe violations', async () => {
    routes();
    const { container } = renderWithProviders(<AuditPage />);
    await screen.findByRole('table', { name: 'Журнал аудита' });
    await expectNoAxeViolations(container);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implementatsiya — API va yorliqlar**

`frontend/src/admin/api.ts` — fayl oxiriga qo'shing (import'larga `apiUrl` ni emas, `type Query` ni `../api/client` dan; tiplarga `AuditOut, BuildOut, EtlRequestOut, EtlRunDetail, EtlRunOut, EtlRunRequest, KeyOut, RotateOut, VerifyOut`; `dayStartIso` ni `../i18n/format` dan qo'shing):
```ts
export const ETL_PAGE_SIZE = 50;
export const AUDIT_PAGE_SIZE = 100;

export function useEtlRuns(page: number) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: [...adminKeys.etlRuns, page],
    queryFn: ({ signal }) => api<Page<EtlRunOut>>('/admin/etl/runs', { signal, query: { page, page_size: ETL_PAGE_SIZE } }),
    placeholderData: keepPreviousData,
    staleTime: 0,
    refetchInterval: (query) => (query.state.data?.items.some((run) => run.status === 'running') ? 15_000 : false),
  });
}

export function useEtlRun(id: number | null) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: [...adminKeys.etlRuns, 'detail', id],
    queryFn: ({ signal }) => api<EtlRunDetail>(`/admin/etl/runs/${id ?? 0}`, { signal }),
    enabled: id !== null,
    staleTime: 0,
  });
}

export function useRequestEtlRun() {
  const invalidate = useInvalidate(adminKeys.etlRuns);
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (body: EtlRunRequest) => api<EtlRequestOut>('/admin/etl/run', { method: 'POST', body }),
    onSuccess: invalidate,
  });
}

export function useBuilds() {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: adminKeys.builds,
    queryFn: ({ signal }) => api<BuildOut[]>('/admin/builds', { signal }),
    staleTime: 0,
  });
}

export function useRollbackBuild() {
  const client = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (buildId: number | null) => api<BuildOut>('/admin/builds/rollback', { method: 'POST', body: { build_id: buildId } }),
    onSuccess: () => client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: adminKeys.builds }),
  });
}

export interface AuditFilters {
  from: string;
  to: string;
  userId: string;
  action: string;
  result: '' | 'ok' | 'fail' | 'denied';
}

export const EMPTY_AUDIT_FILTERS: AuditFilters = { from: '', to: '', userId: '', action: '', result: '' };

/** Sana — Toshkent kuni; "По" kuni ham kiradi (to_ts — keyingi kun boshi, qat'iy "<"). */
export function auditQuery(filters: AuditFilters): Query {
  return {
    from_ts: filters.from ? dayStartIso(filters.from) : undefined,
    to_ts: filters.to ? dayStartIso(filters.to, 1) : undefined,
    user_id: filters.userId || undefined,
    action: filters.action || undefined,
    result: filters.result || undefined,
  };
}

export function useAudit(filters: AuditFilters, page: number) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: [...adminKeys.audit, filters, page],
    queryFn: ({ signal }) =>
      api<Page<AuditOut>>('/audit', { signal, query: { ...auditQuery(filters), page, page_size: AUDIT_PAGE_SIZE } }),
    placeholderData: keepPreviousData,
    staleTime: 0,
  });
}

export function useVerifyAudit() {
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: () => api<VerifyOut>('/audit/verify', { method: 'POST' }),
  });
}

export function useKeys() {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: adminKeys.keys,
    queryFn: ({ signal }) => api<KeyOut[]>('/admin/keys', { signal }),
    staleTime: 0,
  });
}

export function useRotateKey() {
  const invalidate = useInvalidate(adminKeys.keys);
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (purpose: string) => api<RotateOut>(`/admin/keys/${encodeURIComponent(purpose)}/rotate`, { method: 'POST' }),
    onSuccess: invalidate,
  });
}
```

`frontend/src/admin/labels.ts` — fayl oxiriga qo'shing (import'larga `type MessageKey` ni `../i18n` dan qo'shing):
```ts
type Tone = { tone: BadgeTone; label: string };

function pick(map: Record<string, [BadgeTone, MessageKey]>, key: string): Tone {
  const entry = map[key];
  return entry ? { tone: entry[0], label: t(entry[1]) } : { tone: 'neutral', label: key };
}

function text(map: Record<string, MessageKey>, key: string): string {
  const entry = map[key];
  return entry ? t(entry) : key;
}

export function runStatus(status: string): Tone {
  return pick(
    {
      running: ['blue', 'etl.status.running'],
      success: ['ok', 'etl.status.success'],
      failed: ['neg', 'etl.status.failed'],
      failed_quality: ['neg', 'etl.status.failed_quality'],
    },
    status,
  );
}

export function buildStatus(status: string): Tone {
  return pick(
    {
      building: ['blue', 'builds.status.building'],
      ready: ['neutral', 'builds.status.ready'],
      published: ['ok', 'builds.status.published'],
      superseded: ['neutral', 'builds.status.superseded'],
      failed: ['neg', 'builds.status.failed'],
    },
    status,
  );
}

export function keyStatus(status: string): Tone {
  return pick(
    {
      active: ['ok', 'keys.status.active'],
      decrypt_only: ['warn', 'keys.status.decrypt_only'],
      retired: ['neutral', 'keys.status.retired'],
    },
    status,
  );
}

export function auditResult(result: string): Tone {
  return pick(
    { ok: ['ok', 'audit.result.ok'], fail: ['neg', 'audit.result.fail'], denied: ['warn', 'audit.result.denied'] },
    result,
  );
}

export function stepLabel(name: string): string {
  return text(
    {
      extract: 'etl.step.extract',
      dims: 'etl.step.dims',
      facts: 'etl.step.facts',
      aggregates: 'etl.step.aggregates',
      quality: 'etl.step.quality',
      publish: 'etl.step.publish',
    },
    name,
  );
}

export function sourceLabel(source: string): string {
  return text({ synthetic: 'etl.source.synthetic', file: 'etl.source.file' }, source);
}

export function modeLabel(mode: string): string {
  return text({ full: 'etl.mode.full', incremental: 'etl.mode.incremental' }, mode);
}

export function severityLabel(severity: string): string {
  return text({ error: 'etl.severity.error', warning: 'etl.severity.warning' }, severity);
}

export function purposeLabel(purpose: string): string {
  return text(
    {
      pii: 'keys.purpose.pii',
      bank: 'keys.purpose.bank',
      mfa: 'keys.purpose.mfa',
      integration_creds: 'keys.purpose.integration_creds',
      audit_meta: 'keys.purpose.audit_meta',
    },
    purpose,
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/admin/EtlPage.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react';
import type { EtlErrorOut, EtlRunOut, EtlStepOut } from '../api/types';
import { t } from '../i18n';
import { formatDate, formatDateTime, formatDuration, formatNumber } from '../i18n/format';
import { Badge } from '../ui/Badge';
import { Button } from '../ui/Button';
import { Card } from '../ui/Card';
import { ErrorBanner } from '../ui/ErrorBanner';
import { FormError } from '../ui/Field';
import { Modal } from '../ui/Modal';
import { Pagination } from '../ui/Pagination';
import { Select } from '../ui/Select';
import { PageSkeleton } from '../ui/Skeleton';
import { Table, type Column } from '../ui/Table';
import { useToast } from '../ui/Toast';
import { ETL_PAGE_SIZE, useEtlRun, useEtlRuns, useRequestEtlRun } from './api';
import { modeLabel, runStatus, severityLabel, sourceLabel, stepLabel } from './labels';

type Mode = 'full' | 'incremental';

function duration(started: string, finished: string | null): string {
  if (!finished) return t('etl.inProgress');
  return formatDuration(Date.parse(finished) - Date.parse(started));
}

export default function EtlPage() {
  const toast = useToast();
  const [page, setPage] = useState(1);
  const runs = useEtlRuns(page);
  const request = useRequestEtlRun();
  const [source, setSource] = useState('synthetic');
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<Mode>('incremental');
  const [openId, setOpenId] = useState<number | null>(null);

  const submit = (event: FormEvent) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    request.mutate(
      { source, mode },
      { onSuccess: (result) => toast.show(t('etl.queued', { id: result.request_id }), 'success') },
    );
  };

  const columns: Column<EtlRunOut>[] = [
    { key: 'id', header: t('etl.col.id'), sortValue: (r) => r.id, cell: (r) => r.id },
    { key: 'source', header: t('etl.col.source'), align: 'left', cell: (r) => sourceLabel(r.source) },
    { key: 'mode', header: t('etl.col.mode'), align: 'left', cell: (r) => modeLabel(r.mode) },
    {
      key: 'status',
      header: t('etl.col.status'),
      align: 'left',
      cell: (r) => {
        const status = runStatus(r.status);
        return <Badge tone={status.tone}>{status.label}</Badge>;
      },
    },
    { key: 'started', header: t('etl.col.started'), sortValue: (r) => r.started_at, cell: (r) => formatDateTime(r.started_at) },
    { key: 'duration', header: t('etl.col.duration'), cell: (r) => duration(r.started_at, r.finished_at) },
    { key: 'rows', header: t('etl.col.rows'), cell: (r) => `${formatNumber(r.rows_in)} / ${formatNumber(r.rows_upserted)}` },
    { key: 'build', header: t('etl.col.build'), cell: (r) => (r.build_id === null ? t('common.dash') : `№${r.build_id}`) },
  ];

  return (
    <>
      <Card title={t('etl.runTitle')} sub={t('etl.runSub')}>
        <form className="toolbar" onSubmit={submit}>
          <Select
            label={t('etl.source')}
            value={source}
            options={[
              { value: 'synthetic', label: t('etl.source.synthetic') },
              { value: 'file', label: t('etl.source.file') },
            ]}
            onChange={setSource}
          />
          <Select
            label={t('etl.mode')}
            value={mode}
            options={[
              { value: 'incremental', label: t('etl.mode.incremental') },
              { value: 'full', label: t('etl.mode.full') },
            ]}
            onChange={(value) => setMode(value === 'full' ? 'full' : 'incremental')}
          />
          <Button type="submit" icon="refresh" loading={request.isPending}>
            {t('etl.start')}
          </Button>
        </form>
        <FormError error={request.error} />
      </Card>
      <Card title={t('etl.historyTitle')}>
        {runs.isError ? (
          <ErrorBanner error={runs.error} lastSuccessAt={runs.dataUpdatedAt} onRetry={() => void runs.refetch()} />
        ) : runs.isPending ? (
          <PageSkeleton />
        ) : (
          <>
            <Table
              caption={t('etl.historyTitle')}
              columns={columns}
              rows={runs.data.items}
              rowKey={(r) => r.id}
              onRowClick={(r) => setOpenId(r.id)}
            />
            <Pagination page={page} pageSize={ETL_PAGE_SIZE} total={runs.data.total} onPage={setPage} />
          </>
        )}
      </Card>
      {openId !== null ? <RunDialog id={openId} onClose={() => setOpenId(null)} /> : null}
    </>
  );
}

function RunDialog({ id, onClose }: { id: number; onClose(): void }) {
  const detail = useEtlRun(id);
  const stepColumns: Column<EtlStepOut>[] = [
    { key: 'name', header: t('etl.col.step'), align: 'left', cell: (s) => stepLabel(s.name) },
    {
      key: 'status',
      header: t('etl.col.status'),
      align: 'left',
      cell: (s) => {
        const status = runStatus(s.status);
        return <Badge tone={status.tone}>{status.label}</Badge>;
      },
    },
    { key: 'started', header: t('etl.col.started'), cell: (s) => formatDateTime(s.started_at) },
    { key: 'duration', header: t('etl.col.duration'), cell: (s) => duration(s.started_at, s.finished_at) },
    { key: 'rows', header: t('etl.col.rows'), cell: (s) => formatNumber(s.rows) },
  ];
  const errorColumns: Column<EtlErrorOut>[] = [
    { key: 'step', header: t('etl.col.step'), align: 'left', cell: (e) => stepLabel(e.step) },
    {
      key: 'severity',
      header: t('etl.col.severity'),
      align: 'left',
      cell: (e) => <Badge tone={e.severity === 'error' ? 'neg' : 'warn'}>{severityLabel(e.severity)}</Badge>,
    },
    { key: 'object', header: t('etl.col.object'), align: 'left', cell: (e) => [e.target, e.src_id].filter(Boolean).join(' · ') || t('common.dash') },
    { key: 'code', header: t('etl.col.code'), align: 'left', cell: (e) => <span className="mono">{e.code}</span> },
    { key: 'message', header: t('etl.col.message'), align: 'left', wrap: true, cell: (e) => e.message },
  ];
  const run = detail.data?.run;

  return (
    <Modal open onClose={onClose} title={t('etl.detailTitle', { id })}>
      {detail.isError ? <ErrorBanner error={detail.error} onRetry={() => void detail.refetch()} /> : null}
      {detail.isPending ? <PageSkeleton /> : null}
      {run && detail.data ? (
        <>
          <dl className="dl">
            <dt>{t('etl.col.source')}</dt>
            <dd>{sourceLabel(run.source)}</dd>
            <dt>{t('etl.col.mode')}</dt>
            <dd>{modeLabel(run.mode)}</dd>
            <dt>{t('etl.col.status')}</dt>
            <dd>{runStatus(run.status).label}</dd>
            <dt>{t('etl.window')}</dt>
            <dd>{run.window_from && run.window_to ? `${formatDate(run.window_from)} – ${formatDate(run.window_to)}` : t('common.dash')}</dd>
            {run.error ? (
              <>
                <dt>{t('etl.error')}</dt>
                <dd>{run.error}</dd>
              </>
            ) : null}
          </dl>
          <Table caption={t('etl.steps')} captionHidden={false} columns={stepColumns} rows={detail.data.steps} rowKey={(s) => s.name} mobile="scroll" free />
          <Table
            caption={t('etl.errors')}
            captionHidden={false}
            columns={errorColumns}
            rows={detail.data.errors}
            rowKey={(e) => `${e.step}:${e.code}:${e.src_id ?? ''}:${e.created_at}`}
            empty={t('etl.noErrors')}
            mobile="scroll"
            free
          />
        </>
      ) : null}
    </Modal>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/admin/BuildsPage.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useState } from 'react';
import type { BuildOut } from '../api/types';
import { t } from '../i18n';
import { formatDateTime } from '../i18n/format';
import { Badge } from '../ui/Badge';
import { Button } from '../ui/Button';
import { Card } from '../ui/Card';
import { ConfirmDialog } from '../ui/ConfirmDialog';
import { ErrorBanner } from '../ui/ErrorBanner';
import { PageSkeleton } from '../ui/Skeleton';
import { Table, type Column } from '../ui/Table';
import { useToast } from '../ui/Toast';
import { useBuilds, useRollbackBuild } from './api';
import { buildStatus } from './labels';

/** Faqat e'lon qilingan (joriy bo'lmagan) buildga qaytish mumkin (2-reja builds.rollback). */
function canActivate(build: BuildOut): boolean {
  return !build.active && (build.status === 'published' || build.status === 'superseded');
}

export default function BuildsPage() {
  const toast = useToast();
  const builds = useBuilds();
  const rollback = useRollbackBuild();
  const [target, setTarget] = useState<number | 'previous' | null>(null);

  const close = () => {
    setTarget(null);
    rollback.reset();
  };

  const confirm = () => {
    if (target === null) return;
    rollback.mutate(target === 'previous' ? null : target, {
      onSuccess: (build) => {
        toast.show(t('builds.done', { id: build.build_id }), 'success');
        close();
      },
    });
  };

  const columns: Column<BuildOut>[] = [
    {
      key: 'build',
      header: t('builds.col.build'),
      align: 'left',
      sortValue: (b) => b.build_id,
      cell: (b) => (
        <span className="cell-main">
          <b>№{b.build_id}</b>
          {b.active ? <Badge tone="ok">{t('builds.active')}</Badge> : null}
        </span>
      ),
    },
    {
      key: 'status',
      header: t('builds.col.status'),
      align: 'left',
      cell: (b) => {
        const status = buildStatus(b.status);
        return <Badge tone={status.tone}>{status.label}</Badge>;
      },
    },
    { key: 'created', header: t('builds.col.created'), cell: (b) => formatDateTime(b.created_at) },
    { key: 'published', header: t('builds.col.published'), cell: (b) => (b.published_at ? formatDateTime(b.published_at) : t('common.dash')) },
    { key: 'load', header: t('builds.col.load'), cell: (b) => (b.load_id === null ? t('common.dash') : `№${b.load_id}`) },
    {
      key: 'action',
      header: t('builds.col.action'),
      cell: (b) =>
        canActivate(b) ? (
          <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => setTarget(b.build_id)}>
            {t('builds.makeActive')}
          </Button>
        ) : null,
    },
  ];

  if (builds.isError) return <ErrorBanner error={builds.error} lastSuccessAt={builds.dataUpdatedAt} onRetry={() => void builds.refetch()} />;
  if (builds.isPending) return <PageSkeleton />;
  const hasPrevious = builds.data.some(canActivate);

  return (
    <Card>
      <div className="toolbar">
        <p className="note">{t('builds.note')}</p>
        <span className="toolbar__grow" />
        <Button variant="secondary" icon="history" disabled={!hasPrevious} onClick={() => setTarget('previous')}>
          {t('builds.rollbackPrevious')}
        </Button>
      </div>
      <Table
        caption={t('admin.page.builds')}
        columns={columns}
        rows={builds.data}
        rowKey={(b) => b.build_id}
        initialSort={{ key: 'build', dir: 'desc' }}
        free
      />
      <ConfirmDialog
        open={target !== null}
        title={t('builds.confirmTitle')}
        message={target === 'previous' ? t('builds.confirmPrevious') : t('builds.confirmText', { id: target ?? '' })}
        confirmLabel={target === 'previous' ? t('builds.rollbackPrevious') : t('builds.makeActive')}
        busy={rollback.isPending}
        error={rollback.error}
        onCancel={close}
        onConfirm={confirm}
      />
    </Card>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/admin/AuditPage.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react';
import { apiUrl } from '../api/client';
import type { AuditOut } from '../api/types';
import { t } from '../i18n';
import { formatDateTime, formatNumber } from '../i18n/format';
import { Badge } from '../ui/Badge';
import { Button } from '../ui/Button';
import { Card } from '../ui/Card';
import { ErrorBanner } from '../ui/ErrorBanner';
import { FormError, TextInput } from '../ui/Field';
import { Icon } from '../ui/icons';
import { Modal } from '../ui/Modal';
import { Pagination } from '../ui/Pagination';
import { Select } from '../ui/Select';
import { PageSkeleton } from '../ui/Skeleton';
import { Table, type Column } from '../ui/Table';
import { AUDIT_PAGE_SIZE, EMPTY_AUDIT_FILTERS, auditQuery, useAudit, useVerifyAudit, type AuditFilters } from './api';
import { auditResult } from './labels';

const ACTION_RE = /^[a-z_.]*$/;

function objectText(row: AuditOut): string {
  if (!row.object_type && !row.object_id) return t('common.dash');
  return [row.object_type, row.object_id].filter(Boolean).join(':');
}

function userText(row: AuditOut): string {
  if (row.username) return row.username;
  return row.user_id === null ? t('common.dash') : `#${row.user_id}`;
}

export default function AuditPage() {
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState<AuditFilters>(EMPTY_AUDIT_FILTERS);
  const [applied, setApplied] = useState<AuditFilters>(EMPTY_AUDIT_FILTERS);
  const [errors, setErrors] = useState<{ action?: string; userId?: string; to?: string }>({});
  const [page, setPage] = useState(1);
  const [open, setOpen] = useState<AuditOut | null>(null);
  const audit = useAudit(applied, page);
  const verify = useVerifyAudit();

  const set = <K extends keyof AuditFilters>(key: K, value: AuditFilters[K]) => setDraft((d) => ({ ...d, [key]: value }));

  const apply = (event: FormEvent) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    const found: typeof errors = {};
    const action = draft.action.trim();
    if (!ACTION_RE.test(action)) found.action = t('audit.actionRule');
    if (draft.userId && !/^\d{1,9}$/.test(draft.userId)) found.userId = t('audit.userIdRule');
    if (draft.from && draft.to && draft.from > draft.to) found.to = t('audit.dateOrder');
    setErrors(found);
    if (found.action || found.userId || found.to) return;
    setApplied({ ...draft, action });
    setPage(1);
  };

  const reset = () => {
    setDraft(EMPTY_AUDIT_FILTERS);
    setApplied(EMPTY_AUDIT_FILTERS);
    setErrors({});
    setPage(1);
  };

  const columns: Column<AuditOut>[] = [
    { key: 'time', header: t('audit.col.time'), align: 'left', cell: (r) => formatDateTime(r.ts) },
    { key: 'user', header: t('audit.col.user'), align: 'left', cell: userText },
    { key: 'action', header: t('audit.col.action'), align: 'left', cell: (r) => <span className="mono">{r.action}</span> },
    {
      key: 'result',
      header: t('audit.col.result'),
      align: 'left',
      cell: (r) => {
        const result = auditResult(r.result);
        return <Badge tone={result.tone}>{result.label}</Badge>;
      },
    },
    { key: 'object', header: t('audit.col.object'), align: 'left', cell: objectText },
    { key: 'ip', header: t('audit.col.ip'), align: 'left', cell: (r) => r.ip ?? t('common.dash') },
    { key: 'request', header: t('audit.col.request'), align: 'left', cell: (r) => <small className="mono">{r.request_id ?? t('common.dash')}</small> },
  ];

  const result = verify.data;

  return (
    <>
      <Card title={t('audit.filters')} sub={t('audit.defaultWindow')}>
        <form className="toolbar" noValidate onSubmit={apply}>
          <TextInput type="date" label={t('audit.from')} value={draft.from} onChange={(e) => set('from', e.target.value)} />
          <TextInput type="date" label={t('audit.to')} value={draft.to} error={errors.to} onChange={(e) => set('to', e.target.value)} />
          <TextInput
            label={t('audit.userId')}
            inputMode="numeric"
            value={draft.userId}
            error={errors.userId}
            onChange={(e) => set('userId', e.target.value.trim())}
          />
          <TextInput
            label={t('audit.action')}
            placeholder={t('audit.actionPlaceholder')}
            autoCapitalize="none"
            spellCheck={false}
            value={draft.action}
            error={errors.action}
            onChange={(e) => set('action', e.target.value)}
          />
          <Select
            label={t('audit.result')}
            value={draft.result}
            options={[
              { value: '', label: t('audit.result.all') },
              { value: 'ok', label: t('audit.result.ok') },
              { value: 'fail', label: t('audit.result.fail') },
              { value: 'denied', label: t('audit.result.denied') },
            ]}
            onChange={(value) => set('result', value === 'ok' || value === 'fail' || value === 'denied' ? value : '')}
          />
          <Button type="submit" icon="filter">
            {t('audit.apply')}
          </Button>
          <Button variant="secondary" onClick={reset}>
            {t('audit.reset')}
          </Button>
        </form>
      </Card>
      <Card
        title={t('admin.page.audit')}
        actions={
          <>
            <a className="btn btn--secondary" href={apiUrl('/audit/export.csv', auditQuery(applied))} download>
              <Icon name="download" />
              <span>{t('audit.export')}</span>
            </a>
            <Button variant="secondary" icon="shield" loading={verify.isPending} onClick={() => verify.mutate()}>
              {t('audit.verify')}
            </Button>
          </>
        }
      >
        <FormError error={verify.error} />
        {result ? (
          result.ok ? (
            <p className="result result--ok" role="status">
              <Icon name="check" />
              <span>{t('audit.verifyOk', { count: formatNumber(result.checked) })}</span>
            </p>
          ) : (
            <div className="result result--bad" role="alert">
              <Icon name="alert" />
              <div>
                <p>{t('audit.verifyBad', { id: result.first_bad_id ?? '—' })}</p>
                {result.bad_ids.length > 0 ? <p>{t('audit.verifyBadIds', { ids: result.bad_ids.join(', ') })}</p> : null}
              </div>
            </div>
          )
        ) : null}
        {audit.isError ? (
          <ErrorBanner error={audit.error} lastSuccessAt={audit.dataUpdatedAt} onRetry={() => void audit.refetch()} />
        ) : audit.isPending ? (
          <PageSkeleton />
        ) : (
          <>
            <Table
              caption={t('admin.page.audit')}
              columns={columns}
              rows={audit.data.items}
              rowKey={(r) => r.id}
              onRowClick={setOpen}
              empty={t('audit.empty')}
            />
            <Pagination page={page} pageSize={AUDIT_PAGE_SIZE} total={audit.data.total} onPage={setPage} />
          </>
        )}
      </Card>
      {open ? (
        <Modal open onClose={() => setOpen(null)} title={t('audit.detailTitle', { id: open.id })} note={formatDateTime(open.ts)}>
          <dl className="dl">
            <dt>{t('audit.col.user')}</dt>
            <dd>{userText(open)}</dd>
            <dt>{t('audit.col.action')}</dt>
            <dd className="mono">{open.action}</dd>
            <dt>{t('audit.col.result')}</dt>
            <dd>{auditResult(open.result).label}</dd>
            <dt>{t('audit.col.object')}</dt>
            <dd>{objectText(open)}</dd>
            <dt>{t('audit.col.ip')}</dt>
            <dd>{open.ip ?? t('common.dash')}</dd>
            <dt>{t('audit.col.request')}</dt>
            <dd className="mono">{open.request_id ?? t('common.dash')}</dd>
          </dl>
          <h3>{t('audit.details')}</h3>
          <pre className="details">{JSON.stringify(open.details, null, 2)}</pre>
        </Modal>
      ) : null}
    </>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/admin/KeysPage.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useState } from 'react';
import type { KeyOut } from '../api/types';
import { t } from '../i18n';
import { formatDateTime } from '../i18n/format';
import { Badge } from '../ui/Badge';
import { Button } from '../ui/Button';
import { Card } from '../ui/Card';
import { ConfirmDialog } from '../ui/ConfirmDialog';
import { ErrorBanner } from '../ui/ErrorBanner';
import { Icon } from '../ui/icons';
import { PageSkeleton } from '../ui/Skeleton';
import { Table, type Column } from '../ui/Table';
import { useToast } from '../ui/Toast';
import { useKeys, useRotateKey } from './api';
import { keyStatus, purposeLabel } from './labels';

/** Faqat holat va rotatsiya; eski versiyalarni o'chirish va KEK almashtirish — CLI (1-reja chetlanishi). */
export default function KeysPage() {
  const toast = useToast();
  const keys = useKeys();
  const rotate = useRotateKey();
  const [purpose, setPurpose] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const close = () => {
    setPurpose(null);
    rotate.reset();
  };

  const confirm = () => {
    if (!purpose) return;
    rotate.mutate(purpose, {
      onSuccess: (result) => {
        toast.show(
          t('keys.rotated', { purpose: purposeLabel(result.purpose), version: result.version, count: result.reencrypted }),
          'success',
        );
        close();
      },
    });
  };

  const columns: Column<KeyOut>[] = [
    {
      key: 'purpose',
      header: t('keys.col.purpose'),
      align: 'left',
      sortValue: (k) => `${k.purpose}:${String(999 - k.version).padStart(3, '0')}`,
      cell: (k) => (
        <span className="cell-main">
          <b>{purposeLabel(k.purpose)}</b>
          <small className="mono">{k.purpose}</small>
        </span>
      ),
    },
    { key: 'version', header: t('keys.col.version'), cell: (k) => k.version },
    {
      key: 'status',
      header: t('keys.col.status'),
      align: 'left',
      cell: (k) => {
        const status = keyStatus(k.status);
        return <Badge tone={status.tone}>{status.label}</Badge>;
      },
    },
    { key: 'created', header: t('keys.col.created'), cell: (k) => formatDateTime(k.created_at) },
    { key: 'retired', header: t('keys.col.retired'), cell: (k) => (k.retired_at ? formatDateTime(k.retired_at) : t('common.dash')) },
    {
      key: 'action',
      header: t('keys.col.action'),
      cell: (k) =>
        k.status === 'active' ? (
          <Button variant="secondary" icon="refresh" onClick={() => setPurpose(k.purpose)}>
            {t('keys.rotate')}
          </Button>
        ) : null,
    },
  ];

  if (keys.isError) return <ErrorBanner error={keys.error} lastSuccessAt={keys.dataUpdatedAt} onRetry={() => void keys.refetch()} />;
  if (keys.isPending) return <PageSkeleton />;

  return (
    <Card>
      <p className="note">
        <Icon name="info" /> {t('keys.note')}
      </p>
      <Table
        caption={t('admin.page.keys')}
        columns={columns}
        rows={keys.data}
        rowKey={(k) => `${k.purpose}:${k.version}`}
        initialSort={{ key: 'purpose', dir: 'asc' }}
        free
      />
      <ConfirmDialog
        open={purpose !== null}
        title={t('keys.confirmTitle', { purpose: purposeLabel(purpose ?? '') })}
        message={t('keys.confirmText')}
        confirmLabel={t('keys.rotate')}
        danger
        busy={rotate.isPending}
        error={rotate.error}
        onCancel={close}
        onConfirm={confirm}
      />
    </Card>
  );
}
```

### Task 17: Berish — `frontend/Dockerfile` (build → Caddy), Caddy'da statika, SPA fallback va kesh sarlavhalari

**Ruling (serving):** dev va prod bitta yo'l bilan: `frontend/Dockerfile` ning `web` bosqichi (`caddy:2-alpine` + `dist` → `/srv`) — dev stack'dagi `caddy` servisi shu image'dan quriladi, prod (4-reja) ham shu image'ni oladi. Vite dev-server Caddy orqasida ishlatilmaydi: u CSS'ni `<style>` teglari bilan kiritadi va `style-src 'self'` ni buzadi. Frontend o'zgarganda: `docker compose -f deploy/compose.yml up -d --build caddy`.

**Files:**
- Create: `frontend/Dockerfile`, `frontend/.dockerignore`
- Modify: `deploy/caddy/Caddyfile.dev` (to'liq almashtirish), `deploy/compose.yml` (`caddy` servisi), `Makefile`, `README.md`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `npm run build` (Task 1), 1-reja `deploy/compose.yml`/`Caddyfile.dev`.
- Produces: image `ugekazna-web:dev` (`/srv` — build natijasi); Caddy sayt manzillari `localhost` va `caddy` (E2E konteyneri `https://caddy` ga ulanadi); `/assets/*` — `Cache-Control: public, max-age=31536000, immutable`; qolgan statika (`/`, `index.html`, `favicon.svg`) — `no-cache`; `/assets/` dan tashqari noma'lum yo'l → `index.html`; yo'q `/assets/*` → 404.

- [ ] **Step 1: Dockerfile va .dockerignore**

`frontend/Dockerfile`:
```dockerfile
# syntax=docker/dockerfile:1.7
# Frontend: Node'da build (lockfile bo'yicha) → Caddy image'ga faqat statik fayllar.

# ── 1-bosqich: build ──────────────────────────────────────────────
FROM node:22-alpine AS build
WORKDIR /app
# Korporativ TLS proksi (ixtiyoriy): certs/*.crt bo'lmasa bo'sh fayl — Node faqat ogohlantiradi.
COPY certs/ /certs/
RUN cat /certs/*.crt > /tmp/extra-ca.pem 2>/dev/null || true
ENV NODE_EXTRA_CA_CERTS=/tmp/extra-ca.pem \
    npm_config_fund=false \
    npm_config_audit=false \
    npm_config_update_notifier=false
COPY package.json package-lock.json ./
RUN npm ci
COPY . .
RUN npm run build

# ── 2-bosqich: statik fayllar Caddy bilan ────────────────────────
FROM caddy:2-alpine AS web
COPY --from=build /app/dist /srv
```

`frontend/.dockerignore`:
```
node_modules
dist
coverage
e2e
*.log
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Caddyfile.dev**

`deploy/caddy/Caddyfile.dev` (to'liq almashtiring):
```
{
	admin off
}

# "caddy" — ichki tarmoqdagi E2E konteyneri uchun (https://caddy).
localhost, caddy {
	tls internal
	encode zstd gzip

	request_body {
		max_size 1MB
	}

	header {
		Strict-Transport-Security "max-age=63072000; includeSubDomains"
		X-Content-Type-Options "nosniff"
		X-Frame-Options "DENY"
		Referrer-Policy "no-referrer"
		Permissions-Policy "accelerometer=(), camera=(), geolocation=(), gyroscope=(), magnetometer=(), microphone=(), payment=(), usb=()"
		Cross-Origin-Opener-Policy "same-origin"
		Cross-Origin-Resource-Policy "same-origin"
		Content-Security-Policy "default-src 'self'; script-src 'self'; style-src 'self'; img-src 'self' data: blob:; connect-src 'self'; font-src 'self'; object-src 'none'; frame-ancestors 'none'; base-uri 'none'; form-action 'self'"
		-Server
	}

	# Probe'lar faqat ichki tarmoq uchun.
	@probes path /api/v1/healthz /api/v1/readyz
	respond @probes 404

	handle /api/* {
		reverse_proxy api:8000
	}

	# Frontend: frontend/Dockerfile `web` bosqichi → /srv. Hash-routing, HTML faqat "/".
	handle {
		root * /srv

		@assets path /assets/*
		header @assets Cache-Control "public, max-age=31536000, immutable"

		@notAssets not path /assets/*
		header @notAssets Cache-Control "no-cache"

		# SPA fallback: /assets/ dan tashqari yo'q yo'l → index.html; yo'q asset → 404.
		@spa {
			not path /assets/*
			file {
				try_files {path} /index.html
			}
		}
		rewrite @spa {file_match.relative}

		file_server
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: compose va Makefile**

`deploy/compose.yml` — `caddy:` servisini to'liq quyidagiga almashtiring:
```yaml
  caddy:
    <<: *hardening
    build: {context: ../frontend, target: web}
    image: ugekazna-web:dev
    cap_add: [NET_BIND_SERVICE]
    ports: ["8443:443"]
    volumes:
      - ./caddy/Caddyfile.dev:/etc/caddy/Caddyfile:ro
      - caddy_data:/data
      - caddy_config:/config
    networks: [edge]
    depends_on: [api]
```

`Makefile` — `.PHONY` qatoriga `web` qo'shing va oxiriga:
```makefile
web: ## frontend'ni qayta qurish va Caddy'ni yangilash
	$(COMPOSE) up -d --build caddy
```

`README.md` — "Tez boshlash" bo'limi oxiriga qo'shing:
````markdown
Frontend: https://localhost:8443/ (build `frontend/Dockerfile` → Caddy). O'zgarishdan keyin: `make web`
yoki `docker compose -f deploy/compose.yml up -d --build caddy`.

Frontend testlari (Node faqat Docker'da):
```bash
NODE="docker compose -f deploy/compose.frontend.yml run --rm node"
$NODE npm ci
$NODE npm run check            # tsc + eslint + vitest
curl -skf https://localhost:8443/api/v1/openapi.json -o frontend/openapi.json && $NODE npm run gen:api   # API tiplari
```
````

- [ ] **Step 4: Smoke-test (to'g'ri berilish, CSP va kesh)**

```bash
docker compose -f deploy/compose.yml up -d --build
docker compose -f deploy/compose.yml ps caddy
curl -skI https://localhost:8443/ | grep -iE "^(HTTP|content-type|cache-control|content-security-policy)"
ASSET=$(curl -sk https://localhost:8443/ | grep -o '/assets/[^"]*\.js' | head -1); echo "$ASSET"
curl -skI "https://localhost:8443$ASSET" | grep -i "^cache-control"
curl -sk -o /dev/null -w "%{http_code}\n" https://localhost:8443/assets/nope.js
curl -sk https://localhost:8443/some/deep/path | grep -c 'id="root"'
curl -sk https://localhost:8443/ | grep -ciE "<style|style=|onclick=|<script>" || true
CSS=$(curl -sk https://localhost:8443/ | grep -o '/assets/[^"]*\.css' | head -1)
curl -sk "https://localhost:8443$CSS" | grep -c "data:font" || true
curl -sk -o /dev/null -w "%{http_code}\n" https://localhost:8443/api/v1/auth/me
```
Expected (tartib bilan): `caddy` — running; `/` → `HTTP/2 200`, `content-type: text/html`, `cache-control: no-cache`, CSP sarlavhasi bor; `$ASSET` — `/assets/index-<hash>.js`; asset → `cache-control: public, max-age=31536000, immutable`; yo'q asset → `404`; chuqur yo'l → `1` (index.html qaytdi); inline stil/skript soni → `0`; CSS'dagi `data:font` → `0`; `/auth/me` sessiyasiz → `401`. Brauzerda https://localhost:8443/ → login sahifasi, DevTools Console'da CSP xatolari yo'q.
