### Task 18: E2E — dev/test seed buyrug'i va Playwright (390 / 1024 / 1440)

**Ruling (E2E seed):** ma'lum parol va ma'lum TOTP sirli foydalanuvchilar alohida modul `backend/app/devtools.py` (`python -m app.devtools seed-e2e`) orqali yaratiladi — `app/cli.py` ga (prod operator buyruqlari) qo'shilmaydi. Buyruq `ENV ∉ {dev, test}` bo'lsa hech narsa qilmay 2 kodi bilan chiqadi va prod'da hech qachon chaqirilmaydi (`scripts/e2e.sh` faqat dev compose'ga ulanadi). Har ishga tushishda holatni tiklaydi (parol, TOTP qadami, bloklar, sessiyalar) — TOTP replay himoyasi takroriy E2E'ni yiqitmaydi; har kenglik o'z foydalanuvchisi bilan kiradi.

**Files:**
- Create: `backend/app/devtools.py`, `backend/tests/test_devtools.py`, `frontend/e2e/package.json`, `frontend/e2e/package-lock.json` (npm install natijasi), `frontend/e2e/tsconfig.json`, `frontend/e2e/playwright.config.ts`, `frontend/e2e/totp.ts`, `frontend/e2e/fixtures.ts`, `frontend/e2e/specs/totp.spec.ts`, `frontend/e2e/specs/auth-shell-admin.spec.ts`, `frontend/e2e/specs/enroll-password.spec.ts`, `frontend/e2e/specs/branch-scope.spec.ts`, `scripts/e2e.sh`
- Modify: `deploy/compose.frontend.yml` (`e2e` servisi), `Makefile`, `README.md`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: 1-reja `User`, `Position`, `PositionBranch`, `Role`, `UserSession`, `BackupCode` modellari, `passwords.hash_password`, `keystore.encrypt_str`, `Purpose.MFA`, `totp_aad`, `bootstrap_runtime`, `new_session`, `get_settings`; 2-reja filial seed'i (`branch_id = 3` mavjud); frontend (Task 1–17) va dev stack (Task 17).
- Produces:
  - `app.devtools`: `ALLOWED_ENVS = {"dev", "test"}`, `E2E_PASSWORD = "E2e-Sinov-Kalit-2026!"`, `E2E_TOTP_SECRET = "JBSWY3DPEHPK3PXPJBSWY3DPEHPK3PXP"`, `E2eUser`, `E2E_USERS` (`e2e_admin_390`, `e2e_admin_1024`, `e2e_admin_1440` — admin, 2FA; `e2e_new` — spec_kazna, 2FA yo'q, parol almashtirish majburiy; `e2e_branch` — economist_filiala, filial 3), `seed_e2e() -> int`, `main(argv) -> int`.
  - `frontend/e2e/totp.ts`: `base32Decode(input) -> Buffer`, `totp(secret, timeMs = Date.now()) -> string`.
  - `frontend/e2e/fixtures.ts`: `E2E_PASSWORD`, `E2E_TOTP_SECRET`, `NEW_PASSWORD`, `adminFor(project)`, `isMobile(project)`, `signIn(page, username)`, `expectNoHorizontalScroll(page)`.
  - `scripts/e2e.sh [playwright argumentlari]`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Backend seed testlarini yozish**

`backend/tests/test_devtools.py`:
```python
import pyotp
import pytest
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
from sqlalchemy import select

from app import devtools
from app.auth.models import PositionBranch, User
from app.auth.service import totp_aad
from app.core import keystore
from app.core.config import Settings
from app.core.crypto import Purpose
from app.core.db import new_session
from app.main import create_app


def test_refuses_outside_dev_and_test(monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch, capsys: pytest.CaptureFixture[str]) -> None:
    monkeypatch.setattr(devtools, "get_settings", lambda: Settings(env="prod"))
    assert devtools.main(["seed-e2e"]) == 2
    assert "Rad etildi" in capsys.readouterr().out


def test_seed_is_idempotent_and_resets_state() -> None:
    assert devtools.main(["seed-e2e"]) == 0
    with new_session() as db:
        admin = db.execute(select(User).where(User.username == "e2e_admin_1440")).scalar_one()
        admin.failed_logins = 4
        admin.totp_last_step = 999_999_999
        admin.is_active = False
        db.commit()

    assert devtools.main(["seed-e2e"]) == 0
    with new_session() as db:
        users = {
            u.username: u
            for u in db.execute(select(User).where(User.username.in_([s.username for s in devtools.E2E_USERS]))).scalars()
        }
        assert set(users) == {s.username for s in devtools.E2E_USERS}
        admin = users["e2e_admin_1440"]
        assert admin.is_active and admin.failed_logins == 0 and admin.totp_last_step is None
        assert admin.totp_enabled and admin.totp_secret_enc is not None
        assert keystore.decrypt_str(Purpose.MFA, admin.totp_secret_enc, totp_aad(admin.id)) == devtools.E2E_TOTP_SECRET
        new = users["e2e_new"]
        assert new.must_change_password and not new.totp_enabled and new.totp_secret_enc is None
        branches = db.execute(
            select(PositionBranch.branch_id).where(PositionBranch.position_id == users["e2e_branch"].position_id)
        ).scalars().all()
        assert list(branches) == [3]


def test_seeded_admin_can_sign_in_with_the_known_totp_secret() -> None:
    assert devtools.main(["seed-e2e"]) == 0
    with TestClient(create_app(), base_url="https://testserver") as client:
        r = client.post("/api/v1/auth/login", json={"username": "e2e_admin_1024", "password": devtools.E2E_PASSWORD})
        assert r.status_code == 200
        assert r.json()["next"] == "mfa_verify"
        code = pyotp.TOTP(devtools.E2E_TOTP_SECRET).now()
        r = client.post("/api/v1/auth/2fa/verify", json={"mfa_token": r.json()["mfa_token"], "code": code})
        assert r.status_code == 200
        assert "admin:users" in r.json()["permissions"]
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Seed buyrug'ini yozish**

`backend/app/devtools.py`:
```python
"""Faqat dev/test muhiti uchun yordamchi buyruqlar.

    python -m app.devtools seed-e2e

`seed-e2e` Playwright E2E testlari uchun ma'lum parol va ma'lum TOTP sirli
foydalanuvchilarni yaratadi yoki holatini qayta tiklaydi (idempotent). ENV dev/test
bo'lmasa hech narsa qilmaydi va 2 kodi bilan chiqadi — prod'da bu hisoblar paydo bo'lmaydi.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import sys
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import UTC, datetime

from sqlalchemy import delete, select, update
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.auth import passwords
from app.auth.models import BackupCode, Position, PositionBranch, Role, User, UserSession
from app.auth.service import totp_aad
from app.bootstrap import bootstrap_runtime
from app.core import keystore
from app.core.config import get_settings
from app.core.crypto import Purpose
from app.core.db import new_session

ALLOWED_ENVS = frozenset({"dev", "test"})
E2E_PASSWORD = "E2e-Sinov-Kalit-2026!"
E2E_TOTP_SECRET = "JBSWY3DPEHPK3PXPJBSWY3DPEHPK3PXP"


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class E2eUser:
    username: str
    full_name: str
    role: str
    totp: bool
    must_change: bool
    branch_ids: tuple[int, ...] = ()


E2E_USERS: tuple[E2eUser, ...] = (
    E2eUser("e2e_admin_390", "Тестов Мобильный", "admin", totp=True, must_change=False),
    E2eUser("e2e_admin_1024", "Тестов Планшетный", "admin", totp=True, must_change=False),
    E2eUser("e2e_admin_1440", "Тестов Настольный", "admin", totp=True, must_change=False),
    E2eUser("e2e_new", "Новиков Сотрудник", "spec_kazna", totp=False, must_change=True),
    E2eUser("e2e_branch", "Филиалов Экономист", "economist_filiala", totp=True, must_change=False, branch_ids=(3,)),
)


def _out(message: str) -> None:
    sys.stdout.write(message + "\n")


def _position_id(db: Session, spec: E2eUser) -> int:
    role_id = db.execute(select(Role.id).where(Role.code == spec.role)).scalar_one()
    if not spec.branch_ids:
        # Tizim rollari uchun seed qilingan lavozim (kodi rol kodi bilan bir xil).
        return int(db.execute(select(Position.id).where(Position.code == spec.role)).scalar_one())
    code = f"e2e_{spec.username}"
    position = db.execute(select(Position).where(Position.code == code)).scalar_one_or_none()
    if position is None:
        position = Position(code=code, name=f"E2E: {spec.full_name}", role_id=role_id)
        db.add(position)
        db.flush()
    position.role_id = role_id
    position.is_active = True
    db.execute(delete(PositionBranch).where(PositionBranch.position_id == position.id))
    for branch_id in spec.branch_ids:
        db.add(PositionBranch(position_id=position.id, branch_id=branch_id))
    return int(position.id)


def _upsert(db: Session, spec: E2eUser, password_hash: str, now: datetime) -> User:
    position_id = _position_id(db, spec)
    user = db.execute(select(User).where(User.username == spec.username)).scalar_one_or_none()
    if user is None:
        user = User(
            username=spec.username,
            full_name=spec.full_name,
            position_id=position_id,
            password_hash=password_hash,
            password_changed_at=now,
            must_change_password=spec.must_change,
        )
        db.add(user)
        db.flush()
    user.full_name = spec.full_name
    user.position_id = position_id
    user.password_hash = password_hash
    user.password_changed_at = now
    user.must_change_password = spec.must_change
    user.is_active = True
    user.failed_logins = 0
    user.locked_until = None
    user.totp_last_step = None
    user.totp_enabled = spec.totp
    user.totp_secret_enc = (
        keystore.encrypt_str(Purpose.MFA, E2E_TOTP_SECRET, totp_aad(user.id)) if spec.totp else None
    )
    db.execute(
        update(BackupCode).where(BackupCode.user_id == user.id, BackupCode.used_at.is_(None)).values(used_at=now)
    )
    db.execute(
        update(UserSession)
        .where(UserSession.user_id == user.id, UserSession.revoked_at.is_(None))
        .values(revoked_at=now, revoke_reason="e2e_reset")
    )
    return user


def seed_e2e() -> int:
    env = get_settings().env
    if env not in ALLOWED_ENVS:
        _out(f"Rad etildi: seed-e2e faqat dev/test muhitida ishlaydi (ENV={env})")
        return 2
    bootstrap_runtime()
    now = datetime.now(UTC)
    password_hash = passwords.hash_password(E2E_PASSWORD)
    with new_session() as db:
        for spec in E2E_USERS:
            _upsert(db, spec, password_hash, now)
        db.commit()
    _out("E2E foydalanuvchilari tayyor: " + ", ".join(spec.username for spec in E2E_USERS))
    return 0


def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="python -m app.devtools")
    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest="command", required=True)
    sub.add_parser("seed-e2e", help="E2E foydalanuvchilarini yaratish/tiklash (faqat dev/test)")
    parser.parse_args(argv)
    return seed_e2e()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())
```

`frontend/e2e/totp.ts`:
```ts
import { createHmac } from 'node:crypto';

const ALPHABET = 'ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ234567';

export function base32Decode(input: string): Buffer {
  let bits = 0;
  let value = 0;
  const out: number[] = [];
  for (const char of input.replace(/=+$/, '').toUpperCase()) {
    const index = ALPHABET.indexOf(char);
    if (index < 0) throw new Error(`base32: noto'g'ri belgi ${char}`);
    value = ((value << 5) | index) & 0xffff;
    bits += 5;
    if (bits >= 8) {
      out.push((value >>> (bits - 8)) & 0xff);
      bits -= 8;
    }
  }
  return Buffer.from(out);
}

/** RFC 6238: SHA-1, 30 s, 6 raqam (backend `app.auth.totp` bilan bir xil). */
export function totp(secret: string, timeMs: number = Date.now()): string {
  const counter = Buffer.alloc(8);
  counter.writeBigUInt64BE(BigInt(Math.floor(timeMs / 1000 / 30)));
  const digest = createHmac('sha1', base32Decode(secret)).update(counter).digest();
  const offset = (digest[digest.length - 1] ?? 0) & 0x0f;
  const binary =
    (((digest[offset] ?? 0) & 0x7f) << 24) |
    ((digest[offset + 1] ?? 0) << 16) |
    ((digest[offset + 2] ?? 0) << 8) |
    (digest[offset + 3] ?? 0);
  return String(binary % 1_000_000).padStart(6, '0');
}
```

import { expect, type Page } from '@playwright/test';
import { totp } from './totp';

/** backend/app/devtools.py bilan bir xil qiymatlar (faqat dev/test). */
export const E2E_PASSWORD = 'E2e-Sinov-Kalit-2026!';
export const E2E_TOTP_SECRET = 'JBSWY3DPEHPK3PXPJBSWY3DPEHPK3PXP';
export const NEW_PASSWORD = 'Yangi-Mustahkam-77#Qadam';

const ADMINS: Record<string, string> = {
  'mobile-390': 'e2e_admin_390',
  'tablet-1024': 'e2e_admin_1024',
  'desktop-1440': 'e2e_admin_1440',
};

/** Har kenglik o'z foydalanuvchisi bilan: bitta TOTP qadami ikki marta ishlatilmaydi. */
export function adminFor(project: string): string {
  return ADMINS[project] ?? 'e2e_admin_1440';
}

export function isMobile(project: string): boolean {
  return project === 'mobile-390';
}

export async function signIn(page: Page, username: string): Promise<void> {
  await page.getByLabel('Логин', { exact: true }).fill(username);
  await page.getByLabel('Пароль', { exact: true }).fill(E2E_PASSWORD);
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Войти' }).click();
  await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Подтверждение входа' })).toBeVisible();
  await page.getByLabel('Код из приложения', { exact: true }).fill(totp(E2E_TOTP_SECRET));
}

export async function expectNoHorizontalScroll(page: Page): Promise<void> {
  const overflow = await page.evaluate(() => document.documentElement.scrollWidth - window.innerWidth);
  expect(overflow).toBeLessThanOrEqual(0);
}
```

import { expect, test } from '@playwright/test';
import { totp } from '../totp';

// RFC 6238 A-ilova (SHA-1, "12345678901234567890"): 8 raqamli kodning oxirgi 6 tasi.
const RFC_SECRET = 'GEZDGNBVGY3TQOJQGEZDGNBVGY3TQOJQ';

test('TOTP generatori RFC 6238 test vektorlariga mos', ({}, info) => {
  test.skip(info.project.name !== 'desktop-1440', 'brauzersiz test — bir marta yetarli');
  expect(totp(RFC_SECRET, 59_000)).toBe('287082');
  expect(totp(RFC_SECRET, 1_111_111_109_000)).toBe('081804');
  expect(totp(RFC_SECRET, 2_000_000_000_000)).toBe('279037');
});
```

`frontend/e2e/specs/auth-shell-admin.spec.ts`:
```ts
import AxeBuilder from '@axe-core/playwright';
import { expect, test } from '@playwright/test';
import { adminFor, expectNoHorizontalScroll, isMobile, signIn } from '../fixtures';

test('login → 2FA → navigatsiya → filtrlar → admin → sessiya tugashi', async ({ page }, info) => {
  const mobile = isMobile(info.project.name);
  const desktop = info.project.name === 'desktop-1440';
  const username = adminFor(info.project.name);

  await page.goto('/#kazna/svod');
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(/#login\?next=kazna%2Fsvod$/);
  await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Вход в систему' })).toBeVisible();
  if (desktop) expect((await new AxeBuilder({ page }).analyze()).violations).toEqual([]);

  await signIn(page, username);
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(/#kazna\/svod$/);
  await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { level: 1, name: 'Казначейство: сводка' })).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.getByText('Раздел в разработке')).toBeVisible();
  await expectNoHorizontalScroll(page);
  if (desktop) expect((await new AxeBuilder({ page }).analyze()).violations).toEqual([]);

  // Chap menyu (mobil'da — Drawer).
  if (mobile) await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Меню раздела' }).click();
  await page.getByRole('link', { name: 'Платёжный календарь' }).click();
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(/#kazna\/calendar$/);
  await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { level: 1, name: 'Платёжный календарь' })).toBeVisible();

  // Davr filtri URL'da va yangilanganda saqlanadi (mobil'da — BottomSheet).
  if (mobile) await page.getByRole('button', { name: /^Фильтры/ }).click();
  await page.getByLabel('Период', { exact: true }).selectOption('q1');
  if (mobile) await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Готово' }).click();
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(/#kazna\/calendar\?per=q1$/);
  await page.reload();
  await expect(page.getByRole('group', { name: 'Активные фильтры' })).toContainText('I квартал');

  // Bo'lim almashtirish filtrni saqlaydi.
  await page.getByRole('navigation', { name: 'Разделы' }).getByRole('link', { name: 'Обзор' }).click();
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(/#obzor\/svod\?per=q1$/);
  await expectNoHorizontalScroll(page);

  // Admin → foydalanuvchilar.
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: /Меню пользователя/ }).click();
  await page.getByRole('menuitem', { name: 'Администрирование' }).click();
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(/#admin\/users$/);
  await page.getByLabel('Поиск', { exact: true }).fill(username);
  await expect(page.getByText(username, { exact: true })).toBeVisible();
  if (!mobile) await expect(page.getByRole('table', { name: 'Пользователи' })).toBeVisible();
  await expectNoHorizontalScroll(page);
  if (desktop) expect((await new AxeBuilder({ page }).analyze()).violations).toEqual([]);

  // Sessiya tugadi (cookie yo'q) → login, qaytish manzili saqlanadi.
  await page.context().clearCookies();
  await page.reload();
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(/#login\?next=admin%2Fusers$/);
});
```

import { expect, test } from '@playwright/test';
import { E2E_PASSWORD, NEW_PASSWORD } from '../fixtures';
import { totp } from '../totp';

test('yangi xodim: 2FA ulash → zaxira kodlar → majburiy parol almashtirish', async ({ page }, info) => {
  test.skip(info.project.name !== 'desktop-1440', 'oqim kenglikka bog\'liq emas — bir marta yetarli');

  await page.goto('/');
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(/#login$/);
  await page.getByLabel('Логин', { exact: true }).fill('e2e_new');
  await page.getByLabel('Пароль', { exact: true }).fill(E2E_PASSWORD);
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Войти' }).click();

  await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Подключение двухфакторной аутентификации' })).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.getByRole('img', { name: /QR-код/ })).toBeVisible();
  const secret = (await page.getByTestId('totp-secret').innerText()).replace(/\s+/g, '');
  await page.getByLabel('Код из приложения', { exact: true }).fill(totp(secret));
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Подтвердить' }).click();

  await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Резервные коды' })).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.getByTestId('backup-codes').getByRole('listitem')).toHaveCount(10);
  const download = page.waitForEvent('download');
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Скачать' }).click();

  await page.getByLabel('Я сохранил резервные коды в надёжном месте').check();
  await next.click();

  await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Необходимо сменить пароль' })).toBeVisible();
  await page.getByLabel('Текущий пароль', { exact: true }).fill(E2E_PASSWORD);
  await page.getByLabel('Новый пароль', { exact: true }).fill(NEW_PASSWORD);
  await page.getByLabel('Повторите новый пароль', { exact: true }).fill(NEW_PASSWORD);
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Сохранить пароль' }).click();

  await expect(page).toHaveURL(/#obzor\/svod$/);
  await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { level: 1, name: 'Обзор: сводка' })).toBeVisible();
});

`scripts/e2e.sh`:
```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# E2E: dev stack ishlab turgan bo'lishi kerak (docker compose -f deploy/compose.yml up -d --build).
# Foydalanish: scripts/e2e.sh [playwright argumentlari], masalan: scripts/e2e.sh --project=desktop-1440
set -euo pipefail
cd "$(dirname "$0")/.."

docker compose -f deploy/compose.yml exec -T api python -m app.devtools seed-e2e
docker compose -f deploy/compose.frontend.yml run --rm e2e \
  sh -c 'npm ci && npx tsc --noEmit -p . && npx playwright test "$@"' e2e "$@"
```

chmod +x scripts/e2e.sh
git update-index --chmod=+x scripts/e2e.sh 2>/dev/null || true
